innāṉku maṇaṅkaḷē ulakiṟ perumpāṉmiyātaliṉ, ivaṟṟi eṉṟumē poruntākkāmam eṉṟu koḷḷātu, mutalil poruntātaṉa pōṉṟiruntu piṉṉarp poruntum kāmamākiya peruntiṇi eṉṟē koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
peruntiṇik kuṟippākiya maṭalēṟutal mutaliya nāṉkilum pāṅkaṉ iṭiniṉṟu nalvaḻippaṭuppāṉ ākaliṉ ivaṟṟiṟkup pāṅkaṉ nimittam āyiṉāṉ.			(to. po. 143 kuḻa.)
piṉṉili muyaṟal -
peruntiṇit talivi talivaṉiṭam taṉ kuṟiyic colli irantu piṉ niṟṟal.
“muṉpu avaṉik kaṇṭatum avaṉpāl vēṭki koṇṭu tuyaruṟuvēṉākiya nāṉ mīṇṭum avaṉik kaṇṭu kitoḻutēṉ; āyiṉ, avaṉ eṉṉi ēṟiṭṭum pārtti laṉē!” eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.
itu peṇpāṟkūṟṟup peruntiṇikkaṇ oru tuṟi.
	(pu. ve. mā. 16 - 2)
piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṉ maṟukkappaṭṭup piṉṉum kūṭaluṟum neñciṟkuc coṉṉatu -
tōḻiyiṭam irantu vēṇṭiyum uṭaṉpāṭu peṟāta talivaṉ, talivipāl ceṉṟa taṉ neñciṉi nōkki, “maḻiyil akappaṭṭa uppuvaṇṭiyiluḷḷa uppup potikaḷ karintu aḻivatupōla, taliviyiṉ kūntalaḻakil īṭupaṭṭut tōḻiyāl maṟukkappaṭṭum aṉpu oḻiyāmal kāmattāl nāṇ aḻintu varuntum nī, muṉpē kaṭkuṭittu mayaṅkiyōr ataṉmēlum kaṭkuṭittu aṟivi muṟṟum iḻattal pōla, nī aṟivu muṟṟum iḻantu āci vayappaṭṭut taṭumāṟu kiṟāy” eṉṟa kūṟutal.	(kuṟun. 165)
piṉṉiṉṟāṉ kūṟiyatu -
“tōḻi! kāli pakal māli yāmam vikaṟi eṉṟa ciṟupoḻutukaḷip pirittaṟiya iyalāta niliyil ellā nērattiyum oṉṟākak karuti mayaṅkum kāmaniliyiluḷḷa yāṉ taliviyip pirintiruttal āṟṟēṉ. nī eṉkuṟiyi maṟuttāyāyiṉ, yāṉ maṭalēṟit taliviyip peṟutal kūṭum. āyiṉum acceyal talivikkup paḻi taruvatātaliṉ acceyalukkut tuṇintēṉallēṉ. atu ceyyātu uyir vittukkoṇṭu vāḻtalum eṉakku aritu. ātaliṉ uyirnīttalē naṉṟu” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(kuṟun. 32)
`pukaraṟu kōti poṟuttaṟ kuṟi irattal' -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉuṭiya vāyilākap pukka pāṅkaṉ tōḻi pāṇaṉ mutaliyōr, “talivaṉuṭiya tavaṟṟip poṟuttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyami yāmi kulappeṇṇākiya taliviyiṉuṭiya kaṭami” eṉṟu kūṟi avaḷi vēṇṭip pulavi tīrttal. (maruta naṭiyiyal).	(vī. cō. 95 uri mēṟ)
pukaḻtal (1) -
iravukkuṟikkaṇ taliviyik kūṭi makiḻnta talivaṉ, “ivaḷ tēṉ veḷippaṭum kumutamalar; yāṉ niṟaviya kiraṇaṅkaḷikkoṇṭu ataṉi malarttum vāṉakattu matiyāvēṉ” (kōvi. 166) eṉṟu avaḷ taṉpāl koṇṭa kātaltiṟattip pukaḻtal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘mukam kaṇṭu makiḻtal’ 
eṉṉum (166).	(na. a. 158)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.
pukaḻtal (2) -
talivaṉ taliviyi avaḷaḻaku mutaliyavaṟṟik kūṟip pukaḻtal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘iṭantalippāṭu’ eṉum kiḷavik kaṇṇatoru tuṟi (itu kaḷaviyaliṉ puṇarccivaki palavaṟṟilum nikaḻvatu; paṇpu pārāṭṭal, nalam pārāṭṭal pōṉṟatu.)	(na. a. 135)
pukaḻtal (3) -
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ aḻakiyum avaḷ tanta iṉpatti yum pukaḻntu kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum kiḷavik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 137)
pukaḻtal (4) -
“pāvāy! uṉ kaṇkaḷil nañciṉip paṭitta piramaṉ uṉ vāyitaḻil amutattip paṭikkātu viṭṭāl, uṉ kaṇ nañcu civaperumāṉukkum tāṅkutal iyalātu!“(tañci kō. 135) eṉṟāṟpōlat talivaṉ taliviyip pukaḻtal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu itu. ‘pakaṟkuṟi’kkum urittu.	(na.a.149)
pukaḻum koṭumiyum -
takkavar eṉṟu uyarttik kūṟal pukaḻ; takātavar eṉṟu tāḻttik kūṟal koṭumi.
talivaṉ taliviyiṭattut taṅkiya kālattu avaṉit takkavaṉ eṉṟu pukaḻtalum, avaṉ parattiyariṭattut taṅkiya kālattut takātavaṉ eṉṟu ikaḻtalum vāyil kaḷukku uṇṭām; ākavē, talivikkup pukaḻ aṉṟik koṭumi illi eṉpatu.	(iṟi. a. 45,46)
‘pukaḻum māṉamum eṭuttu vaṟpuṟuttalum tūtiṭiyiṭṭa vaki’ -
muraṇpaṭṭa maṉṉar mutalāṉōr iruvariṭiyē ceṉṟu, ivvāṟu puriyiṉ pukaḻ eytum eṉavum, ivvāṟu ceyyiṉ māṉam varum eṉavum kiḷantu kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttali iṭiyē koṇṭa tūtiṟpiriyum vaki. itaṉ kaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum.	(to. akat. 43 ca. pāla.)
‘pukaḻum māṉamum eṭuttu vaṟpuṟuttit talivaṉ pirital’ -
talivaṉ taṉakkēṟṟa pukaḻiyum perumiyiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭi itaṉāl pirital vēṇṭum eṉṟu taliviyiyum tōḻiyiyum vaṟpuṟuttaṟkaṇ kūṟṟu nikaḻttutal.
	(to. po. 41 nac.)
ēṭṭuk kalvi toḻiṟkalvi ivaṟṟiṉ poruṭṭup pirintu payaṉ peṟṟuvaruvatāl kiṭṭum pukaḻiyum, piriyāmiyāl varum kuṟṟattiyum eṭuttuk kūṟutal.	(40 pārati)
poruḷīṭṭal, kalvi potunalappaṇi ceytal mutaliya-vaṟṟāṉ uḷatākiya pukaḻiyum, poruḷīṭṭāmi, kallāmi, potunalappaṇi ceyyāmi mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ uḷatākum kuṟṟattiyum talivikkum tōḻikkum kūṟit tāṉ mīṇṭu varumvari āṟṟiyirukkumāṟu kūṟutal.			(225 kuḻa.)
itaṉit tirukkōviyār uri ‘paki taṇiviṉip pirivu’ eṉavum, ‘vēntaṟkuṟṟuḻip pirivu’ eṉavum kūṟum. (pērāciriyar uric cūttiraṅkaḷ 22, 23)
‘pukaṉṟa uḷḷamoṭu putuvōr cāyaṟku, akaṉṟa kiḻavaṉip pulampunaṉi kāṭṭi, iyaṉṟa neñcam talippeytu arukki, etirpeytu maṟutta īrattu maruṅkiṉ’ talivi kūṟṟu -
putalvaṉi viḷiyāṭṭi virumpiṉa uḷḷattoṭē, putuvatu puṇarnta parattiyar taṉmāṭṭu maṉam nekiḻnta meṉmiyiṉporuṭṭu avarkku aruḷ ceyyap pirintu vantōṉi, taṉatu taṉimi mikavum aṟivittu, avaṉmēṟ ceṉṟa neñciṉic cellāmal avaṉiṭattiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭu arukap paṇṇi, piṟaruḷ oruttiyik kāṇāḷ āyiṉum kaṇṭāḷ pōlat taṉ muṉṉarp peytukoṇṭu, vāyil maṟuttataṉāṉ tōṉṟiya nayaṉuṭimikkaṇ talivi kūṟṟu nikaḻum eṉpatu. eṉavē, maṟuppāḷ pōla nayantāḷāyiṟṟu.
"ūraṉē! nī uṉparattiyi viṭuttu viḻākkālattil āṭiya makaḷiroṭu taṅkiyiruntatu kēṭṭu avaḷ kōpam koṇṭāḷ pōlum. taṉ mukattiṉ ceyaṟkiaḻaku keṭa aḻutu ēṅkit taṉ viralip palakālum tirukikkoṇṭu, paṟkaḷik kaṭittataṉāl avi nuṉi maḻuṅka, ūr muḻutum niṉṉit tūṟṟikkoṇṭu alikiṟāḷ. avaḷik kāṇac celvāyāka" eṉṟu talivi kaṟpaṉiyākat talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal;
“avaḷukku, eṉṉip pōlap putalvaṉip peṟṟuk koṇṭu periya maṉikkaṇ uṉṉiyiṉṟiyum varuvirun tōmpi illaṟam naṭattum kaṭamiyilliyē!” eṉṟal. 			(akanā. 176)
“tōḻi! talivarip pulattal kūṭumō? oru kālattil eṉṉit taḻuvutalil viruppam koṇṭār; ippoḻutu nāṉ avarit taḻuva iruppiṉum, putalvarkkup pāl koṭukkum niliyilirukkum nāṉ taḻuviṉ, tam mārpil pālpaṭṭu viṭum eṉa añcukiṟār. nammit taḻuvutali nīttuviṭṭār eṉpataṉi uṭkoṇṭu, vanta talivariṭam, ‘umakku ēṟṟavar parattiyarē; eṉputalvaṉ eṉṉiṭam aṉpuṭiyaṉ; nāṉ avaṉiṭam celkiṟēṉ’ eṉṟu mella avaṉiṭam ceṉṟēṉ. ‘makaṉiṭam eṉakkumāttiram āci yilliyā?’ eṉṟu kūṟit talivar “eṉ mutukiṉit taḻuva, pala kālam uḻappaṭṭa vayalil maḻinīr peyvatāl maṇ nekiḻvatupōla, eṉ neñcu avariṭam nekiḻa, pulattali viṭuttu avarkūṭṭattukku uṭaṉpaṭṭēṉ” eṉṟu tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal. (akanā. 26)	(to. po. 147 nac.)
"pukāak kālip pukketirp paṭṭuḻip, pakāa viruntiṉ pakutikkaṇ" talivi kūṟal -
uṇavu uṇṇum naṇpakaṟpoḻutilō iravilō talivi illattukkaṇ talivaṉ pukku etirppaṭṭavaḻi, avaṉi nīkkātu viruntiṉaṉāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭa ceyti paṟṟit talivi piṟaku tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“cuṭarttoṭīi! nam talivaṉ, aṉṟorunāḷ, nāṉum tāyum māttiramē irunta poḻutil nam illattiṟku vantu, ‘uṇṇu nīr vēṭṭēṉ’ eṉṟa kūṟa, aṉṉiyum eṉṉi nōkki, ‘uṇṇunīr ūṭṭi vā, eṉṉa, nāṉum talivaṉatu kuṟumpu aṟiyātu nīruṭaṉ avaṉirukkum iṭam cēra, avaṉ vaḷiyal aṇinta eṉ muṉkiyip paṟṟi varutta, nāṉum ceyvataṟiyātu, ‘aṉṉāy! ivaṉ ceyva taṉip pār’ eṉṟu kūccaliṭavē, aṉṉi alaṟik koṇṭu ōṭivantu’ ‘yātu nikaḻntatu?’ eṉṟa viṉava, nāṉ avaṉtavaṟṟik kūṟa virumpātu, ‘uṇṇunīr vikkiṉāṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟa, aṉṉi avaṉ mutukit taṭavik koṭuttup puri ēṟātavāṟu ceyyumpōtu, avaṉ eṉṉik kaṭik kaṇṇāl nōkkip puṉmuṟuval ceytāṉ” eṉṟa kūṟṟu. (kali. 51) 	(to. po. 107 nac.)
pukāk kālattup pukka talivaṉi viruntu ēlātu cevili iravum tuyilātāḷit talivi muṉintu kūṟal -
iravil uṇavu uṇṇum kālattu viruntiṉaṉāka vanta talivaṉi ēṟkātu viṭṭataṉōṭu, avaṉukkum taṉakkum ētō toṭarpu irukkum pōlum eṉṟu aiyuṟṟuc cevili iravu muḻutum uṟaṅkāmal taṉṉik kāttataṉāl, talivi avaḷic ciṉantu kūṟutal.
“ōriravu talivaṉ nam maṉikkaṇ viruntiṉaṉāka vara, cevili avaṉi ēṟṟukkoḷḷa maṟuttatōṭu, avaṉ varukiyi nammōṭu iṇittu aiyuṟṟu aṉṟiravu muḻutum uṟaṅkāmalēyē iruntāḷ. ataṉāl namakku iravukkuṟi piḻittatu. ittakiya tīṅku ceyta aṉṉi peṇkoli purinta naṉṉaṉ eytiya narakatti  aṭivāḷāka!” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (kuṟun. 292)	(to. po. 107 nac.)
puṭiyuri -
akapporuḷuri irupattēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu (kā. 91). eṭuttuk koṇṭa pāṭṭiṉ poruḷi māttiram kūṟāmal, ata ṉoṭu toṭarpuṭiya veḷicceytikaḷiyum kūṟutal.
	(vī. cō. 96)
puṇarkuṟi -
talivaṉtaliviyar cantikkum kuṟiyiṭam; ‘puṇarkuṟi ceyta pular kural ēṉal’ (akanā. 118). atu pakaṟkuṟiyum iravuk kuṟiyum eṉa iruvakippaṭum.
puṇarcci -
akaṉintiṇiyuḷ kuṟiñcitiṇikkuriya uripporuḷ puṇarcciyum puṇarcci nimittamum. otta aṉpuṭiya talivaṉtaliviyar kūṭṭam puṇarcciyām. uḷḷattāṟ puṇarum uḷḷappuṇarcciyum, meyyuṟṟum puṇarum meyyuṟu puṇarcciyum kaḷaviṉkaṇ nikaḻvaṉa. kaṟpiṉkaṇ kaḷaviṟpuṇarcciyum vatuvip puṇarcciyum talimakaṟku eytaluṇṭu; muṉṉatu kaḷaviṉ vaḻivanta kaṟpukkum piṉṉatu kaḷaviṉvaḻi vārāk kaṟpukkum uriya.	(na. a. 34, 58, 55)
puṇarcci urittal -
talivaṉ talivi iruvartam toṭarpu paṟṟiyum ōraḷavu tōḻi aṟinta piṉ, talivi piṟi toḻa maṟuttal mutaliya vaṟṟiyum kaṇṭu, piṉ avaḷuṭiya kaṇ civappuṟutalum utaṭu veḷuppēṟutalum mutalāyiṉa paṟṟi avaḷi viṉava, avaḷ tāṉ cuṉiyāṭiyatāl ēṟpaṭṭa vēṟupāṭu aḵtu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷāka, piṉṉarum viṭātu, “nī āṭiya cuṉi uṉkaṇkaḷic civakkac ceytu uṉ utaṭukaḷi veḷukkac ceytu uṉ taliyil aḻakiya malarkaḷic cūṭṭum āṟṟa luṭiyatō?” eṉṟāṟpōla avaḷi viṉavi, avaḷ talivaṉoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭamiyit tāṉ aṟiya luṟuvataṉik kuṟippāka veḷiyiṭutal.(na.a. 139 uri)
itu ‘cuṉi viyanturittal’ eṉavum paṭum.(i .vi. 507 uri)
itu ‘nāṇanāṭṭam’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru tuṟi.
	(kōvi. 70)
puṇarcci tuṇital -
taliviyik kaṇṭa talivaṉ avaḷ māṉuṭamakaḷē eṉṟu teḷintu, avaḷ taṉṉiṭattuk koṇṭa kātaliyum uṇarntu, avaḷik kūṭṭuvitta teyvatti makiḻntu vāḻtti avaḷik kūṭutaṟkut tuṇital.
itu ‘teyvappuṇarcci tuṇital’ eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
	(i. vi. 491 uri)
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 7)
puṇarcci makiḻtal -
taliviyuṭaṉ kūṭi iṉpuṟṟa talivaṉatu makiḻcci.
“peṇṇē! aimpoṟikaḷukkum uriya aimpula iṉpaṅkaḷi yum niṉpāl oruṅkē peṟṟēṉ” (ku. 1101) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟit talivaṉ makiḻtal.
itu ‘taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 127)
puṇarcciyiṉ makiḻtal (1) -
“ittalivi kaḻunīr malar; yāṉ ataṉkaṇ tēṉip parukum vaṇṭu” (kōvi. 123) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟit talivaṉ taliviyi nukartal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘uḷmakiḻn turittal’ eṉṉum. (123)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu, ‘iyital’ eṉappaṭum. itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’kkum uriyatu; muṉ mūvakip puṇarccikaḷilum nikaḻvatu.	(na. a. 149)
puṇarcciyiṉ makiḻtal (2) -
piṟa puṇarccikaḷil talivaṉ kūṟṟākavē varum ikkiḷavi, parattiyiṟ pirivil nikaḻum ūṭalil, uṇartta uṇarntu ūṭal taṇinta niliyil talivaṉum taliviyum kūṭi iṉpuṟṟa ciṟappiṉik kaviyē kūṟuvatāka nikaḻum.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uṇartta uṇarum ūṭal.	(na. a. 205)
puṇarcciyiṉ vitumpal -
vēṭki mītūrtaliṉ talivi kalavikku viriyum uḷḷatti ṉaḷāy ataṉit tōḻikku urittal.
“tōḻi! eṉ niṟiyum nāṇum aḻintami pōṉṟē eṉ vaḷiyalkaḷum kiyiṉiṉṟum kaḻaṉṟu pōyiṉa; nīyum tuṇikku iṅku illi. inniliyil kātal mikuntu ēṅkiya eṉ pōṉṟōr kalavikku iṇaṅkāmal ūṭit taṉittiruttal iyalumā?” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.		(ampikā. 512)
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
puṇarcci vitumpal
vitumpal - virital. kaṟpukkālattil illaṟam naṭattum talivaṉum taliviyum tammuḷ pulattaṟkuk kāraṇam uḷ vaḻiyum vēṭki mikutiyāl pulavātu puṇarccikkaṇṇē virital.	(kuṟaḷ ati. 129 parimē.)
puṇartal (1) -
kūṭi makiḻtal; iḵtu ellāp puṇarcciyilum varum tuṟi.
tam illattil iruntukoṇṭu tām īṭṭiya poruḷip piṟarkkum pakuttuk koṭuttu uṇṭāṟpōla, ittalivi yiṉ kūṭṭam eṉakku iṉpam tarukiṟatu” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(kuṟaḷ 1107)
puṇartal (2) -
talivaṉ taliviyi viti kūṭṭi vikka uḷḷattāṉum meyyāṉum kūṭutal. nālvaki nilattum puṇarcci nikaḻumēṉum, pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟkuriya muṟpaṭṭa kaḷavup puṇarcciyē puṇartaṟ ciṟappuṭiyatu. ippuṇartal eṉṟa oḻukkam kuṟiñci eṉappaṭum. itaṟkuriya nilam maliyum malicārnta iṭamumām; kālam kūtirum muṉpaṉiyumākiya perumpoḻutum, iṭiyāmamākiya ciṟupoḻutum ām.
puṇartal eṉpataṉkaṇ iyaṟkippuṇarcci, iṭantalip pāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam, pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉpaṉavum, pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉ pakutiyākiya pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi mutaliyaṉavum aṭaṅkum.
puṇartal nimittam -
talivaṉum taliviyum puṇarnta piṉṉar nikaḻum pirivacca mum, vaṉpuṟiyum, talivaṉ nīṅkiyamiyum, pāṅkaṟku urittaṉavum, pāṅkaṉ kaḻaṟiyaṉavum, talivaṉ etirmaṟut taṉavum, pāṅkaṉ etirntaṉavum, nīṅkiyamiyum tali makaḷik kaṇṭamiyum, āṟṟāṉ āyiṉamiyum, talivaṟku vanturittaṉavum, talivaṉ ceṉṟamiyum, talivaṉ taliviyit talippeytamiyum ittoṭakkattaṉa vellām talivikkum tōḻikkum ivvakiyāṉē nōkki vikaṟpikka, aviyellām puṇartal nimittamāy aṭaṅkum.	(iṟi. a. 1 uri)
puṇartal pirital iruttal iraṅkal ūṭal eṉṟa muṟi vippiṟkuriya kāraṇam -
akapporuḷāvatu puṇarcciyākalāṉum atu talivaṉ taliviyarākiya iruvarkkum oppa nikaḻtalāṉum puṇarc ciyi muṟkūṟi, puṇarntuḻiyallatu pirivu iṉmiyāṉum atu talivaṉkaṇṇatākiya ciṟappāṉum talivi piriviṟkup pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku iṉmiyāṉum piriviṉi ataṉpiṉ kūṟi, pirintuḻit talivi āṟṟiyiruttal mulli yātaliṉ ataṉi aṭuttuk kūṟi, aṅṅaṉam āṟṟiyirukkum talivi talivaṉ mīṇṭu varuvatākak kuṟitta paruvam kaṭantavaḻi iraṅkutali ataṉpiṉ kūṟi, innāṉku poruṭkum potuvākalāṉum kāmattiṟkuc ciṟattalāṉum ūṭali ataṉpiṉ kūṟi immuṟiyē vittār āciriyar eṉpatu.
	(to. po. 14 nac.)
puṇartal mutaliya uripporuḷ -
puṇartalāvatu kūṭutaluṟutalum piriviṉṟi uṭaṉuṟitalum. itu talivaṉ talivi iruvarkkum uriyatu.
piritalāvatu kaḷaviṉkaṇ oruvaḻittaṇattal variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital eṉṉumivi kāraṇamāka vum, kaṟpiṉkaṇ ōtal - paki - tūtu - kāval - poruḷ - paratti eṉṉumivi kāraṇamākavum talivaṉ talivi yip pirintu cēyaṉātalum ayal uṟitalum ām. itu talivaṉukku uriyatu.
kaṟpiṟkuriya pirivukaḷuḷ ōtalum parattiyum tavirnta ēṉiyavi ciṟupāṉmi kaḷaviṉkaṇṇum nikaḻtaluṇṭu. iruttal, iraṅkal, ūṭal eṉpaviyum talivaṉ pirinta vaḻi nikaḻvaṉa ātaliṉ avi oruvāṟu pirivuḷ aṭaṅkum. atu tōṉṟap piritali iraṇṭāvatāka vittār. ivvāṟṟāṉ aka oḻukalāṟukaḷ puṇarvu pirivu eṉṉum iruvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
iruttalāvatu kaṭami kāraṇamāka aṟavaḻiyil, talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟaviṭattē avaṉ kuṟitta paruvam varumaḷavum talivi appiriviṉip poṟuttukkoṇṭu illattil uṟitalām. itu talivikkurittu. talivaṉ pācaṟikkaṇ āṟṟiyiruttalum iruttaliṉpāṟpaṭum.
iraṅkalāvatu oruvaḻittaṇattal nīṭṭikkumiṭattum, talivaṉ kuṟitta paruvam kaḻiyumiṭattum pirivāṟṟāta talivi varuntutal. itu talivikkē uriyatu.
ūṭalāvatu talivaṉ parattami karutiṉāṉāka niṉintum parattami yuṟṟāṉākat terintum talivi citintum muṉintum nontum talivaṉatu kūṭṭatti maṟuttalum paḻittalumām. itu talivikkurittu. uṇarppuvayiṉ talivi ūṭal tavirātavaḻic ciṟupāṉmi talivaṉ nontu kūṟutal itaṉpāṟpaṭum.	(to. akat. 116 ca.pāla.)
puṇarntuṭaṉ pōyakāli iṭiccurattup paṭṭataṉit talivaṉ mīṇṭuvanta kālattut tōḻikkuc collutal -
“tōḻi! taliviyum yāṉum uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟapōtu talivikkup pātiri, atiral, marā ākiya malarkaḷi aṇivittu avaḷ piṉṉaḻakik kāṇa virumpic ciṟitu tūram eṉ muṉṉē naṭantu cellumāṟu kūṟiṉēṉ. avaḷ ataṟku nāṇi, taṉ māṉ pōṉṟa pārviyāl eṉṉi nōkkit taliyit tāḻttikkoṇṭāḷ. nāṉum muṉṉē cella maṉamiṉṟi orunāḷ curattilēyē avaḷōṭu taṅkiviṭṭēṉ” (akanā. 261) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 45 nac.)
puṇarntuṭaṉ varuvōrip porunti viṉātal -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻtta avaḷit tēṭivarap piṉtoṭarnta cevili vētiyari viṉavi atu vaḻiyākac ceṉṟavaḷ, taṉṉi nōkki varum talivaṉtaliviyarākiya vēṟṟavar iruvari nōkki, “uṅkaḷip pārttatum, eṉmakaḷum avaḷkaṇa vaṉum mīṇṭuviṭṭaṉareṉa makiḻntēṉ. nummip pōṉ ṟār iruvar vaḻiyiṭic cellak kaṇṭīrō?” eṉa viṉavutal.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.		(kōvi. 244)
puṇarā irakkam -
taliviyik kūṭappeṟāta kikkiḷit talivaṉ varuntutal.
“ivaḷi ippoḻutē ceṉṟu taḻuvik kūṭi iṉpuṟṟā laṉṟi eṉṉāl uyir vāḻtalē muṭiyātu!” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘āṇpāṟkūṟṟu; kikkiḷikkaṇṇatoru tuṟi.
	(pu. ve. mā. 14-8)
putalvaṟ pulattal -
talivi makaṉiṭam kōpittal.
parattiillam nōkkic cellum uṉ tantiyit teruvil kaṇṭu aḻutu aḻittu uṉṉit tūkkik koḷ ḷac ceytu uṉ vāyeccilāl avar aṇinta cantaṉam citiya avarip pulli iṅṅaṉam iṉpam ceyta makaṉē! iṉimēl uṉakkup payantukoṇṭē uṉtantiyār iṅku vārār. nī yārit tūkkik koḷḷac colluvāy?” (ampikā. 503) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivi taṉ makaṉi vekuḷvāḷ pōlat talivaṉatu puṟattoḻukkattik kaṭintu kūṟutal.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum pakutikkaṇ ‘uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉum uṭpiriviṉkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
	(i. vi. 555 uri)
“putalvaṉ kūṟiya māṟṟam talimakaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kūṟak kēṭṭāṉ” eṉpataṟinta paratti ataṟkup pulantu talimakaṟkuc colliyatu.
“nāriyiṉ pārppu aḻikkum kural vayalveḷiyellām kēṭkum ūraṉ nī. uṉputalvaṉ aḻittu uṉṉiṭam kūṟiya coṟkaḷik kēṭṭuc ceyaṟpaṭum nī, iṉi eṅkaḷiṭam aṉpuṟṟiruttal aritu. uṉ maṉivi illattiṟkē ceṉṟu avaḷoṭu vāḻvāyāka!” eṉṟu paratti talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.	(aiṅ. 86)
putalvaṉmēl vittup pulavi tīrtal -
kaṟpukkālattil parattiyiṟ pirinta talivaṉ mīṇṭu illattukku varap pulavi tīrntu avaṉik kūṭi iṉpuṟut tiya talivi, mīṇṭum avaṉ parattiyarmāṭṭup pirintu vara, atu kaṇṭu vāyil maṟukka, talivaṉ vāyilil viḷiyāṭik koṇṭirunta putalvaṉit tūkkikkoṇṭu avaṉi vāyilākak koṇṭu uḷḷē vara, talivi makaṉi vāṅki aṇittukkoṇṭu talivaṉ puṟattoḻukkam paṟṟip pala pēcip putalvaṉmēl vittup pulavi nīṅkiyatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 396)
putalvaṉmēl vittup pulaviyiṉ nīṭal -
talivi taṉ putalvaṉ talivaṉuṭiya kāmakkiḻattiyar palaruṭiya illaṅkaḷukkum aḻittuc cellappaṭṭu mīṇṭatit taṉ tōḻiyāl aṟintu varuntiyirukkum pōtu, parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ vāyil vēṇṭa, talivi putalvaṉi  vekuḷvāḷ pōla avaṉuṭiya puṟattoḻukkatti yeṭuttuk kūṟi avaṉiṭam kōpam nīṭṭittal.
“makaṉē! poyyiyē meyyākap pāvikkum parattiyar cērikkuc ceṉṟu, apparattiyarāl tūkki vittuk koḷḷappaṭṭuk koṇṭāṭi muttamiṭappaṭṭu vantuḷḷa nī, avar vāyt tampalam paṭṭa eccil vāyiṉāl eṉṉiṭam pāl arunta vārātē!” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.			(mā. a. pāṭal. 470)
putalvaṉmēl vittu vāyil maṟuttal -
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu vantu vāyil vēṇṭi niṉṟāṉākat talivi, tāṉ makap peṟṟa kālamtoṭṭē avaṉ parattiyartoṭarpu koṇṭa ceytiyit taṉ makaṉmēl vittuk kūṟuvāḷ pōlat talivaṉukku vāyil maṟuttatu.
“pāṭakam eṉṟa ūriluḷḷa taliva! ipputalvaṉi nāṉ peṟṟa mukūrttam eṉakkaḷitta vāḻviṉ perumi col lumpaṭikku eḷiyatoṉṟō? eṉ talimikkup porun tuvatāka eṉmēl vitta niṉ puttiyip parattimi ceyvittatōṭu, eṉakku maṅkip paruvattut taṅki yoruttiyi uṇṭākki vāḻvittatē!” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(mā. a. pā. 548)
putalvaṉ vāyil vēṇṭal -
“taliviyē! uṉ ūṭal taṇivikka vēṇṭi nam talivaṉ aṉuppa muṉpu vanta pāṇarum pākarum pōla vilakkak kūṭiya vāyil aṉṟu itu. uṉ putalvaṉiyē taḻuvi. vāyilāka eṭuttukkoṇṭu varukiṟāṉ ātaliṉ, iṉiyum uṉ ūṭal nīṭṭittal kūṭātu” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 502)
putalvaṉip pulantu talivi maṟuttāḷip pōlat tōḻikku vāyil nērntatu -
“tōḻi! parattiyar putumaṇaviḻāk koṇṭāṭa vēṇṭi muḻaviṉi olikka, ataṉik kēṭṭa nam talivaṉ tērēṟic ceṉṟapōtu avaṉi iṭippaṭṭu eṉputalvaṉ ceṉṟu kaṇṭatāl, talivaṉ putumaṇam puṇarappeṟātu iṭa ruṟṟami eṉakkup perunakippiṉi viḷikkiṟatu!” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (ivvāṟu kūṟi, uḷḷārat talivaṉpāl koṇṭirunta kātalāl avaṉi ēṟṟukkoṇṭāḷ eṉpatu kuṟippu.)	(ain. aim. 26)
putalvaṉi vāyil nērvittal -
taliviyiṉ ūṭalit tīrkkum vāyilākap putalvaṉi eṭuttukkoṇṭu avaḷiṭam celvāyeṉat tōḻi talivaṉukkuk kūṟutal.
“talivaṉē! uṉ ciṟuputalvaṉ ōr iḷaṅkaḷiṟu. nī kaḷavuk kālattil periya mutukaḷiṟṟik kāyntu veruṭṭiya tiṟaluṭiyi; ātaliṉ uṉ putalvaṉākiya inta iḷa yāṉikku añcāmal avaṉiyē taḻuvi eṭuttukkoṇṭu taliviyiṭam ceṉṟu avaḷ ūṭalit taṇippāyāka.“
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu’	(ampikā. 501)
puri -
maruta nilattūr 	(cūṭā. ē- 36)
`pullittōṉṟum kikkiḷi' -
kāmakkuṟippiṟku amitiyillāta iḷamipparuvattā ḷoruttiyiṭam talivaṉoruvaṉ avaḷit taṉakku maṉikkiḻattiyākak kōṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟu maruntu piṟitillāp peruntuyar eyti, tāṉ naṉmi ceytavaṉāka vum avaḷ tīmi ceytavaḷākavum palavāṟu pēci, accollukku avaḷiṭamiruntu maṟumoḻi peṟātu, piṉṉum tāṉē colli iṉpuṟutal eṉṟumē kikkiḷi yātaliṉ kikkiḷi yātaṟkuc ciṟantatām.(to. po. 50 nac.)
pullēm eṉal -
itu pulavi poruḷāt tōṉṟiya pāṭāṇpāṭṭu ākum. (cāmi. 146) ; atu kāṇka.
itu ‘pulavip pāṭāṇpāṭṭu’ eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
	(ivi. 617 - 45)
pulantu kūṟal -
talivaṟkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi taliviyi aṇukit talivaṉ nili kūṟavum, nāṇattāl talivi nēriṭiyāka icivu terivikkātataṉāl, tōḻi vekuṇṭu, taṉṉiṉum mēmpaṭṭa naṇpiṉarkaḷik kalantu talivi tāṉ virumpi yatic ceyka eṉavum, taṉ tavaṟṟiṉi avaḷ poṟuttiṭuka eṉavum kūṟutal.

itaṉit ‘tōḻi taliviyi muṉital’ eṉpa. 	(na. a. 148)
itu ‘kuṟi nayappuk kūṟal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 87)
pulampal -
talivi talivaṉiṉṟit taṉittu varuntutalum, talivaṉ taliviyiṉṟit taṉittu varuntutalum ām. ‘pulampē taṉimi’.	(to. co. 331 cēṉā.)  (ṭu)
pulampaṉ -
neytal nilat talivaṉukkuriya tiṇi nilippeyar.			(na.a. 24)
pularntapiṉ vaṟuṅkaḷam talivi kaṇṭiraṅkal -
maṟunāḷ poḻutu viṭintatum, talivi iravukkuṟiyiṭa mākiya tāḻimarattaṭiyil talivaṉuṭiya māli irukkak kaṇṭu, appēṟuṟṟa tāḻiyi vāḻttip pulamputal.
“puṉṉikaḷē! tāḻikaḷē! ‘iravil yāṉ iṅku vantu pōṉa piṉ talivar iṅku vantārā?’ eṉṟa ceytiyic colluṅkaḷ” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(kappaṟ. 171)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 160)
pulari vikaṟi -
katiravaṉ tōṟṟatti yoṭṭiya maṟunāḷ pattu nāḻiki yaḷavum viṭiyal eṉavum, kāli eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. mutal nāḷ iraviṉ iṟutip pattu nāḻikiyaḷavum vikaṟi eṉappaṭum. vikaṟiyiṉ iṟutiyum viṭiyaliṉ toṭakka mum ākiya iraṇṭaṉiyum oṭṭiya nēram pularivikaṟi yām.
vikaṟikkālak kaḻivum pularutal muṉṉum ākiya ak kālam eṉpar aṭiyārkkunallār.	(cilap. 14 - 3)
pulavi (1) -
talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ illaṟam nikaḻttumpōtu puṇarcciyāṉ vanta makiḻcci kuṟipaṭāmal kālam karutik koṇṭu uyppatōr uḷḷanikaḻcci. aḵtāvatu talivaṉ māṭṭuk kuṟṟam. ētum iṉṟākavum, avaṉiṭam kōpam koḷvatu pōlak kāṭṭiṉ, akkōpattit taṇikka avaṉ ceyyum taṇṇaḷi piṉṉarp pēriṉpam taruvatu eṉpataṉi uṭkoṇṭu talivi nikaḻttum ceyal. talivaṉ taliviyiṭam kuḷirppak kūṟit taḷirppat taḻuvi avaḷpulaviyi nīkkuvāṉ. atu ‘pulavi veyyaḷyām muyaṅkuṅ kālē’ eṉa varum.	(to. po. 499 pērā.)
ippulavi poruḷākap pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉiyē kiḷavittalivaṉākak kūṟuvataṉip puṟapporuḷ pāṭāṇ tuṟiyāka veṇpāmāli kūṟum.	(pu. ve. 9:47)
pulavi (2) -
talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa iruvar neñcum puṇarcci virumpātu ataṉic ciṟitu veṟuttāṟ pōṉṟu irukkak karutiyavaḻi, kāraṇam etuvum illātupōyiṉum, tāmē oru kāraṇam kaṟpittukkoṇṭu oruvarōṭoruvar kōpam koṇṭavar pōla oḻukutal 			(kuṟaḷ ati. 111 parimē.)
pulavi āṟṟuvittu ikuḷi col -
tōḻi talivaṉi āṟṟuvittuk kūṟal.	(cāmi. 94)
itu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇṇatu.
pulavi kaṟpu oṉṟāl naṇṇic cīṟal -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉiṭam talivitāṉ kaṟpittuk koṇṭa pulaviyiṉāl avaṉi aṇuki avaṉ puṟattoḻukkatti iḻittuk kūṟal. (maruta naṭiyiyal).
	(vī. cō. 95 uri mēṟ.)
pulavi nīkkik kūṭiya talivaṉ taṉṉuḷḷē colliyatu -
“naṟkuṇaṅkaḷum takutiyumuṭiya talivarkkum tam kātaliyar tammiṭam mikavum kaṭumiyāka ūṭuvatē iṉpam payappatu.” tāṉ nukarnta iṉpa mikuti ūṭalukkup piṉ nērntatu eṉpataṉāl pulaviyip pārāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ.	(kuṟaḷ 1305)
pulavi nuṇukkam -
pulavikku nuṇṇitāṉa kāraṇamoṉṟu kaṟpittuk kūṟal.
kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉum taliviyum oru cēkkiyil kūṭiyiruntavaḻi, talivaṉiṭam kōpamkoḷḷa oru kāraṇamum illāta niliyilum, kātal mikutalāṉ nuṇṇitākiyatoru kāraṇattit tēṭik kaṇṭupiṭittu ataṉit talivaṉmēl ēṟṟik kūṟit talivi ciṟukōpam koḷḷutal. nuṇukkamāṉa kāraṇattāl ēṟpaṭṭa ciṟu kōpam ‘pulavi nuṇukkam’ eṉappaṭṭatu.			(kuṟaḷ. ati. 132 parimē.)
pulavippōli -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu pakkattu ūrukkuc ceṉṟakkālum, ōriru nāḷkaḷ talivaṉ taliviyi maṟantavaṉ pōṉṟu kuṟiyiṭattu vārātirup piṉum, kaḷavilē kaṟpukkaṭam pūṇṭu oḻukum talivi, taṉvayiṉ urimiyum talivaṉ taṉṉiṭattu uṇmiyāṉa aṉpuṭiyaṉallaṉ eṉṟu avaṉvayiṉ ayaṉmiyum tōṉṟa, uḷḷattil pulavikoḷvāḷ. kaḷavuk kālattil ūṭalum uṇarttalum veḷippaṭa nikaḻāmiyiṉ, itaṉip pulavi eṉṉātu ‘pulavip pōli’ eṉṟu koḷ ḷal vēṇṭum.
kuṟi piḻittavaḻit talivaṉ pulattal uṇṭu. ap pulaviyit tōḻi nīkkuvāḷ. ātaliṉ itaṉiyum ‘pulavippōli’ eṉṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. itu talivaṉukkē uriyatu.			(to. po. 111 nac. uri)
pulavi poruḷāt tōṉṟiya pāṭāṇpāṭṭu -
talivi talivaṉi nōkki, “cantaṉam pūciya niṉ mārpiṉi ivvūrp parattiyar taḻuviya kāṭciyi ivvikaṟiyil niṉ mārpakattuk kāṇappaṭum kuṟikaḷāl kaṇṭu terinta nāṉ, palarum nukarak koṭukkappaṭṭa potuccelva mākiya niṉ mārpiṉit taḻuvutali viḻiyēṉ” eṉṟāṟ pōlap pulantu kūṟutal. ittuṟikkup pāṭṭuṭittali vaṉē kiḷavittalivaṉ āvāṉ.	(pu. ve. mā. 9 - 47)
pulaviyuṭ pulampal -
peruntiṇit talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ ūṭit taṉimiyil varuntutal.
talivaṉ taṇittapiṉpum talivi ūṭal taṇiyātu avaṉatu māliyi iḻuttuc citittup piṉ tuyaruṟṟup pulamputal.
itu puṟapporuḷil peruntiṇiyiṉ oru pakutiyāṉa peṇpāṟ kūṟṟil oru tuṟi.	(pu. ve. mā. 16-8)
pulaṉeṟikkuc ciṟanta pākkaḷ -
kalippāvum, paripāṭalumē pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟkuc ciṟanta pākkaḷ. vāyuṟi vāḻttu, ceviyaṟivuṟūu, puṟaṉili vāḻttu, kikkiḷi eṉṟa poruṇmikkaṇ maruṭpā ciṟantu varum.
kalippā aintiṇip poruḷāya pulaṉeṟivaḻakkil kāmamum, kikkiḷi peruntiṇiyākiya ulakiyalē paṟṟiya pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkil kāmamum paṟṟi varum. paripāṭal teyva vāḻttu uṭpaṭak kāmapporuḷ kuṟittu ulakiyalē paṟṟi varum.	(to. po. 53 nac.)
pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam -
pulavarāl pāṭutaṟku aminta pulavar mēṟkoṇṭa vaḻakkam.
akaṉintiṇiyākiya kāmapporuḷē pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkat tukkuc ciṟantatu; ēṉiya kikkiḷiyum peruntiṇiyum attuṇic ciṟantila. pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkattiṟkuk kalippāvum paripāṭalumē ciṟantavi; ēṉiya āciriyam veṇpā vañci maruṭpā eṉpaṉa attuṇic ciṟantila.
iḵtu uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka illatu puṇarkkum nāṭaka ilakkaṇam pōla, yātāṉum orōvaḻi orucārārmāṭṭu ulakiyalāṉ nikaḻum oḻukkattiṉi ellārkkum potu vākki iṭaṉum kālamum niyamittuc ceyyappaṭuvatāl illateṉappaṭātu ulakiyalēyām. 			(to. po. 53 nac., mu. vī. 378)
cuvipaṭa varuvaṉa ellām ōriṭattu vantaṉavākat tokuttu ulakattār oḻukalāṟṟoṭu cērttuk kūṟuvatu.			(po. 56 iḷa.)
yāḻōr kūṭṭam ulakiyalātaliṉ, illatu aṉṟām. 			(po. 498 pērā.)
akattiṇip pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamellām makkaḷ vāḻkki yiṉ meyyiyalpukaḷiyē taḻuvi naṭakkum. (po. 52 pārati)
ulaka vaḻakkaṅkaḷil ciṟantaṉavāyuḷḷavaṟṟic cuvipaṭac cila variyaṟikaḷoṭu kūṟuvatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam.			(po. 42 kuḻa.)
ilakkaṇa neṟiyāṉ amintu varum nallicip pulavōr tam eḻuvakic ceyyuḷ marapu. pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkākiya ceyyu ḷiṉkaṇ akapporuḷ paṟṟi varuvaṉa iruvaki vaḻakkum kalantē varum.	(to. akat. 55 ca. pāla.)
cuvi mikuti payappa ulaka naṭiyōṭu oppum ovvāmiyum uṭiyavākkik kūṟutal pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam. (kāmattuppāl tōṟṟuvāy. parimē.)
iḵtu illatu iṉiyatu nallatu eṉṟu pulavarāl nāṭṭap paṭṭatōr oḻukkam. 	(iṟi. a. 1 uri)
illōṉ talivaṉāka varum toṭarnilic ceyyuḷ poypporuḷ kuṟippaṉa.	(to. co. 155 cēṉā.)
puḷ ōtimattōṭu pulampal -
ayalār taṉṉi alar kūṟutalit talivi, piṟa paṟavi kaḷuṭaṉum aṉṉattuṭaṉum kūṟip pulampal.
“tattam āṇkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭi uṟiyum aṉṟiṟ paṟavikaḷē! aṉṉaṅkaḷē! eṉ kātalar pirintamiyāl eṉṉip paḻi kūṟum peṇkaḷ, tammuṭiya kātalarkaḷi viṭṭup piritali aṟiyārkaḷō? uṅkaḷ kātalarkaḷ piriyap puṟappaṭṭāl nīṅkaḷum avaruṭaṉēyē ceṉṟuviṭuṅkaḷ. iṉṟēl, ivvūrp peṇkaḷ uṅkaḷiyum ēcip pēcuvar!“
iḵtu ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 292)
puḷḷuvaṉ -
pāli nila makaṉ. (cūṭā. aiai 67)
puṟattoḻukkam -
parattiyaroṭu kūṭi yoḻukutal.	(to. po. 205 nac.)
puṟattoḻukkil neṭunāḷ oḻukit takāteṉa viṭuttut taliviyoṭu kūṭi oḻukāniṉṟa talivaṉ, “yāṉ avvāṟu oḻuka nīyir niṉitta tiṟam yātu?” eṉṟāṟkut tōḻi colliyatu -
“‘nāṭu kāvaṟporuṭṭu aracaṉ vāḻka! viruntāṟṟutaṟ poruṭṭu nel mutaliya viḷivu polika! iravalarkku ītaṟporuṭṭup poṉ mika uṇṭākuka!’ eṉṟu talivi illaṟattukku vēṇṭuvaṉa virumpi oḻukiyatallatu piṟitu niṉintilaḷ. avaḷ aṅṅaṉamāka, nī oḻukum takāta oḻukkattāl uṉakkum uṉ tuṇivaṉākiya pāṇaṉukkum tīṅku varum eṉṟañci, ‘nīyum pāṇaṉum vāḻka!’ eṉṟu yāṅkaḷ vēṇṭiṉōm” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 1)
puṟanakarp pōkku -
talivaṉ tāṉ putiyaḷākak kikkoṇṭa parattiyoṭu makiḻat taliviyi viṭuttu apparattiyit tēril ēṟṟik koṇṭu avaḷoṭu cōliyil viḷiyāṭavum putunīril tiḷikkavum ūrppuṟappakutikaḷukkup pōtal.	(na. a. 66)
puṉṉam pulari -
viṭiyaṟku muṉṉarttākiya vikaṟiyiṉ iṟutippakuti; nīrāṭaṟku innērattē puṟappaṭṭuc cellutal marapu. ‘pulari vikaṟi’ eṉpatum atu.	(pari. 6-58)
puṉattiṭik kaṇṭu makiḻtal -
taliviyit tiṉippuṉattilē kaṇṭu talivaṉ makiḻtal. 
“meṉmiyāṉa uṭalum eḻilum koṇṭa ivaḷi ik kuṟavar tiṉippuṉam kākka vittuḷḷami avarkaḷuṭiya aṟiviṉmiyiyē kāṭṭuvatu!” (maturik kōvi. 78) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅki mati uṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(i. vi. 507 uri)
puṉam kaṇṭu makiḻtal -
taliviyum pāṅkiyum orucēra vantu taṉṉi makiḻvikkum vakiyil aminta tiṉippuṉattik kaṇṭu talivaṉ makiḻtal.
“aṉṉa naṭi koṇṭa aḻakiyāṉa eṉ kātaliyum avaḷ tōḻiyum paraṇil ēṟit tōṉṟak kāraṇamāṉa tiṉik katirik koyyavarum ikkiḷikaḷukku nāṉ yātu kimmāṟu ceyyavallēṉ!” (ampikā. 83) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkimati uṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(i. vi. 507)
puṉaltaru puṇarcci -
veḷḷa nīriṭiyē vīḻnta taliviyit talivaṉ kātta viṭattu avviruvarkkum uṇṭāṉa kūṭṭam.			(to. po. 114 nac. uri)
puṉal taru puṇarcciyāl tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟṟal -
nīrveḷḷattit toṭarpupaṭuttit talivikkut taliva ṉoṭu kūṭṭam nērntatākat tōḻi ceviliyiṭam kūṟutal.
“aṉṉāy! nāṅkaḷ āṟṟil kuṭintu nīrāṭuṅkāl, taliviyi oru nīrccuḻi koṇṭōṭi oḷikka, etir pārāmal aṅku vanta talivaṉ katumeṉa veḷḷattil pāyntu ceṉṟu avaḷit tuṉpa moṉṟum nērātavāṟu taḻuvik koṇarntu kari cērttāṉ. talivi avaṉ niṉivākavē uḷḷāḷ” (tañci. kō. 301) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 177)
puṉal viḷiyāṭṭil tammuḷ urittal -
kaṟpukkālattil, talimakaṉatu puṟattoḻukkam kāraṇamāka avaṉuṭaṉ puṉalāṭiya parattiyar tōḻiyar, apparatti yari viḷittu, “nammi viṭap pēraḻakuṭiya orutti vantu talivaṉit tirittuk koḷḷak koṭuttup piṉ varun tātu talivaṉi muṉṉuṟak kāppōm” eṉat tammuḷ kūṟik koḷvatu.
itu “parattiyiṟ pirivu” eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru tuṟi.	(kōvi. 371)
puṉalāṭṭuvittami kūṟip pulattal -
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu vantu taliviyi vāyil vēṇṭip peṟṟu avaḷoṭu paḷḷiyiṭattāṉāka, talivi, “nī ceyta ēṉiya piḻikaḷip poṟukkalām. palarum aṟiyap paratti yoruttiyip putuppuṉalāṭṭi vantu oṉṟum aṟiyātāy pōla naṭikkum ceyal poṟukka voṇṇātatu” eṉṟu parattiyip puṉalāṭṭuvitta ceytiyik kūṟip pulavi yuṟutal.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirital’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatorukūṟṟu.
puṉintatu niṉiyal -
taliviyiṉ kulinta oppaṉikaḷi avaḷtōḻi puṉiyu māṟu puṉintatākat talivaṉ niṉittu avaḷiṭam kūṟutal.
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉāl eytiya pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyik kūṭiya piṉṉar avaḷuṭiya kulinta āṭi aṇikaḷit tirutti, “aḻakiya māliyi aṇintavaḷē! kalaṅkātē. piṟar ciṟitum aiyuṟātavakiyil, niṉkātal tōḻi niṉakku aṇi ceyyumāṟu pōlavē niṉ iyaṟki vaṉappukkup pēraḻaku ceyyum vakiyil niṉ āṭi aṇi kaḷic ciṟanta muṟiyil tirutti aḻaku ceytuḷḷēṉ” eṉṟu avaḷ kavali tīrak kūṟutal.
itu ‘tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
itaṉik kaḷaviyaṟkāriki ‘kōlam ceyturittal’ eṉṉum pak. 75.	(ta. ne. vi.pak. 24)
puṉinturi -
1. mikippaṭutti aḻakuṟac collum vācakam. 2. pāyiram.
ulakiyali oṭṭiyum oṭṭātum kaṟpaṉi ceytu kūṟuvatu puṉinturi. aḵtu uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatu puṇarttalum, uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka illatu puṇartta lum, illōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatu puṇarttalum illōṉ talivaṉāka illatu puṇarttalum eṉa nāṉku vakippaṭum.
ippuṉinturi nāṭakattukkup payaṉpaṭuvatu; nāṭaka uṟuppup patiṉāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟākiya ‘yōṉi’ eṉṉum uṟuppāl kuṟippiṭappaṭum ceytiyākum.			(to. po. 3 nac.) (kaṇēcayyar aṭik.)
pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākiya ceyyuḷ vaḻakkattiṟkup puṉin turi paṟṟiya ceytikaḷi iṇittuk koḷvatum iṉṟi yamiyātatu.
pūkkam -
maruta nilattūr (tivā. pak. 110); ‘pūriyam’ eṉpatum atu.
pūṅkoṭi aṉpu pakartal -
tōḻi taliviyi, “tirumaṇam naṭakkumaḷavum evvāṟu āṟṟiyiruntāy?” eṉṟu viṉaval.
“taliviyē! talivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi muyaṅkik kaṇamum pirita lāṟṟātu nī itupōtu kāṭṭum ittaṉi aṉpum kātalum, nam cuṟṟattār uṉṉi iṟceṟittapōtum, talivaṉ variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟapōtum eṅkē oḷintiruntaṉavō?” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. 	(ampikā. 439)

iḵtu ‘ilvāḻkki’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
pūttaru puṇarcci -
taṉakku eṭṭāta marattiṉiṉṟum tāṉ virumpiya pūvip paṟittutaviya talivaṉpāl naṉṟi pārāṭṭit talivi avaṉik kūṭum kūṭṭam. (to.po. 114 nac. uri) ip puṇarcciyum piṟavum tōḻi cevilikkuk kūṟi, avaḷ naṟṟāykkuk kūṟutali nikaḻttuvippāḷ eṉpatu.
pūppiyal urittal -
talivi tāṉ tiṅkaḷtōṟum aṭiyum pūppu eytiya ceytiyic cēṭi vāyilākat talivaṟkut terivittal.
kaḷavu oḻukip piṉ varintu kaṟpuneṟi cāṉṟa illaṟam nikaḻttum talivaṉ taṉṉoṭu kūṭi oḻukātu, tāṉ  pūppeytiya piṉ paṉṉiru nāḷkaḷ taṉṉoṭu uṟintu piṉ, parattiyar illattuc ceṉṟu taṅkiya nilimikkaṇ, talivi tāṉ maṟu pūppeytiya ceytiyic cevvaṇi aṇinta cēṭi orutti vāyilākat talivaṟkup paratti yillattiṟkuc ceṉṟu terivikkum avalanili poruntāk kāmap pakutiyāka, akapporuṭ peruntiṇit tuṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka orucār āciriyarāl koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(na. a. 243)
peṭṭa vāyil peṟṟuc cēṟal -
muṉpu iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉpōtu talivikku uyiraṉi yāḷ eṉṟu tāṉ terintukoṇṭa pāṅkiyiṉ vāyil peṟṟut taliviyuṭaṉkūṭa muyalutal karutit talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam cellutal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘pāṅkiyiṭic cēṟal’ eṉṉum. (50)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ; pāṅkimatiyuṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉum kiḷavik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 140)
peṭṭa vāyil peṟutal -
talivaṉ taṉṉāl virumpappaṭṭa tūtām pāṅkaṉāl taliviyip peṟutal, taliviyāl virumpappaṭṭa tōḻiyit tūtākap peṟutal.	(to. po. 99 iḷa.)
talivaṉ talivikku vāyilātaṟkuriyāri ārāyntu  palaruḷḷum taliviyāl virumpappaṭṭāḷākiya tōḻi taṉakkut taliviyi aṭivataṟku vaḻiceyyak kūṭiyavaḷ eṉpataṉit talivi kuṟippiṉāl peṟutal. maṟintu taliviyik kaṇṭu niṉṟa talivaṉ talivikkut tōḻiyiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyik kaṇṭu, avaḷiyē taliviyit tāṉ aṭivataṟku vaḻi eṉṟu tuṇivāṉ.			(to.po. 102 nac.)
iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarnta piṉṉar, taṉṉi avaḷ kāṇātavāṟum tāṉ avaḷik kāṇumāṟum aminta aṇimikkaṇ talimakaṉ niṉṟāṉ; maṟṟiyōrellām talimakaṭkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭum, ivaḷ (-tōḻi) vicēṭat tāl ceyyum vaḻipāṭum, ellārkkum talimakaḷ ceyyum aruḷicceykiyum, ivaṭku vicēṭattāl ceyyum aruḷicceykiyum kaṇṭamiyāl, “talimakaṭkuc ciṟantavaḷ ivaḷē; ituvum eṉakkōr cārpu” eṉpataṉi uṇarntāṉ. uṇarntamiyāṉ ivaḷiṭattē celvāṉ.
	(iṟi. a. 5 uri)
peṇpāṟ kiḷavi -
kāmam mikunta niliyil peruntiṇit talivi kūṟuvatu.
“mika nuṇṇitiṉ ārāyntu ivvūravar vāṉattiluḷḷa matiyiṭattuk kāṇappaṭum muyaliyum naṉku aṟivar; āṉāl eṉṉip pirinta talivar irukkumiṭam māttiram aṟiyum āṟṟal peṟṟilarpōlum” eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.
iḵtu irupāṟ peruntiṇikkaṇṇatoru tuṟi.
	(pu. ve. mā. 17-9)
peṇpāṟkuriya iyalpu -
accamum nāṇum pētimiyum immūṉṟum nāḷtōṟum muntuṟutal peṇṭirukku iyalpām. ākavē, vēṭki mikkavaḻiyum accam nāṇam maṭam ivi kāraṇamākat talivi meyyuṟu puṇarccikku iciyātu varinteytu-taliyē perumpālum vēṇṭum eṉpatu.
	(to. po. 96 iḷa.)
accam - aṉpu kāraṇattāl tōṉṟum payam.
nāṇam - kāmakkuṟippu nikaḻntavaḻippaṭuvatōr uḷḷa oḻukkam.
maṭam - ceviliyar kaṟpittavaṟṟi uṇarntu, piṟar avi paṟṟik kūṟumvaḻit tāṉ muṉ aṟintati veḷiyiṭā tiruttal.
payirppu - muṉpu kāṇātaṉa kaṇṭavaḻi, maṉam koḷḷāta aruvaruppu.
pētimi - ceyyat takuvaṉavaṟṟi aṟiyātiruttal.
niṟi - maṉattip poṟivaḻiccellātu aṭakki vittal.
iruvarum kaṇṇuṟṟuk kātal kūrnta uḷḷap puṇarcciyum kantaruvamē ātaliṉ, tirumaṇattiṉ piṉ meyyuṟupuṇarcci peṟalām eṉṟu āṟṟumāṟu ceyvaṉavē ip peṇmik kuṇaṅkaḷām. 	(99 nac.)
avi accamum  nāṇum  maṭaṉumām. avaṟṟic cārntu niṟpaṉa piṟavum talimakaṭku uriya. aviyāvaṉa payirppu, pētimi, poṟi mutaliyaṉa.
accamāvatu aṉpu kāraṇamākat tōṉṟum uṭku. nāṇamāvatu peṇmikkup polivu tarum uḷappāṅku. maṭaṉāvatu cevili mutalāṉōr uṇarttuvaṉavaṟṟi ārāyātu mēṟkoḷḷalum aṅṅaṉam koṇṭavaṟṟi viṭātoḻukalum. payirppāvatu payilātavaṟṟiṉ mēṟ koḷḷum aruvaruppu; pētimiyāvatu piḷḷit taṉmi. poṟiyāvatu vaṟumi mutalāyavaṟṟip poṟuttoḻukutal.	(to. kaḷa. 8 ca. pāla.)
peṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷit tuṟikaḷ -
kāṇṭal, nayattal, uṭkōḷ, melital, melivoṭu vikal, kāṇṭal valittal, pakal muṉivurittal, iravu nīṭu paruvaral, kaṉaviṉ araṟṟal, neñcoṭu melital eṉṟa pattum ām.
talivaṉ taṉṉiṭam otta aṉpuṭiyavaṉā eṉpataṉi uṇarāmal, tāṉē avaṉiṭam aṉpu ceytu, avaṉ kūṭṭam peṟātu vāṭi niṟkum taliviyiṉ varuttam paṟṟiya ivi, orutalikkāmam paṟṟiya akappuṟak kikkiḷi āyiṉa.	(pu. ve. mā. 15)
peṇpāṟ kūṟṟup peruntiṇit tuṟikaḷ -
vēṭki muntuṟuttal, piṉṉili muyaṟal, piriviṭi āṟṟal, varavu etirntiruttal, vārāmikku aḻital, iravut talic cēṟal, illavi nakutal, pulaviyuḷ pulampal, poḻutu kaṇṭu iraṅkal, parattiyi ēcal, kaṇṭu kaṇ civattal, kātaliṉ kaḷittal, koṇṭu akampukutal, kūṭṭattuk kuḻital, ūṭaluḷ nekiḻtal, uri kēṭṭu nayattal, pāṭakac cīṟaṭi paṇinta piṉ iraṅkal, paḷḷimicit toṭartal, celka eṉa viṭuttal eṉṉum ivi pattoṉ patum ām. peṇpāṟkup poruntāta veḷippaṭṭa kāmattaviyātaliṉ ivi puṟattiṇi yāyiṉa.			(pu. ve. mā. 16)
`peynīr pōlum uṇarvu' -
putu maṭkalattuḷ ūṟṟappaṭṭa nīr veḷiyē kacintu tāṉ kalattiṉuḷ iruppatip pulappaṭuttum. atu pōlat talivi taṉ maṉattuppaṭṭa vēṭkiyit taṉ meyppāṭ ṭāl talivaṉ uṇarac ceyvāḷēyaṉṟi, avaṉ muṉṉarc coṟkaḷāl veḷippaṭuttāḷ. aṅṅaṉam coṟkoṇṭu veḷippaṭuttutal kīḻmakkaḷ iyalpām.
talivi talivaṉiṭam taṉvēṭkiyi veḷiyiṭāḷē oḻiyat taṉ uyirttōḻiyiṭam veḷiyiṭuvāḷ. tōḻi taliviyiṉ vēṭkiyit talivaṉukkuk kūṟutaṟku vāyppum urimiyum peṟṟavaḷ.	(to. po. 118 nac.)
peyarka eṉa urittal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṉiṭiyē taliviyiṉ cuṟṟattār piṉ toṭarntu varutalik kaṇṭa talivaṉ, avarkaḷoṭu porutu ūṟu viḷippatit tavirkka virumpit taliviyi viṭṭu oru kuṉṟiṉ maṟivil ceṉṟapōtu, avaḷit talippaṭṭa cuṟṟattār tammūrkkut tirumpi varuka eṉa vēṇṭutal.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 426)
peyarp peyar -
poruḷ paṟṟi varum peyar; poruppaṉ, cilampaṉ; aṇṇal, tōṉṟal; cērppaṉ; makiḻnaṉ - pōlvaṉa. 
	(to. po. 20 nac.)
uripporuṭkuriya talimakkaḷ peyar ivvāṟu peyarp peyarāka vantaṉa.
peruntiṇi (1) -
taṉṉi uḷḷaṉpoṭu virumpāta talivaṉoruvaṉit taḻuva virumpit talivi yorutti avaṉatiruppiṭam nōkki iruḷil cellutal eṉṉum pāṭāṇ tuṟi. itaṉkaṇ pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉē kiḷavit talivaṉ āvāṉ. “pāṇṭiyaṉit taḻuva, makkaḷ naṭamāṭṭamillāta iruḷil cellum talivikku vaḻikāṭṭa mēkam miṉṉi yatu” eṉṟu kūṟutal ittuṟic ceyti.	(pu. ve. mā. 9:46)
peruntiṇi (2) -
peruntiṇi - periyatākiya oḻukkam. naṭuvaṇ aintiṇi yākiya otta kāmattiṉ mikkum kuṟintum varutalāṉum, eṇvaki maṇattiṉuḷ piramam piracāpattiyam āriṭam teyvam eṉpaṉa attiṇippāṟ paṭutalāṉum, ivi peruntiṇi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. otta aṉpum kulaṉum mutalāyiṉa ottu varuvatu ulakiṉuḷ aritākaliṉ, ain tiṇi ulakil arukiyallatu vārātu.	(to. po. 1 iḷa.)
piramam mutaliya eṇvaki maṇattiṉuḷ, kikkiḷikkuriya maṇam mūṉṟu; aintiṇikkuriya maṇam oṉṟu; ēṉiya piramam piracāpattiyam āriṭam teyvam eṉṟa nāṉku maṇaṅkaḷiyum tāṉ oṉṟumē peṟutaliṉ, ēṉiyavaṟṟi ṉum periya tiṇiyāyp peruntiṇi eṉṉum peyar peṟṟatu 	(1 nac.)
talivaṉ maṭalēṟutal, iṉpam tuyttaṟkuriya paruvam kaḻinta piṉṉarum eḻum puṇarcci viruppam, teḷiya oṇṇāmal aṟivaḻikkum kaḻikāmam, kari kaṭanta kāmattāl virum pāri valitiṟ puṇarum vaṉkaṇmi ākiya puripaṭu kāma iḻioḻukka vakikaḷiyuṭiya ciṟiya tiṇikkup perun tiṇi eṉṟa peyar maṅkala vaḻakkāṉ vantatu. 	(50 pārati)
koṭiya kuṭṭa nōyāṉatu ‘perunōy’ eṉavum, koṭiya veṭikaṭṭi nōy ‘peruvāri nōy’ eṉavum vaḻaṅkutal pōla, koṭiya muraṭṭut taṉamāṉa maṭalēṟutal mutaliya takā oḻukkattiyuṭiya tiṇi ‘peruntiṇi’ eṉṉap paṭṭatu.	(1 kuḻa.)
peruntiṇiyāvatu perukiyatākiya oḻukkam. aḵtāvatu aḷaviṟanta vēṭki. aḷaviṉ kaṭanta nili perun tiṇiyām.	(to. akat. 1 ca. pāla.)
peruntiṇi eṉpatu poruntāk kāmam. 			(na. a.5; i. vi. 377; vī. cō. 97).
peruntiṇi eṉpatu poruntāk kāmam pōl tōṉṟip piṉṉarp poruntuvatu eṉṟalē ēṟpuṭittu. iḵtu akappuṟattiṇi iraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu; ēṉiyatu kikkiḷi.
peruntiṇik kuṟippu -
maṭalēṟutal, iḷami tīrnta piṉṉum kāma nukarcciyil mikkiruttal, kāmattu mikutiṟattāṉ aṟivu aḻintiruttal, mikka kāmattāl valiyap paṟṟik kūṭutal eṉṟa nāṉkum perun tiṇik karuttil amivaṉavām.	(to.po. 51 nac.)
perunayappu urittal -
taṉ kātal mikutiyit talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“ulakil vēṟu eṅkum yāriṭamum illāta eḻilum piṟa nalaṉum ivaḷiṭam tuyttu iṉpuṟṟēṉ.” eṉṉum talivaṉatu pukaḻcciyuri.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘vaṉpuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 129)
perumpāṉmi kūṟi maṟuttal -
kaḷavukkālattil taṉṉi varivu kaṭāviya tōḻikkut talivaṉ, “yāṉ taliviyit teyvamāṉuṭam eṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ. ivaḷ taṅkukiṟa iṭam teyvamakaḷir irukku miṭamō, aṉṟik kuṟattiyariṭamō? kūṟuvāyāka” eṉat taliviyip perumippaṭuttik kūṟuvāṉ pōla viri vil avaḷi maṇattaṟku maṟuttatu.
itu ‘varivu muṭukkam’ eṉam kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru tuṟi.
	(kōvi. 251)
perumpoḻutiṉ vaki -
kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi, iḷavēṉil, mutirvēṉil, eṉṟa āṟum ām. ivi ōrāṇṭiṉ ivviraṇṭu tiṅkaḷākap pakukkappaṭṭu āvaṇi mutal kaṇakkiṭappeṟum. kār - āvaṇi, puraṭṭāci; kūtir - aippaci, kārttiki; muṉpaṉi - mārkaḻi, ti; piṉpaṉi - māci, paṅkuṉi; iḷa vēṉil - cittiri, vikāci; mutirvēṉil - āṉi, āṭi; eṉṟivvāṟu kālavariyaṟi koḷḷappaṭum.			(na. a. 11; i. vi. 12)
`perumaṇal ulakam' -
periya maṇal mēṭukaḷ alikaḷāl uṇṭākkappaṭṭa kaṭaṟkarip pakutiyākiya neytal nilam. ulakam eṉṉum col maṇṇulakam muḻutumē yaṉṟit taṉkaṇ kūṟupaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷiyum uṇarttum eṉpatu.
	(to. po. 5 nac., cīvaka. 2606 nac.)
perumi ceytal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṟutikkaṇ, talivaṉ talivikku avaḷ tōḻi aṇivittāṟpōlavē kulinta āṭi aṇikalaṉkaḷi tiruttiyamittal (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) iḵtu ‘ēṟpuṟa aṇital’ eṉavumpaṭum (na. a. 125)	
	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
perumip potupporuḷ -
tāḷi, pūval, nāval, kaḻañcu, vēli, māṟu, pataṭi, kōri, cūri, kāri, tuvari, atti, payaṟu uḻuntu kaṭali mocci koḷḷu eṉṟa aivakip paruppukkaḷ, curumpu, vaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa tiṇitōṟum kāṇappaṭum perumip potupporuḷkaḷām.	(vī. cō. 96 uri mēṟ.)
perumiyum uraṉum -
perumiyum uraṉum talivaṟkuriya paṇpukaḷ. ivaṟṟāṉ uḷḷappuṇarcciyē nikaḻntu varintukoḷḷum ulaka vaḻakkum, meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻntuḻiyum kaḷavu nīṭṭi yātu varintukōṭalum, uḷḷam ceṉṟa vaḻiyellām nekiḻntu ōṭātu ārāyntu ceyaṟpaṭutalum, uḷḷam melintavaḻi ammelivu tōṉṟāmal maṟittalum, tavaṟāṉa vaḻiyiṟ cella viḻiyum uḷḷatti mīṭṭalum talivaṟku uriya eṉpatu.
perumiyāvatu aṟivum, āṟṟalum, pukaḻum, koṭiyum, ārāytalum, paṇpum, naṇpum, paḻipāvam añcutalum mutaliyaṉavāy mēṟpaṭum perumippakuti.
uraṉāvatu uṟutiyākak kikkoṇṭa koḷkiyum, maṉattil irakaciyatti maṟittu vittirukkum āṟṟalum, ecceyaliyum kalaṅkātu tuṇitalum mutaliya valimip pakuti.	(to. po. 98 nac.)
‘perumiyum uraṉum āṭūu mēṉa’ ātaliṉ, talivaṉ uḷḷappuṇarcciyāṉ varital vēṇṭip pāṅkaṟku urittatu-
perumiyum uraṉum uṭiya talivaṉ, kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil pākaṉ taṉ tēri ūrntu varac ceṉṟapōtu neytaṟpūkkaḷi iṭiyiṭiyē toṭutta taḻi yāṭiyi aṇintu melliya aḻakiya tēmaliyum viḻunteḻunta kūriya paṟkaḷiyum nīṇṭa kūriya kūntaliyum oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiyum uṭiya kuṟumakaḷ, taṉ tōḻiyaroṭu veṇmaṇalil utirnta puṉṉittā tiṉip poṉṉākac cēkarittuk koṇṭu illaṟam naṭattutali viḷiyāṭṭāka viḷiyāṭikkoṇṭirunta taṉi nōkkit taṉ tēriṉi mellac celutti avaḷiṭam vantu, “ivviḷiyāṭṭu illaṟattiṉi eṉṉi maṇantu uṇmiyākavē naṭattuvatil tavaṟoṉṟum illiyē!” eṉṟa kūṟa, avaḷ ataṉi maṉamāra ēṟṟa makiḻcciyāl vanta uvakikkaṇṇīri maṟittut taliyic ciṟitu kaviḻttuk koṇṭa niliyāṉ avaḷatu kuṟippaṟinta tāṉ avaḷi virivil maṇakkāmaliruttal taṉakkup periya varuttattit tarum eṉṟa karuttit taṉ pāṅkaṉiṭam kūṟutal.(akanā. 230)
iḵtu uḷḷap puṇarcci oṉṟumē koṇṭu meyyuṟu puṇarcciyākiya kaḷavuppakuti illāmalēyē varivu nikaḻtalum uṇṭu eṉpataṉit terivikkiṟatu.
	(to. po. 95 iḷa.)
`peṟṟōr iruṅkiḷi maruṅkiṉ tūtu viṭuttal' -
talivaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟa talivi talivaṉ ūril taṅkip putalvarip peṟumvari aṅkēyē peṟṟōr toṭar pillāmaliruntu piṟaku tam nili paṟṟip peṟṟōrkkuc ceyti aṉupputal. (pāli naṭiyiyal)
	(vī. cō. 93 uri mēṟ.)
peṟaṟkaritu eṉṟal -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ talivinalaṉ nukarnta talivaṉ, “ivaḷuṭiya toṭarpu kiṭṭutaṟku aritu” eṉpataṉi maṉaṅkoṇṭu avaḷip peritum pārāṭṭutal. (‘arumi aṟital’ kō. 17 eṉpatum atu) (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal.)
	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ)
pēynili (1) -
itu maṉṟal eṭṭaṉuḷ iṟutiyāyatu; iyalpālum kaṭiyāyatu. taṉṉiviṭa yāṇṭu mūttavaḷiyum, kaḷ ḷuṇṭu mayaṅkiyavaḷiyum uṟakkattil āḻntavaḷiyum puṇarvatu; taṉṉiṉum mika iḻintōḷi maṇappatum itaṉpāṟpaṭum.	(to. po. 92 nac.)
ituvum poruntāk kāmattiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
	(ta. ne. vi. 14 uri)
 pērviṉātal -
poḻiliṭattiṟku vanta talivaṉ, pāṅkiyiṭam avarkaḷatu peyar yātu eṉṟu viṉavutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅki mati uṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉum kiḷavik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 140)
`pērāc ciṟappiṉ irunāṉku kiḷavi' -
kuṟiyāta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya eṭṭuvakippaṭum talivaṉ kūṟṟu. aviyāvaṉa - meytoṭṭup payiṟal, poy pārāṭṭal, iṭam peṟṟut taḻāal, iṭiyūṟu kiḷattal, nīṭu niṉintu iraṅkal, kūṭutaluṟutal, colliya nukarcci vallē peṟutal, tīrāt tēṟṟam eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mutalaṉa āṟum iyaṟkip puṇarccikkē uriyaṉa. ēṉiya iraṇṭum iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu eṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum uriyaṉa.	(to. po. 99 iḷa.)
iṭantalippāṭu iruvakittu; talivaṉum taliviyum tāmē kūṭum iṭantalippāṭum, pāṅkaṉ ceṉṟu aṟintu vantu kūṟap piṉ talivaṉ kuṟiyiṭatti aṭiyum iṭan talippāṭum eṉa. iruvakippaṭṭa iṭantalippāṭum, otta ciṟappiṉa eṉpataṟkup ‘pērāc ciṟappiṉ’ eṉṟār ivveṭṭum iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇ nikaḻvaṉa.(102 nac.)
`pērāc cīṟṟamoṭu perumuṉivu pēcal' -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vāyil vēṇṭi illam pukunta taliva ṉiṭam nīṅkāta kōpattoṭu avaṉtoṭarpit talivi veṟuttup pēcutal. (maruta naṭiyiyal).
	(vī. cō. 95 uri mēṟ.)
picācam -
eṇvaki maṇattuḷ oṉṟu, mūttōr tuyiṉṟōrp puṇarcciyum, iḻintōḷi maṇam ceytalum, āṭi māṟutalum, piṟavumām.	(to. po. 92 nac.)
picācamāvatu tuñciṉārōṭum mayaṅkiṉārōṭum kaḷittārōṭum cettārōṭum vilaṅkiṉōṭum iḻitaku marapiṉ yārum illā oruciṟikkaṇ puṇarntoḻukum oḻukkam.	(yā. vi. pak. 575)
potuppaṭak kūṟi vāṭi aḻuṅkal -
talivaṉatu parattimiyip poṟutta taṉṉit tōḻi pārāṭṭiyavaḻit talivi, “tamatu nalattit talivaṉ nukarak koṭuttu iḻantu piṉ talivaṉ piriyat tam taliyaṇiyiyē tuṇiyākak koṇṭu kiṭantu, eṉṉip pōl uyir tēyvār iṉi yār uḷarō?” eṉṟu potuvākap parattiyarkku iraṅki urippāḷ pōlat talivaṉuṭiya puṟattoḻukkamākiya koṭu miyi niṉittu vāṭutal.
itaṉi uṇartta uṇarum ūṭaluḷ, ‘taṉittuḻi iṟivi tuṉittaḻutu iraṅkal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. (na. a. 205) itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
	(kōvi. 354)
poyc cūḷuri -
itu nampiyakap poruḷ kūṟum akapporuṭ perun tiṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟu; poyyāka uṟutimoḻi kūṟutal.
talivaṉparattimi ellārāṉum aṟiyappaṭṭatāka, parattiyillattiliruntukoṇṭu avaḷit tōynta cuvaṭu kūṭa māṟāmal taliviillattiṟku vanta talivaṉ, talivi avaṉuṭiya parattimiyi eṭutturittu avaṉōṭu ūṭiyapōtu, taṉ tavaṟṟukku varunti avaḷi amitiyuṟac ceyya āvaṉa puritali viṭuttu, tāṉ paratti yoruttiyiyum aṟiyātavaṉākap poyyāka uṟuti moḻi kūṟi, piṉ tavaṟṟip pallāṟṟāṉum cāṉṟukaḷōṭu avaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavaḻit taṉ uri poyyātali aṟintu paṇintamoḻi kūṟum nilikkuc celuttum ‘poyccūḷuri’ poruntāk kāmap pakutippaṭṭatāy, akapporuṭ peruntiṇit tuṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka orucār āciriyarāl koḷḷappaṭṭatu.	(na. a. 243)
poypārāṭṭal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉpōtu talivaṉ  taliviyi aḷavu kaṭappap puṉintu pukaḻtal. “matiyum malarkaḷum niṉ mukattiṟkum uṟuppukkaḷukkum oppākamāṭṭāmal nāṇu kiṉṟaṉa” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu ‘taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkippuṇarcci’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 127)
poymoḻintaṉaḷeṉa āṟṟuvittatu -
cevili veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttuvatākak kūṟuvatu poy eṉṟu kūṟit tōḻi taliviyi amitiyuṟac ceytal.
kaḷavoḻukkattil talivaṉōṭu iṉpam tuyttu varum taṉatu uṭal vēṟupāṭṭik kaṇṭu kalaṅkiya cevili kaṭṭuvicciyi viṉavi avaḷ kūṟiyavāṟu veṟiyāṭṭu eṭukka niṉittati aṟinta talivi kalaṅkavē, tōḻi avaḷi nōkki, “tāy niccayamāka veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻtta māṭṭāḷ; veṟiyāṭap pōvatākac coṉṉāl, atu kuṟittu uṉ neñcam evvāṟuḷḷatu eṉpatik kāṇavē aṅṅaṉam poy kūṟiyuḷḷāḷ” eṉṟu kūṟit taliviyi amitiyuṟac ceytal.
itu kaṟpiṉic cērnta ‘aṟattoṭu nili’ eṉṟa kiḷavik kaṇṇatoru tuṟi. ‘poyyeṉa moḻital’ eṉpatum itu.	(ta. ne. vi.pak. 38)
`poyyum vaḻuvum............ eṉpa' poruḷ -
meyyuṟu puṇarcciyāṉātal uḷḷap puṇarcciyāṉātal kaḷaviṉkaṇ oṉṟupaṭṭuk karaṇamiṉṟik kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum maṉiyaṟatti mēṟkoḷḷumiṭattu, kayamiyā ṉātal, piṟa kāraṇattāṉātal āṭavaṉ uḷam tirintu taliviyik kiviṭṭavaḻi, ‘itu takavu aṉṟu’ eṉap piṟar iṭitturippar. tām maṇantu koṇṭamikkuc cāṉṟum cāṉṟāḷarum peṟāmiyāl yāṉ avaḷi aṟiyēṉ” eṉṟu avaṉ maṟittal kūṭum. taṉpāl tōṉṟiya makkaṭku urimi nalkāmal vilakki oḻukumiṭattu, avaṉi maṉṟat tār aḻittu ucāvuvar. avaḷ kāmakkiḻattiyē aṉṟi maṉikkiḻatti allaḷ” eṉṟu avaṉ vaḻuvutalum kūṭum. ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷ orōviṭattut tōṉṟiya piṉṉark kāta luṇarvum kaṭamippāṭum paḻikku añcutalum taliva ṉiṭam nilipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉa eṇṇi, aṟaneṟi tērnta taliyāya cāṉṟōr karaṇattic cīrpeṟa amittut tantaṉar eṉṟu nūlōr kūṟuvar.	(to.kaṟ. 4 (ca. pāla)
poyyuri purital -
pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ tōḻi talivaṉiṭam avaṉāl virumpappaṭṭa talivi yāvaḷ eṉpatu taṉakkut teriyātu eṉap poyyākak kūṟutal. itu ‘pāṅki aṟiyāḷ pōṉṟu viṉātal’ (na.a. 144) eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal.)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
porutta maṟinturittal -
kaṟpukkālattil talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ piriya niṉiyavē, pāli nilattāṉākiya talivaṉi marutanilat talivaṉip pōlak koṇṭu pulantu kūṟiya taliviyiṭam tōḻi, “nām evvaḷavu kūṟiyum talivaṉ niṉivu poruḷmēltāṉ mikutiyāka irukkiṟatu. iṉi, nām pēcuvataṉāl payaṉ eṉ?” eṉṟu talivaṉuṭiya uḷḷap pāṅkiṉi aṟintu tāṉ ataṟku nontu kūṟutal.
itu ‘poruḷ vayiṉ pirivu’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru tuṟi.	(kōvi. 337)
poruntā eṉṟal -
pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭattiṉkaṇ tōḻi talivaṉiṭam mikka iḷamip paruvattāḷākiya taliviyiṭam kāmap poruḷ paṟṟiya ceytiyik kūṟutal poruntāta ceyalākum eṉṟal (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ)
itu ‘pāṅki pētimi ūṭṭal’ (na. a. 144) eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
poruntāk kāmam -
talivaṉtaliviyariṭiyē muṉṉarp poruntātatu pōṉṟiruntu piṉṉarp poruntuvatākiya kāmam. itu peruntiṇi yeṉṉum akappuṟattiṇic ceytiyākum.
`poruḷ kaṭikkūṭṭiya neñciṟku(t talivaṉ) nikaḻntatu kūṟi niliyal' -
poruḷ tēṭak karutiya maṉattiṟkut talivaṉ paḻiya nikaḻcciyik kūṟip pirivit tavirttal -
“muṉpu poruḷ kaṭikkūṭṭap pālinilavaḻiyē ceṉṟa pōtu, paruntukaḷ taṅkum periya vēmpiṉ niḻalilē kallāc ciṟuvarkaḷ araṅku iḻittu nellikkāykaḷik koṇṭu vaṭṭāṭum, āṟalikaḷvarkaḷ niṟinta cīṟūri aṭuttu, maṉam taḷarvuṟṟa mālikkālattil, ‘niṉitta ceyali muṭittāl evvaḷavu makiḻcci ēṟpaṭumō, avvaḷavu makiḻcciyi eṉakkut taṉ vaṭivaḻakiṉālum ceyalkaḷālum nalka valla eṉ talivi, illattil viḷakkēṟṟivittu eṉ niṉippil maṉam uruki niṟkum nēramitu’ eṉṟu yāṉ niṉittuppaṭṭa tuṉpam kaṇak killatu ātaliṉ, avaḷip pirintu ceyyum ceyal etaṉ kaṇṇum īṭupaṭamāṭṭēṉ” (naṟ. 3) eṉa muṉ nikaḻcci kūṟit talivaṉ pōkātoḻital.  ituvum pālit tiṇi.)			(naṟ. 3)
poruḷ kōḷ -
itu maṉṟal eṭṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu; āriṭa maṇam eṉavum paṭum.
āvum āṉēṟum poṟkōṭṭup poṟkuḷampiṉavākac ceytu avaṟṟiṭip peṇṇiṉ tanti taṉ peṇṇit takkāṉoruvaṉoṭu niṟutti, “nīrum ivipōlap polintu vāḻka!” eṉat taṉ peṇṇukkup poṉ ṉaṇintu avaḷi avaṉukkuk koṭuppatu.			(to. po. 92 nac.)
ivvāṟu ītaṟkaṇ otta pirāyam mutalāyiṉa uḷa eṉiṉ, kaṟpiṉpāṟpaṭum; alla eṉiṉ poruntāk kāmattiṉpāṟ paṭum.	(ta. ne. vi. 14 uri)
poruḷkōḷ ēḻu -
iḵtu akapporuḷ uri irupattēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu.
koḻuṅkoṭi vaḷḷi, ceḻuṅkuli vāḻi, tīṅkarumpu, malar tikaḻtaru palā, tēṅkamaḻ acōku, ceruviṟpūṭṭu, puṉalyāṟu eṉap pāṭaṟkup poruḷ koḷḷumuṟi ēḻu vakippaṭum.
vaḷḷi - pāṭṭiṉ mutalilēyē poruḷ ciṟattal.
vāḻi - pāṭṭiṉ īṟṟil poruḷ ciṟattal.
karumpu - pāṭṭiṉkaṇ mutaliliruntu iṟuti nōkkip paṭippaṭiyē poruḷ ciṟattal.
palā - pāṭṭiṉ mutalum iṭiyum poruḷ ciṟattal.
acōku - pāṭṭiṉ naṭuvē poruḷ ciṟattal.
viṟpūṭṭu - pāṭṭiṉ iṟuticcīr mutaṟcīrkaḷil poruḷ ciṟattal.
puṉalyāṟu - pāṭṭiṉ evviṭattum poruḷ ciṟattal.
ivveḻuvakip poruḷkōḷuḷ pāṭṭiṟku ēṟṟa toṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.	(vī. cō. 96 uri)
poruḷ naṭi -
akapporuḷ uri irupattēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu; aḷimaṟi, moḻi māṟṟu, koṇṭu kūṭṭu, muṟi niral niṟi, etir niral niṟi, cuṇṇam mutalākac coṟṟoṭarkaḷip poruḷ poruttamuṟa iṇittaṟkup payaṉpaṭuttum poruḷkōḷ vaki.	(vī. cō. 96 uri)
poruḷ muṭiyāniṉṟa (-viṉiyi muṭittuk koṇṭirukkum) talimakaṉ āṟṟāṉākic collutal -
“aṟattāl poruḷ tēṭip poruḷāl iṉpam tuyppōm” eṉṟu ‘mūṉṟaṉpakuti’ (41) kūṟip pirinta talivaṉ, poruḷ tēṭumpōtu iṭiyiṭiyē taliviniṉivu mika, “eṉ neñcam,  eṉ taliviyiṉ pirivut tuṉpattip pōkka avaḷiṭam celluvōm eṉkiṉṟatu. eṉ aṟivō, eṭutta viṉiyi muṟṟa muṭikkātu mīṇṭu pōtal. aṟiyāmiyi yum ikaḻcciyiyum tarum ātaliṉ ūrukku mīḷvataṟku viriyātē eṉkiṉṟatu. eṉ neñcamum aṟivum tammuḷ pakikoṇṭataṉāl, iraṇṭu valiya kaḷiṟukaḷāl irupuṟa mum iḻukkappaṭum tēypurip paḻaṅkayiṟu iṟṟoḻivatu pōla, ivviraṇṭaṟkum iṭippaṭṭa eṉ uṭampu aḻiya vēṇṭuvatutāṉ pōlum!” eṉṟu varuntik kūṟiyatu.			(to. po. 45 nac.) (naṟ. 284)
poruḷ muṟṟi vanta talivaṉiyuṭiya kiḻatti kāmamikka kaḻipaṭar kiḷaviyāl kūṟiyatu -
“ceṅkāntaḷpūppōṉṟa civanta koṇṭiyiyuṭiya kōḻic cēvalē! poruḷ muṟṟi mīṇṭu vanta taliva ṉōṭu yāṉ iṉpamāka uṟaṅkum uṟakkattiṉiṉṟu eḻumāṟu eṉṉik kūvi eḻuppiya nī, maṉiyelikaḷip piṭittut tiṉṉum kāṭṭuppūṉikku iriyāki mikka tuṉpamuṟuka!” eṉṟu talivi kāmamikkataṉāl uṇṭāṉa ariya niṉippiṉāl, kēṭṭalum viṭikūṟalum illāta kōḻiccēvali nōkkik kūṟiyatu.	(kuṟun. 107)
poruḷvayiṟ pirivu -
poruḷ tēṭaṟ kāraṇam paṟṟit taliviyit talivaṉ piriki. 	(kōvi. 332 ava.)
poruḷvayiṉ piritalum poruṭpiṇip piritalum -
poruḷvayiṉ pirital kaṟpukkālattut talivaṉukku nikaḻum pirivukaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itaṉ kālavariyaṟi ōrāṇ ṭukku uṭpaṭṭatu. 	(to. po. 29, 190 nac.)
aṟaneṟi tavaṟāmal poruḷīṭṭutaṟkup pirital aracar antaṇar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar eṉṟa nālvarukkum uriyatu. aracuiṟi vāṅkap pirital maṉṉarkkum, maṉṉar āṇi peṟṟa maṇṭilamākkaḷ taṇṭattalivar vēḷirkaḷ kuṟunila maṉṉar ākiyōrkkum uriyatu.	(29,30,32, 33 nac.)
poruṭpiṇip pirivu eṉpatu talivaṉ illaṟam naṭattu taṟkup poruḷillāmi paṟṟi atu tēṭap piriyum eṉpa taṉṟu; taṉ mutukuravarāl paṭikkappaṭṭa palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa poruḷellām kiṭantatumaṉ! ataṉit tuyttu vāḻvatu āṇmittaṉmi aṉṟu eṉat taṉatu tāḷāṟṟalāl paṭitta poruḷkoṇṭu vaḻaṅki vāḻvataṟkup piriyum eṉpatu. aṉṟiyum tāyapporuḷāl ceyyum tāṉaṅkaḷit tēvarkaḷum pitirarkaḷum iṉpuṟār ātaliṉ, tēvarkāriyamum pitirarkāriyamum taṉatu tāḷāṟṟalāl (-muyaṟciyāl) ceyta poruḷāl ceyyap paṭṭāṟṟāṉ taṉakkup payaṉpaṭum eṉpatu karutiyum talivaṉ poruḷtēṭap pirivāṉ. ippirivu tiṅkaḷum irutuvum elliyāka uṭiyatu.	(iṟi. a. 34,41 uri)
poruḷvayiṉ pirintōṉ virivil mīṇṭu peṟṟa poruḷ koṇṭu, “niṉ nalaṉ nayantu vantēṉ” eṉṟu taliviyiṭam kūṟal -
yāṉikaḷ tiritarum pala piḷavupaṭṭa vaḻikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭukaḷik kaṭantu poruḷtēṭac ceṉṟa yāṉ, ciṟitu poruḷ īṭṭiya aḷavil, niṉ aḻaki niṉittu, mīṇṭum poruḷ tēṭum vēṭkiyiṉṟi, eṉṉuṭaṉ vantavarkaḷiṭam kūṭa yāṉ mīṇṭu pōkum ceytiyik kuṟippiṭāmal niṉṉiṭattu vantu viṭṭēṉ” eṉṟu talivimāṭṭut tāṉ poruḷiṉum kātal mikavuṭiya ceytiyit talivaṉ pulappaṭuttutal.	(to. po. 45 nac.)  (aiṅ. 355)
poruḷvayiṉ pirivut tuṟikaḷ -
1. vāṭṭaṅ kūṟal, 2. pirivu niṉivu urittal, 3. āṟṟātu pulampal, 4. āṟṟāmi kūṟal, 5. tiṇipeyart turittal, 6. poruttam aṟinturittal, 7. pirintami kūṟal, 8. iravuṟu tuyarattiṟku iraṅki urittal, 9. ikaḻcci niṉintu aḻital, 10. uruvu veḷippaṭṭu niṟṟal, 11. neñcoṭu nōtal, 12. neñcoṭu pulattal, 13. neñcoṭu maṟuttal, 14. nāṇ eṇṇi varuntal, 15. vaṟumi kaṇṭu iraṅki urittal, 16. paruvam kaṇṭiraṅkal, 17. mukiloṭu kūṟal, 18. tēr varavu kūṟal, 19. iḷiyar etirkōṭal, 20. uḷ makiḻnturittal - eṉpaṉa irupatumām.	(kōvi. 332- 351)
poruḷatikāram -
akam, puṟam mutaliyavaṟṟik kūṟum tolkāppiyam, ilakkaṇa viḷakkam mutaliya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiṉ mūṉṟām pakuti. collatikārattiyaṭuttu nikaḻvatu itu.
poruḷatikārap puṟaṉaṭi -
palavākiya mukattāṉ virinta poruḷilakkaṇaṅkaḷi yellām col karuviyāl avi muṟṟac collat toṭaṅkiṉ muṭivu kūṭā; āyiṉum, kūṟappaṭṭa poru ḷilakkaṇattōṭum avaṟṟiṟku iyipupaṭa niṉṟa ēṉi ilakkaṇattōṭum amittuk kēṭpōr uḷattu uṇmiyuṟak kūṟavallōr, pūmiyil pakalavaṉ pōl viḷaṅki, tammip piriyā urimi peṟṟa kalimaṭanti yoṭu tirumaṭanti avvāṟu tammiṭam vatiya, mali makaḷi oru kūṟṟiṟ koṇṭu ulakiṟku iṉpam payakkum iṟivaṉip pōla neṭuṅkālam iṉitu vāḻvar.			(i.vi.po. 569)
`poruḷatikāram' eṉṟa peyarkkāraṇam -
iyaṟcol mutalāya nālvakic coṟkaḷāl ākiya toṭarkaḷāl makkaḷ oḻukalāṟu ākiya poruḷi nallicip pulavōr nāṭakavaḻakkum ulakavaḻakkum paṟṟic ceyyuḷ ceyvar. acceyyuṭku uriyaṉavākat toṉṉū lōr ōtiya 34 uṟuppukkaḷuḷ māttiri, eḻuttu, aci, cīr, aṭi, toṭi mutaliyavi vaṭivamippu uṟuppukkaḷ; tiṇi kikōḷ, kūṟṟu, kēṭpōr, meyppāṭu mutaliyavi poruḷamippu uṟuppukkaḷ. ātaliṉ avi paṟṟiya ilakkaṇa marapukaḷik kūṟum ivvatikāram poruḷatikāram eṉappaṭṭatu.(to. po. pāyiram ca. pāla.)
poruḷāvaṉa -
poruḷāvaṉa aṟamporuḷ iṉpamum, avaṟṟatu nili yiṉmiyum, avaṟṟiṉ nīṅkiya vīṭupēṟumām. poruḷ eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟavē, avaṟṟiṉ  pakutiyākiya mutal karu uriyum, kāṭcipporuḷum karuttupporuḷum, avaṟṟiṉ pakutiyākiya aimperumpūtamum, avaṟṟiṉ pakutiyākiya iyaṅkutiṇi nilattiṇiyum piṟavum ām.(to.po. 1 nac.)
poruḷāvaṉa uṟutipporuḷākiya aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉṟa nāṉkaṉuḷ, vīṭu coṟkaḷiṉ viḷak kattiṟku appāṟpaṭṭatātaliṉ, aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉṟa mūṉṟē. avaṟṟuḷ, iṉpatti akam eṉavum, ēṉiya aṟam poruḷ iraṇṭaṉiyum puṟam eṉavum peyariṭṭup poruḷi iraṇṭākap pakuttuk kōṭal tamiḻ marapu.			(to. po. 418; i. vi. 374)
poruḷeṉa moḻital -
poruḷvayiṉ pirital vēṇṭum eṉṟu talimakaḷ kūṟutal.
	(to. po. 211 iḷa.)
tōḻi, talivaṉiṭam, “emar taliviyi niṉakkut tirumaṇam ceytu koṭukka iciyāmikkuk kāraṇam, nī paricamākak koṭukkakkūṭiya poruḷiviṭa mikutiyāṉa poruḷi etirpārppataṉāṟṟāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟutal. kāval mikutiyāṉ talivikku varuttam nikaḻntaviṭattu virivil avaḷ tirumaṇam muṭiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutum tōḻi, tam uṟaviṉariṭam mikunta celvattip paricap poruḷākak koṭuttāl avarkaḷ virivil maṇam muṭittuviṭuvar eṉṟu karutiyum, perumporuḷ koṇartal talivaṉukku ariya ceyal aṉṟu eṉṟu karutiyum, aṉpu aṟam iṉpam nāṇ ivaṟṟi viṭuttut talivaṉ perumporuḷ koṇarumāṟu kūṟutalum akapporuḷukku ēṟṟa ceyalē yām.	(214, 215, nac.)
`poruḷeṉa moḻital' -
tōḻi viṉavāvaḻiyum talivi taṉatu kaḷavoḻukkattit tāyarkkup pulappaṭuttumāṟu kūṟutal. ivvāṟu moḻital pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṉkaṇ nīkkum nilimittu aṉṟu. ataṟku ētu, puṟattē cellaṟku iyalātavāṟu iṟceṟikkappaṭṭuc cevili mutaliyōrāl kāval ceyyappaṭum nili talivikku orēvaḻi nikaḻtal.	(to. poru. 19 ca.pāla.)
poruḷi eṭṭiṟanta palavakiyāṉ ārāytal -
poruḷit tiṇi pāl ceyyuḷ nilaṉ kālam vaḻu vaḻakku iṭam eṉṟa eṭṭu vakiyāṉ ārāytalōṭu, ataṉkaṇ varum tiṇitoṟum marīiya peyar, tiṇi nilip peyar, iruvakik kikōḷ, paṉṉiruvakik kūṟṟu, pattu vakik kēṭpōr, eṭṭu vaki meyppāṭu, nālvaki uvamam, aivaki marapu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiyum ārāytal.(to. po. 1 nac.)
poruḷi eṭṭuvakiyāṉ ārāytal -
eṭṭuvaki yāvaṉa tiṇi, pāl, ceyyuḷ, nilaṉ, kālam, vaḻu, vaḻakku iṭam eṉpaṉa.
tiṇi - akattiṇi, puṟattiṇi eṉṟa iraṇṭu.
pāl - kikkiḷi, mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal, pāli
peruntiṇi eṉṟa ēḻum, veṭci vañci. uḻiñi tumpi vāki kāñci pāṭāṇ eṉṟa ēḻum ākap patiṉāṉku.
ceyyuḷ - āciriyam, veṇpā, kali, vañci, paripāṭal, maruṭpā eṉṟa āṟu.
nilaṉ - mulli, kuṟiñci, marutam, neytal eṉṟa nāṉku.
kālam - kār kūtir muṉpaṉi piṉpaṉi iḷavēṉil mutuvēṉil eṉṟa perumpoḻutu āṟum, māli yāmam vikaṟi viṭiyal naṇpakal eṟpāṭu eṉṟa ciṟupoḻutu āṟum ākap paṉṉiraṇṭu.
vaḻu - akattiṇi vaḻu ēḻum, puṟattiṇi vaḻu ēḻum ākap patiṉāṉku
vaḻakku - nāṭaka vaḻakkum ulakiyal vaḻakkum eṉṟa iraṇṭu.
iṭam - vaḻakkiṭam ceyyuḷiṭam eṉṟa iraṇṭu.
	(to. po. 1 nac.; cilap. patikam. aṭiyārk.)
polivaḻivu urittu varivu kaṭātal -
kaḷavukkālattil iṟceṟippiṉāṉ varuntiya talivi taṉṉi etirppaṭa iyalāmikku varuntiyatic ciṟip puṟamākak kēṭṭa talivaṉ kuṟiyiṭi vantu niṟpat tōḻi etirppaṭṭu, “taṉ tamiyaṉmāratu kāvalik kaṭantu niṉvayattaḷāy niṉṟu polivaḻintu varuntum it taliviyi nī virivil varintu koḷḷātu ivvāṟu ikaḻntu karututaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa?” eṉṟu taliviyatu polivaḻivu kūṟi avaṉi varivu kaṭāyatu.
itu ‘varivu muṭukkam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 265)
poḻilkaṇṭu makiḻtal -
pāṅkaṉ taliviyik kaṇṭu vantu taṉakkuk kūṟiya piṉ taliviyik kāṇac ceṉṟa talivaṉ, taliviyik kaṇṇuṟṟa cōliyic ceṉṟaṭintu, appoḻiliṭi avaḷ uṟuppup pōṉṟa malarkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇṭu appoḻil taṉ cintaṉikkaṇ avaḷākavē kāṭci vaḻaṅkuvatākak kūṟi makiḻtal.
itaṉi ‘avaḷ etirppaṭṭa iṭatti avaḷākak kūṟutal’ eṉpa.	(i. vi. 503 uri)
itu ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.		(kōvi. 38)
poḻiliṭic cēṟal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṟutikkaṇ taliviyiṉatu - arumiyi aṟinta talivaṉ, mutalnāḷ kūṭṭi vitta teyvam maṟu nāḷum kūṭṭivikkum eṉṟa nampikkiyōṭu, iraṇṭām nāḷum taliviyirunta poḻiliṭattē cellutal.
itaṉit ‘tanta teyvam tarumeṉac cēṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. 
	(na. a. 135)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇṇatu orē kūṟṟu.
(pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṟkuriya ēṉiya tuṟikaḷ yāvum iṭantalippāṭṭiṟkum uriya. iṭantalippāṭu nikaḻiṉ pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻātu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻiṉ iṭantalippāṭu nikaḻātu eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.)
	(kōvi. 49)
poḻutu kaṇṭu iraṅkal -
peruntiṇit talivi mālippoḻutu kaṇṭu tuyaruṟutal.
“talivaṉō varavilli. eṉkaṇkaḷō nīr vārntu kalaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. uṭalum vāṭukiṟatu. mālippoḻutum vantu eṉ neñciṉ niṟiyi aḻikkiṉṟatu. ippirivut tuṉpattiṉi evvāṟu poṟuppēṉ?” eṉpatu pōṉṟa avaḷ kūṟṟu.
itu peṇpāṟ kūṟṟup peruntiṇik kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(pu. ve. mā. 16-9)
poḻutu kaṇṭu mayaṅkal -
talivaṉ oruvaḻit taṇappavē, talivi maliyil katiravaṉ maṟiya, paṟavikaḷ iṇiyākat tam kūṭu nōkkic celvaṉa kaṇṭu, tāṉ āṟṟāmi mikku mālinērattik kaṇṭu piriviṉāl maṉam mayaṅkutal.
itaṉik ‘kiḻavōṉ pirintuḻik kiḻatti māliyam poḻutu kaṇṭu iraṅkal’ eṉpa.	(na.a. 154)
iḵtu ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 188)
poḻutu talivitta kiyaṟu kāli -
kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉ yātāṉum oru paruvattē mīṇṭu varuvatākak kūṟip pirintu ceṉṟa apparuvam iru tiṅkaḷ elliyi yuṭiyatāyiṉum, apparuvam tōṉṟiya aḷavilēyē, “talivaṉ kuṟittuc ceṉṟa paruvam vantu viṭṭatē; avaṉ iṉṉum mīṇṭilaṉē” eṉṟu talivi ceyalaṟṟa kālam.	(to. po. 232 iḷa.)
antik kālattē, akaṉintiṇikku itaṟku mēlatākiya meyppāṭu kūṭātu eṉṟu variyaṟukkappaṭṭa ‘kiyaṟa vurittal’ eṉṟa meyppāṭu eytiya kālattē, avvāṟ ṟāmiyiṉ ikantaṉavāka muṭivilē vikkappaṭṭa meyp pāṭukaḷāvaṉa - maṉṟattirunta cāṉṟōr aṟiyat taṉ talivaṉ peyarum peṟṟiyum avaṉoṭu puṇarntamiyum tōṉṟak kūṟiyum, aḻutum, araṟṟiyum poḻutoṭu pulampiyum, ñāyiṟu mutaliyavaṟṟoṭu kūṟattakātaṉa kūṟiyum ivvāṟu nikaḻttutalum piṟavumāṉa ivi, kaḷavukkālattu vāḷāṉ eytum piriviṉkaṇ kūṟāmal pirinta talivaṉ palanāḷkaḷ mīṇṭu vārāta niliyil talivikku nikaḻum. e-ṭu : kali. 142.	(236 nac.)
poḻutu vaḻuvutaliṉākiya kuṟṟam kāṭṭal -
iravup poḻutiṉkaṇ ēṟpaṭum kuṟṟaṅkaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟit talivaṉit tōḻiyum taliviyum iravukkuṟi vilakkutal.
	(to. po. 210 nac.)
`poṟiyiṉ yātta puṇarcci' -
ūḻiṉāl kūṭṭappaṭṭa kūṭṭam; aḵtāvatu iyaṟkip puṇarcci. (to.po. 109 iḷa.) teyvattāṉ nikaḻnta puṇarcci.	(111 nac.)
poṟiyuvanturittal -
kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉuṭiya parattiyiṟ pirivil, parattiyar talivaṉi etirkoṇṭami kēṭṭa talivi neñcuṭintu puṟattu veḷippaṭāmal poṟuttataṉik kaṇṭa tōḻi, “yāṉ ivvāṟākavum, kalaṅkātu niṉṟa mikka poṟiyuṭiya taliviyip paṟṟi yāṉ iṉṟu pēcuvaṉa eṉ!” eṉṟu avaḷi uvantu kūṟuvatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 353)
poṉṟat tuṇital -
talivi iṟakkat tuṇital.
kaḷavukkālattil talivaṉ aṭikkaṭip piritalāl ēṟpaṭṭa taliviyatu uṭalmelivu teyvattāl āyiṟṟu eṉṟu karuti ataṟkup parikāramākac cevili veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttat tuṇintaviṭattu, tōḻi tāṉ aṟattoṭu niṟkalāmā eṉṟu viṉaviyavaḻi, talivi, “tāykku nām kaḷavoḻuk katti uṇarttip piṉ varivu māṭcimippaṭṭa illaṟam naṭattuvati viṭa, tāykku uṇarttātu talivaṉpirivi ṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa nōyiṉāl eṉṉuyiri ippoḻutē nīkkat tuṇintuḷḷēṉ” eṉṟu aṟattoṭunili maṟuttuk kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘kaṟpiṉic cārnta aṟattoṭunili’ eṉṟa kiḷavik kaṇṇatoru tuṟi.	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 39)
poṉṉaṇivu urittal -
kaḷavukkālattil talivaṉ iravukkuṟiyit tavirttu, virivil taliviyi maṇantukōṭal vēṇṭum eṉpataṟ kāka, talivaṉiṭam paṭittumoḻiyāṉ makaṭpēcap palarum varuvatākak kūṟiya tōḻi, “palarum taliviyi makaṭpēca muṉvantuḷḷaṉar; iṉi niṉ karuttu eṉṉa?” eṉṟu palarum poṉṉaṇiya muṉ vantati urittal.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
	(kōvi. 196)
pōkkaḻuṅkiyalpu -
talivaṉ celavi nīkkum taṉmi.
kaṟpukkālattil ōtal kāval tūtu poruḷ paki eṉṟa aintu vakippaṭṭa naṟceyalkaḷiyum  āṟṟat talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu ūriṭiyiṭṭum kāṭiṭiyiṭṭum vēṟṟiṭam eytic cila tiṅkaḷ taṅkic ceyaṟpaṭṭu eṭutta ceyali naṉmuṟiyil muṭittu varuvatē vaḻakkam. talivaṉ ivi poruṭṭut taliviyip piriyumpōtu avaḷ avaṉpirivu kuṟittu varuntavē, cilanāḷkaḷ illattil taṅki avaḷi amitiyuṟac ceytu piṉ taṉ ceyal kuṟittu avaḷip pirintu ceṉṟu mīṭalē aṉpiṉ aintiṇik kaṟpiyaṟ ceyti. ataṉi viṭuttut taṉ pirivāl talivi varuntuvāḷē eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉ kaṭamikaḷi maṟantu avaḷuṭaṉēyē taṅkiyiruttal poruntāk kāmap pakutiyātaliṉ, akapporuṭ peruntiṇit tuṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka orucār āciriyarāl itu koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(na. a. 243)
pōkku aṟivittal -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ uṭaṉpōkkiṉi virumputaṟ kuriya kāraṇam viṉaviya taliviyiṭam tōḻi, “nīṅkaḷ uṭalum uyirum pōla, oruvariyoruvar iṉṟiyami yātavar āyiṉīr. num kātali aṟinta piṟakum talivaṉukku varakkūṭiya tuṉpattiṉi añci yāṉ avaṉi varavu vilakka vēṇṭiyavaḷāyuḷḷēṉ. avaṉ kaḷaviṉi viṭuttu varivukku muyaṉṟāl namar niṉṉi avaṉukkuk koṭukka iciyār. inniliyil nī talivaṉuṭaṉ uṭaṉpōkkil ceṉṟu maṇappatu tavira vēṟu vaḻiyilli” eṉṟu tali makaṉ uṭaṉpōkku virumpiyataṉ kāraṇattik kūṟiyatu.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
	(kōvi. 207)
pōkku uṭaṉpaṭṭa talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu -
“nam talivar koṭiya pāliyik kaṭantu, eṉṉi maṇattaṟporuṭṭup poruḷīṭṭac ceṉṟuḷḷār. avarip palakālum niṉippatāl eṉnutal pacappa, eṉ mēṉi meliya, eṉ tuyaramum palar aṟiya veḷippaṭukiṟatu. virivil eṉṉuyir pōyviṭumō eṉṟa niṉippum varukiṟatu.
“iṅṅaṉam pirinta talivaṉi niṉittu ivaḷ varuntuvatu iyaṟki eṉṟuṇarātu ivvūrp peṇṭir tūṟṟum alari nōkki, ‘virivil paḻiccoṟkaḷ palkuvataṟku muṉ eṉmakaḷ tirumaṇam ceytukoṇṭu iṉiya illaṟam naṭatta aruḷvāyāka!’ eṉṟu teyvatti vaṇaṅki niṟkum nam aṉṉiyiṉmuṉ nam kaḷavuvāḻkki evvaḷavu tavaṟākat tōṉṟukiṟatu?” eṉa variviṭi vittut tali vaṉ piritalukku uṭaṉpaṭṭa talivi, avaṉ piriviṉāṉ ēṟpaṭṭa niliyit tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.(to. po. 42 nac.)
	(akanā. 95)
pōkku uṭaṉpaṭṭa talivi tōḻikku urittami -
pōkku uṭaṉpaṭutalāvatu - talivaṉoṭu tāṉum uṭaṉ pōkkukku eḻutal. talivaṉi aṭitaṟkaṇ ēṟpaṭṭa palavaki iṭiyūṟukaḷāl taṉ neṟṟiyil pacali parattaliyum taṉ taḷiriyotta mēṉi vāṭutaliyum uṭkoṇṭu, ivaṟṟāl ūravar palarum palavākap pēcum paḻimoḻikaḷukku vāyppu ēṟpaṭumē eṉṟu añciyum, aṉṉiyiṉ cīṟṟattit toṭarntu poṟuttukkoṇṭu taṉ illattil iruttal iyalātu eṉa muṭivu ceytum, talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōkkuc cellutal tavira vēṟu uyyum vaḻi illi eṉṟu talivi tōḻikku urittal. ivvāṟu urippavum ippāṭal iṭam ceyyum. 			(akanā. 95) (to. po. 42 nac.)
pōkkum varivum maṉivikaṇ tōṉṟal -
1.	kaḷavukkālam pōlātu iṭiviṭātu iṉpam nukarta lōṭu illaṟam nikaḻttum urimiyit talivi uṟutiyākap peṟutali virumputalāṉum,
2.	āḷviṉik kuṟippuṭimiyiṉ āṇmakkaḷ aṭikkaṭip pirintu pōvar eṉṟu añcum accam makaḷirkku uṇṭu ākalāṉum,
3.	kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttumpōtu ampalum alarum kaḷavi veḷippaṭuttakkūṭum eṉṟu talivi karuta lāṉum,
4.	piṟar taṉṉi aiyamuṟap pārkkum kāraṇattāṉ kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭuviṭṭatō eṉṟu accam koṇṭu pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟikaḷi vilakkum nili ēṟpaṭuvata ṉāṉum,
taliviyiṭattē talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōkkum, talivaṉi variyak karututalum nikaḻum.	(to. po. 225 nac.)
`pōtaviḻ kōti pulampunaṉi tīrttal' -
taliviyiṭamiruntu tūtu varappeṟṟa talivaṉ, kaṟpiṭip pirivil, taṉ ceyali virivil muṭittut talivi illattiṟku vantu avaḷatu taṉimittuyarip pōkkutal. (mulli naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 94 uri mēṟ.)
pōy aḵtu īṇṭal -
talivi, talivaṉuṭiya iraṇṭām maṉivi kāmakkiḻatti yar ākiyōruṭaṉ veṟuppiṉṟi naṭpuṟavōṭu aṉpākap paḻakum ceyti, avaṉukkup pulaṉātal. (maruta naṭiyiyal)
	(vī. cō. 95 uri mēṟ.)
ma
makkaḷ tiṇinilip peyar -
kuṟiñci mulli marutam neytal pāli eṉṟa aivaki nilattum vāḻum uyarmakkaḷ potumakkaḷ ākiyōrukku vaḻaṅkappaṭum potuppeyarkaḷ.
mulli mutaliya nilattu vāḻum makkaḷē akaṉin tiṇikku uriyarāy tiṇinilippeyar peṟuvar. avarkaḷ talimakkaḷākak kiḻami ceyyap peṟuṅkāl, avvattiṇikkuriya marapuppeyarkaḷiyē peṟuvar. akattiṇikkaṇ ivvāṟu marapuppeyarkaḷip peṟum makkaḷē puṟattiṇikkaṇ antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷā ḷar maṟavar pāṇar kūttar eṉṉum camutāyap peyarkaḷip peṟuvar. puṟattiṇi māntari āciriyar ‘ēṉōr’ (to. po. 24) eṉak kuṟippiṭṭār. antaṇar mutalāṉōr akattiṇi māntarāka varumiṭattu īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum tiṇinilip peyarkaḷiyē peṟuvar.			(to. akat. 22 ca. pāla.)
makkaḷuḷ peṇṭirip pāṭum marapu -
makkaḷuḷ peṇpāli iyaṟpeyar cuṭṭip pāṭutal ciṟappiṉmiyiṉ, ‘ceyirtīr kaṟpiṉ cēyiḻi kaṇava’ (puṟanā. 3) eṉṟāṟ pōlac ciṟupāṉmi āṇmakkaḷoṭu paṭuttup pāṭutalē marapu.	(to. po. 81 nac.)
makkaḷik karupporuḷ tokutiyoṭu kūṭṭik kūṟāmiyatu kāraṇam -
ipporuḷatikārattil kūṟappaṭum akamum puṟamumākiya oḻukalāṟukaḷ yāvum māntarkkē uriyavāy avarāl nikaḻttap peṟuvaṉa. ātaliṉ tiṇikkuriya urip poruḷi viḷittaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉavar makkaḷ. ammakkaḷit tiṇi aṟitaṟkuriya karupporuḷōṭu oppak kūṟutal takavaṉṟu. tiṇi uṇartaṟkuk karuviyākiya uḷḷuṟi yuvamam teyvam oḻinta karupporuḷkaḷi iṭamākak koṇṭu varum eṉṉum ilakkaṇam makkaḷik karupporu ḷoṭu cērttuk kūṟiṉ citiyum. ākavē makkaḷik karupporuḷ tokutiyoṭu kūṭṭik kūṟiṉārallar.			(to. akat. 22 ca.pāla.)
makaṭpēccurittal (1) -
ayalavar taliviyip peṇkēṭkum ceytiyit tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟutal.
“taliva! ittaliviyi maṇamceytukoḷḷa makaṭ pēcap paricapporuḷōṭu ayalavar varat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉar. nī ittaliviyiṉ naṭpu mikutal vēṇṭiṉi yāyiṉ, tirumāluṭiya tirukkaṭittāṉattukkuc ceṉṟu ayalavar muṉṉē ivaḷi maṇappāyāka” eṉṟu, ayalavar taliviyi makaṭ pēca varutal kūṟit taliviyōṭu uṭaṉ pōkumāṟu talivaṉit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttutal.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. a. pāṭal. 654)
makaṭpēccurittal (2) -
talivaṉi virivil taliviyi maṇakkumāṟu kūṟiya tōḻi mīṇṭum, “ayalavar taliviyi makaṭpēca varukiṉ ṟaṉar. ātaliṉ nī taliviyi virivil maṇam ceytu koḷka; iṉṟēl, uṭaṉkoṇṭu celka. iraṇṭiṉuḷ oṉṟi iṉṟē ceyka” eṉṟu ayalavar taliviyi maṇam pēca varuvatik kūṟutal.
itaṉi, ‘varivu kaṭātal’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇ ‘piṟar varivu uṇarttal’ eṉṉum tuṟiyākak kūṟupa. 	(na. a. 166)
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 195)
makaṭūuk kuṇam -
peṇpāṟkuriya iyalpu nōkkuka.
peṇṭirkkuriya nāṇam, maṭam, accam, payirppu eṉṉum nāṟkuṇam.	(iṟi. a. 2 uri)
aṉpu kāraṇattāl tōṉṟiya accam, kāmakkuṟippu nikaḻntavaḻip paṭuvatōr uḷḷoṭukkam (nāṇam), cevi liyar koḷuttakkoṇṭu koṇṭatu viṭāmi (maṭaṉ) eṉa ivi.	(to. po. 99 nac.)
aṭakkamum, maṟi pulappaṭuttāmal niṟuttum uḷḷamum, maṉakkōṭṭamiṉmiyum, kaḷaviṉkaṇ ceyyat takuvaṉa kūṟutalum, naṉmi payappaṉavum tīmi payappaṉavum  aṟivit talum, uḷḷakkaruttu aṟital arumiyum iviyellām peṇpāṟkuriya iyalpukaḷ	(to. po. 209 nac.)
makappoṟi kūrnta vaṇṇam urittal -
talivi karuvuṟṟakāli peṟṟa aḻakiṉit talivaṉ makiḻntu kūṟutal.
“talivi karuvuṟṟa kālattil avaḷ uṟuppukkaḷ muḻu aḻakiṉiyum niṟattiṉiyum peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. avaḷ kumuta vāyum, nīlak kaṇkaḷum, makaṉ taṅkiyirukkum ālili vayiṟum ataṉāl makiḻum mukattāmariyum, nāḷum aḻaku mikukiṉṟaṉa” (ampikā. 461) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.

itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. iḵtu uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal.	(i. vi. 555 uri)
makaḷirkku uriya allātaṉa -
talivar kalattil pirintu cellumpōtu makaḷir uṭaṉ cellutalum, kāmam evvaḷavu mikuntālum talivari aṭitaṟkākat tām maṭalēṟutalum, talivar irukkum iṭam nōkkit tāmē ceṉṟaṭitalum makaḷirkku uriya allāta ceyalkaḷām.	(to. po. 34, 35, 98 kuḻa.)
“makaḷir tam kāmatti maṟittuk koḷḷutalē takkatu” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyatu -
“kāmatti veḷiyiṭak kūṭātu eṉṟu nāṉ eṉṉuḷḷēyē maṟikkattāṉ niṉikkiṟēṉ. āyiṉ aḵtu eṉ karutti niṉiyāmal tummal pōla veḷippaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟatē!“.
“iṉṟu vari nāṉ eṉṉi eṉṉuḷḷattiluḷḷa kāmam puṟattārkku evvāṟṟāṉum pulaṉākātu kākkum niṟiyuṭiyēṉ eṉṟē karutiyiruntēṉ. āyiṉ, iṉṟu eṉ kāmam yāṉ maṟittalik kaṭantu maṉṟiṉkaṇ palarum aṟiya veḷippaṭukiṟatu” eṉṉum talivi kūṟṟu.
	(kuṟaḷ 1253, 1254)
makaḷir maṭalēṟutal -
naṭuvaṇintiṇikkaṇṇō, kikkiḷi peruntiṇi ivaṟṟiṉkaṇṇō talivi maṭalēṟiṉāḷākac cūttiram etuvum illi. maṭalēṟuvatākak kūṟuvatuṇṭu; atu kikkiḷippāṟpaṭum.	(to. po. 35 nac.)
makaṟku aṟivuṟuttal -
talivi taṉ makaṉukku aṟivuri kūṟutal.
“naṭivaṇṭi ōṭṭum ciṟuva! eṉ taṅkiyarākiya paratti yariṭattum aṉpu kāṭṭum celva! uṉtantiyip pōlat taṉṉic cērntavarukkut tīṅku ceyvataṉiyum avarka ḷiṭam poyyurippataṉiyum kaṟkāmal, nuntiyiṉ piṟa nalla paṇpukaḷiyē piṉpaṟṟuvāyāka” (ampikā. 504) eṉṟu makaṉiṭam talivi kūṟutal.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu, uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ‘uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉpataṉpāṟpaṭum.	(i. vi. 555 uri)
makaṟpaḻittu neruṅkal -
makaṉit talivi paḻittuk kōpittal.
“makaṉē! kōyilukkup pōvatākak kūṟi iṭiyē paratti yarkaḷik kaṇṭu uriyāṭi varukiṉṟāyē! parattiyar cērikku viruntiṉarāyp pōyc cērvatu num kulattārkkē iyalpākiviṭṭatē! uṉṉi nontu kūṟuvatil payaṉ eṉṉa? uṉ tanti ip paruvattil parattiyarcērikkup pōkavum, nī kuḻantip paruvattilēyē aṅkuc cellat toṭaṅkiviṭṭāyē!” (ampikā. 505) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟit talivi makaṉik kaṭital.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu; ‘uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉpataṉpāṟpaṭum.	(i. vi. 555 uri)
‘makap paḻittu neruṅkal’ eṉpataṉi (to.po. 147 nac.),
‘talivi, tantiyi................ neruṅkaliṉkaṇ kūṟal’ - kāṇka.
makaṉ tāy uyarvu -
talivaṉuṭiya mūtta maṉiviyē ciṟappuṭiyavaḷ. mūtta maṉivikku makappēṟu vāykkātapōtu talivaṉ piṉ muṟi maṉiviyik koḷḷutalum uṇṭu. mutal maṉiviyi nōkka, vaḻimuṟi maṉivi caṟṟuc ciṟappuk kuṟintavaḷē. āyiṉ mutal maṉivikkup putalvaṉ il lāta niliyil, vaḻimuṟi maṉivi, makaṉukkut tāyākum pēṟuṟṟāl, makaṉip peṟṟa avaḷit taṉṉiṉ tāḻnta vaḷākak karutātu talivi taṉṉi ottavaḷāka matittuc ciṟappuc ceyvāḷ.
piṉmuṟi maṉivi mutalmaṉiviyit taṉṉiṉ mikkāḷāka matittalum, piṉmuṟi maṉivi makaṉtāy āyiṉapiṉ mutal maṉivi avaḷukkut taṉṉi otta matippuk koṭuttalum illaṟattiṉ neṟiyākat talivaṉ taṉ maṉiviyarkkuk kaṟpitta ceytikaḷām.	(to. po. 174 nac.)
makiḻnturittal -
kaḷavukkālattil talivaṉ variporuṭkup pirinta kāli pirivu kuṟittu varuntiya taliviyik kuṟittu, avaṉ mīṇṭuvantu tirumaṇattiṟku ēṟpāṭu ceyya, maṇamuracoli kēṭṭa tōḻi, “talivaṉ tanta peṟutaṟkariya taḻikaḷi vāṭāmal vittu attaḻiyē paṟṟukkōṭākat talivi āṟṟiyiruntāḷ” eṉṟu taṉṉuḷḷē makiḻntu kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘maṇañ ciṟappurittal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 300)
makiḻnaṉ -
marutanilat talivaṉukkuriya tiṇinilippeyar.	(na. a. 23)
maṅkala moḻi -
maṅkalattāl kūṟum col. atu cettārit ‘tuñciṉār’ eṉṟu kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 240 iḷa.)
talivaṉ tammi aṟaṉiṉṟit tuṟattaliṉ avaṉukkut tīṅku varum eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu tōḻiyum taliviyum ataṟku añci, “nōyila rāka, nam kātalar!” (akanā. 115) eṉṟāṟ pōla vāḻttutal.	(244 nac.)
maṭappam -
1. maruta nilattu ūr (piṅ. 569) 2. maṭam.	(ṭu)
maṭam -
peṇṭirkkuriya nālvakik kuṇaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu; koḷut tak koṇṭu koṇṭatu viṭāmi.	(iṟi. a. 2 uri)
maṭal ūrtal -
“virumpiya kātaliyip peṟa iṭiyūṟu nērntu nāṉ tuṉpuṟṟēṉ; ākavē ūrampalattil maṭalēṟutal nērntuviṭṭatu; eṉ kāmam attuṇip peritu!“eṉṟu talivaṉ maṭalēṟutal.
iḵtu irupāṟ peruntiṇikkaṇṇatoru tuṟi.
	(pu. ve. mā. 17-2)
“maṭal ūrtaṟkāṉa pakaṟpōtu iṉṟu kaḻintuviṭṭatu!” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivaṉ kūṟiyatu -
“talivi kāraṇamāka nāṉ uṟṟa kātalāl eṉ kaṇkaḷ oru pōtum tuyilutal illi; ātaliṉ iravilum viḻit tiruntu maṭal ūrvēṉ” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.(kuṟaḷ 1136)
maṭal ēṟutal -
taṉ kuṟi tīrappeṟāta talivaṉ paṉaṅkarukkāl kutiri ceytu, taṉ uṭampu muḻutum nīṟu pūcikkoṇṭu, pūḷippū elumpu erukkampū ākiya ivaṟṟi  māliyākak kaṭṭi aṇintukoṇṭu, akkutirimītu amarntu ataṉic cilar vītiyil iḻuttuc cella vītiyil cellutal.
itu nampi akapporuḷ kūṟum akappuṟap peruntiṇi. itu taliviyi muṟiyāka aṭiyāmal taṉ kāma mikutiyi ūraṟiyap pulappaṭutti variya muyalutaliṉ akap puṟap peruntiṇi āyiṟṟu.	(na. a. 244)
maṭal ēṟutalit (talivaṉ) taṉmēl vittuc cāṟṟal -
pāṅki taṉatu kuṟiyi ēṟkāta niliyil varuntiya talivaṉ, maṭalēṟutali ulakiṉmēl vittuk kūṟiyapiṉ, pāṅki ataṉiyum poruṭpaṭuttātu niṉṟa niliyil, tāṉē avvāṟu maṭalēṟappōvatākak kūṟutal.
“uṉ taliviyiṉ ōviyattiyum eṉ ōviyattiyum oru tuṇiyil eḻutik kik koṇṭu, paṉaṅkarukkāl ceyyap paṭṭa kutirimītēṟi uṅkaḷ ūrtteruvil virivil kāṭci yaḷippēṉ” (kōvi. 76) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘taṉ tuṇipu urittal’ eṉṉum.
itu ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru 
kūṟṟu. (75).	(na. a. 145)
maṭal ēṟutaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟum ēṟiya maṭal tiṟak kūṟṟu -
talivaṉ maṭal ēṟuvēṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvatu kikkiḷi; maṭal ēṟutal peruntiṇi.
peṟṟōr taliviyiṉ kaḷavoḻukkattik karutāma lirukkum nili, ataṉāl talivi tōḻi vāyilākac cevilikkuk kūṟi naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṟṟaṟku vāyppu  ēṟpaṭāta nili, tiṉikkatir muṟṟiya piṉṉart talivi puṟampōtaṟku vāyppiṉṟi maṉiyakattē aṭintirukkum nili, iravukkuṟiyip palavaki ētaṅkaḷāl nikaḻtta iyalāta nili ākiyavi ēṟpaṭavē, taliviyik kāṇa vāyppup peṟāta talivaṉ, taṉakkum avaḷukku miṭiyē yuḷḷa toṭarpiṉi ūravarkku aṟivittu avarkaḷ vāyilākat tāṉ avaḷi maṇattaṟku vakuttukkoṇṭa toru vaḻiyē maṭalēṟutal eṉpatu. taliviyiṉ uru vatti oru tuṇiyil varintu kiyiṟkoṇṭu paṉaṅ karukkāl ceyyappaṭṭa kutiri mītēṟic cilari ak kutiriyi iḻuttu varumāṟu ceytu ūrtteruvaḻiyē talivaṉ cellat toṭaṅkiṉ, ūrmakkaḷ taliviyiṉ peṟṟōri aṇuki avaḷi avaṉukku maṇam muṭittu vippar. itaṟkāka maṭal ēṟum talivaṉ maṭalūrntu teruviṭi vantu ūrccāṉṟōri nōkki,
“cāṉṟōrkaḷē! piṟaruṭiya nōyiyum tam nōy pōlak karuti annōyit tīrppataṟkuriya parikāram tēṭutal cāṉṟōr kaṭamiyaṉṟō? miṉṉalip pōṉṟa ippeṇ taṉ oḷiyiyum uruvattiyum kāṭṭi eṉ neñcil iṭam peṟṟuviṭṭāḷ. aṉṟumutalyāṉ uṟakkatti nīttuviṭ ṭēṉ. ivaḷip peṟutaṟku immaṭalēṟi yāṉ pāṭuva taṉik kēḷuṅkaḷ.
“ittalivi eṉakku eytāmiyāl yāṉ kāmakkaṭalil vīḻntu tattaḷittup pakalum iravum immaṭalmāviyē teppamākak koṇṭu nīntuvēṉ.
“eṉṉi mayakkiya ivaḷ eṉakku aḷittirukkum im maṭal mā ivaḷ tanta kāmanōykku maruntāka uḷḷatu.
“immātarāḷatu aḻaku eṉṉum peyarāl eṉṉi etirkka vanta kāmaṉuṭiya paṭikaḷ, pārppavarkaḷ eḷḷi nakiyāṭu māṟu eṉatu āṇmiyiṉ aḻakākiya matilit takarttu viṭṭaṉa.
“kāmamākiya koṭiya pakiyi uḷḷum puṟamum aḻitta lālē talivi aḷitta immaṭalmā eṉakkup pātukāva lāka uḷḷatu.
“ittaliviyiṉ aḻakāl eṉ neñcil paṟṟiya kāmat tī uyiri maṭṭum pōkkāmal eṉ uṭalmuḻuvatiyum cuṭṭerippatāl yāṉ eṅṅaṉam āṟṟuvēṉ?
“kāmanōy ākiya tīyiṉ veppattit taṇittaṟku aminta niḻalē im maṭalmā.
“cāṉṟīr! tavattil īṭupaṭṭa maṉṉaṉ ciṟitu vaḻuviṉum avvaḻuviṉip pōkki avaṉi mīṇṭum tavam cey vittuc ciṟanta muṉivarkaḷ cuvarkkattiṟkup pōkacceytu tuyariṉip pōkkuvatu pōla, eṉ tuyar pōkkutalum num kaṭami” eṉṟu kūṟutal (kali. 139) pōlvaṉa.			(to. po. 51 nac.)
maṭal tiṟam : tuṟikaḷ -
aviyāvaṉa : 1. āṟṟātu urittal, 2. ulakiṉmēl vitturittal, 3. taṉ tuṇivu urittal, 4. maṭalēṟum vaki urittal, 5. aruḷāl aritu eṉa vilakkal, 6. moḻi, naṭi eḻututal ariteṉa vilakkal, 7. avayavam eḻututal ariteṉa vilakkal, 8. uṭaṉpaṭātu vilakkal, 9. uṭaṉpaṭṭu vilakkal eṉpaṉa oṉpatum ām. ivi ēṉiya kōvi kaḷuḷ tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.			(kōvi. 73 - 81)
`maṭavōḷ taṉtiṟam māṇpuṟa nōkkal' -
tōḻi taliviyi uṭaṉpōkku nayappittavaḻit talivi taṉṉuṭiya nāṇ aḻintu viṭumē eṉṟu karututal. (pāli naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 93 uri mēṟ.)
itu ‘nāṇ aḻipu iraṅkal’ eṉavumpaṭum. 	(na. a. 182)
maṭaṟkūṟṟu -
talivaṉ maṭalēṟit taliviyit tāṉ aṭiyap pōva tākak kūṟutal.
talivaṉ taliviyi eytavēṇṭit tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṉṟāṉāka, tōḻi, avaṉ taliviyatu arumi aṟital vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttōṭu, avaṉ viruppatti niṟivēṟṟuvatāka uṭaṉē kūṟāmal avaṉ kūṟṟukkup palavakiyāka maṟumāṟṟam tantu kālatāmatam ceytāḷāka, talivaṉ avaḷ uḷḷam aṟiyātu avaḷiṭam tāṉ maṭalēṟiyāvatu taliviyi eytappōvatākak kūṟutal.
tōḻivāyilākat taliviyi muṟiyāka eyti avaḷik kūṭum aintiṇiyākiya otta kāmatti viṭuttu, avaḷi ūrār aṟiya valiyat taṉ vaḻippaṭuttum ceyalākiya  maṭalēṟutaṟkaṇ talivaṉ muṉintiruttal  aintiṇi yiṉpattiṟkup poruntāta kāmac ceyalātaliṉ, ataṉi akappuṟap peruntiṇik kiḷaviyuḷ oṉṟāka orucārār koṇṭaṉar.	(na. a. 243)
maṇantavar aṇitaṉik kaṇṭōr makiḻtal -
variyum nāḷaṉṟu talivaṉum taliviyum tirumaṇam ceytukoṇṭa maṇakkōlattiṉik kaṇṭa makkaḷ, “taliva! nī taliviyoṭu koṇṭuḷḷa immaṇakkōlak kāṭciyik kaṇṭa em kaṇkaḷ pōla eṅkaḷ uyirum makiḻkiṟatu!” (tiruppatik. 397) eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu ‘varintu kōṭal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. aka. 82)
maṇantavaṉ pōyapiṉ vanta pāṅkiyoṭu, iṇaṅkiṉa mintaṉip pukaḻtal -
parattiyari nāṭit tēril puṟappaṭṭa talivaṉ, vaḻiyil taṉ putalvaṉi etirppaṭṭu avaṉi eṭuttukkoṇṭu taliviyiṉ muṉvara, avaḷ pulavi taṇintu taṉ kaṇavaṉi ēṟka, avaṉum iṉitu kūṭi makiḻntu ceṉṟa piṉ vanta pāṅkiyiṭam talivi taṉ putalvaṉip pukaḻtal.
“tōḻi! eṉ arumi makaṉ iṉṟu eṉakkuc ceyta nalam tāṉ ettuṇip peritu! parattiyari nāṭip puṟappaṭṭa eṉ talivaṉit taṭuttut tērōṭu eṉṉiṭam aḻittu vantuviṭṭāṉ!” (tañci.kō. 405) eṉpatu avaṉip pukaḻnta talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ, ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; ‘uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉṉum vakiyatu.	(na. a. 207)
maṇañciṟappurittal : tuṟikaḷ -
1. maṇamuracu kūṟal, 2. makiḻnturittal, 3. vaḻipāṭu kūṟal, 4. vāḻkkinalam kūṟal, 5. kātal kaṭṭurittal, 6. kaṟpaṟivit tal, 7. kaṟpup payappurittal, 8. maruvutal urittal, 9. kalavi yiṉpam kūṟal eṉpaṉa oṉpatum ām.	(kōvi. 299 - 307)
`maṇam puṇar maṉṉaṉum maṅkiyum vaṭamīṉ, iṇaṅkupu kaṇṭati ikuḷi vāḻttal' -
variyum nāḷaṉṟu tirumaṇam muṭintapiṉ talivaṉ talivikku aruntatiyik kāṭṭiyatik kaṇṭatōḻi, “taliva! nīyum taliviyum mīṉa rāciyil vanta kurutaci pōlap polivuṭaṉ vāḻka!” (tiruppatik. 398) eṉṟu vāḻttiyatu.
itu ‘varintu kōṭal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. aka. 82)
maṇamaṉikku vanta cevilikkup pāṅki avarkaḷ iruvaruṭiya aṉpiṉ tiṟattik kūṟutal -
tirumaṇam naṭanta piṟaku, talivaṉum taliviyum illaṟam naṭattum illattiṟkuc cevili vantapōtu, tōḻi, avarkaḷatu aṉpiṉiyalpiṉi viḷakkik kūṟutal. ‘maṉṟal maṉivaru cevilikku ikuḷi, aṉpuṟavu uṇarttal’ eṉpaṉa nūṟpā aṭikaḷ.
“aṉṉāy! niṉ makaḷuṭiya nalamellām uṇṭu makiḻum talivaṉ avaḷik kaṇamum piriyāmal, taḻuviya ki nekiḻāmal aṉpu ceykiṟāṉ” eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu kaṟpiyalil ‘ilvāḻkki’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 203)
maṇamaṉic ceṉṟa vanta cevili, poṟṟoṭi kaṟpiyal naṟṟāyk kuṇarttal -
tirumaṇattiṟkup piṉṉar, talivaṉum taliviyum taṉimaṉi iruntu tam ilvāḻkkiyi ataṟkuriya aṟamum iṉpamum poṅkum vakiyil naṭattivaruvati avarkaḷ maṉikkē ceṉṟu pārttu makiḻntu mīṇṭa cevili, naṟṟāyiṭam ataṉip paṟṟip pārāṭṭip pēcutal.
“uṉmakaḷ tāṉ uṇṇa vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutum paci yuṇarcciyum maṇaṟcōṟu aṭum viḷiyāṭṭum viṭuttuk kaṇavaṉ varuntum paciyit tīrkka uṇavu aṭum il vāḻkkiyiyum, viruppoṭu viruntu puṟantarutaliyum, talivaṉ virumpumavaṟṟiyē tāṉ virumpic ceytaliyum evvāṟu ivvaḷavu virivil kaṟṟuviṭṭāḷ eṉpatu viyappāka uḷḷatu!” (ampikā. 446) eṉpatu pōṉṟa cevili kūṟṟu.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ, ‘ilvāḻkki’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 203)
maṇamuracu kūṟal -
kaḷavukkālattil variporuṭkup pirinta talivaṉ celvattoṭu mīḷa, paricapporuḷ taruvataṟku taliviyatu illattu mukappil talivaṉuṭiya maṇamuracu muḻaṅka, makiḻnta tōḻi taliviyiṭam , “nām nam tuyartīra, nam illiṉkaṇ nam talivaṉuṭiya muracu muḻaṅkukiṟatu; iṉi namakkuk kuṟiyoṉṟum illi” eṉa varivu nikaḻap pōkum makiḻvil kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘maṇañ ciṟappurittal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 299)
maṇamuracu kēṭṭu makiḻnturittal -
