kaḷavukkālattil variporuṭkup pirinta talivaṉ poruḷoṭu mīṇṭu paricapporuḷ koṭukka muṉvara, tōḻi talivikkut talivaṉuṭiya tērvaravu kūṟiya nērattil, maṉiyakattōr talivaṉi varavēṟkap pūraṇa kumpam tōraṇam mutaliyavaṟṟāl maṉiyi alaṅkarit tati makiḻvuṭaṉ kūṟutal.
itu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 296)
matinili urittu vāyil nērvittal -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ vāyiliṭattāṉāka, tōḻi illiruntu nallaṟam ceyyum kulamakaḷirukku irukka vēṇṭiya nallaṟiviṉ tiṟatti eṭuttup pukaṭṭi, ūṭal nīkki vāyil nērvittal.
“acuviṉiyākiya mutal naṭcattirattiṉip pirintu pala nāḷ vēṟu naṭcattiraṅkaḷiṭattut taṅkit taṉ ceyal kuṟittuc ciṟitum cintiyātu cantiraṉ mīṇṭum acuviṉi naṭcattirat tiṭam varuṅkāl ammutalnāḷ ūṭātu cantiraṉi ēṟṟuk koḷvatu pōla, taliviyākiya nīyum parattiyar palariyum tōyntu paṉṉāḷ kaḻittu iṟutiyil iṟ parattiyiṭam taṅki avaḷiyum nīṅki niṉ illiṉkaṇ vanta illaṟakkiḻavaṉit tuṉpuṟutti namatu kaṭikāvalaṉ ākkātu ēṟṟuk koḷka” eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟi avaḷ poṟiyuṭimip paṇpiṉic cuṭṭi vāyil nērvittal pōlvaṉa.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. a. pāṭal 557)
matiyiṉ avaravar maṉakkaruttu uṇartal -
tōḻi taṉ matinuṭpattāl, talivaṉ taliviyiruva ruṭiya kātaliyum aṟintukoḷḷutal.
“ivaḷum tiṉikākka varavilli; puṉattiṟku, ivaṉum māṉtēṭi varavilli. nam etiril nāṇam koṇṭavar pōla uḷḷa iruvarum, tamakkuḷ mikavum kaḷippatu teri kiṟatu. maṟṟum iruvarum tam kaṇkaḷālēyē pēcikkoḷ vatum terikiṟatē!” eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 114 nac. mēṟ.)
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘matiyuṭampaṭutal’ eṉṉum (71) itu ‘pāṅkimati uṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 141)
matiyuṭampaṭuttal -
tōḻi taliviyiṉ nāṟṟam tōṟṟam mutaliyaṉa kaṇṭu taṉ maṉattē oruvakiyāka muṭivu ceytukoṇṭa niliyil, talivaṉ vantu tōḻiyiṭam uriyāṭat tōḻi talivaṉ taliviyiruvar karuttiṉiyum taṉ karuttiṉōṭu oṉṟu paṭutti uṇartal.
itu kuṟiyuṟa uṇartal, muṉ uṟa uṇartal, iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉ vara uṇartal eṉa mūvakippaṭum. immūṉ ṟum kūṭiya piṉṉarē tōḻi matiyuṭampaṭukkum.			(to. po. 127 nac.)
matiyuṭampaṭuttal aivaki -
talivaṉ tōḻiyi aṇukip pativiṉātal, vēḻam viṉātal, kali viṉātal, vaḻi viṉātal, vāymoḻik kiraṅkal eṉṟa aivakiyāṉ viṉaviyavaḻit tōḻi avaṉ uḷḷakkaruttup paṟṟi aiyaṅkoḷḷumāṟu avaḷ avaṉatu aṟivit taṉṉiṭattup poruntumāṟu ceytal.			(ka. kā. 30 pak. 42)
matiyuṭampaṭuttal kiḷavikaḷ (tirukkōviyār)
1. pāṅkiyiṭic cēṟal, 2. kuṟiyuṟat tuṇital, 3. vēḻam viṉātal, 4. kalimāṉ viṉātal, 5. vaḻi viṉātal, 6. pati viṉātal, 7. peyar viṉātal, 8. moḻipeṟātu kūṟal, 
9. karuttaṟivittal, 10. iṭi viṉātal eṉṉumivi pattum talivaṉ pāṅkiyi aiyuṇarviṉaḷākki atuvaḻiyāka niṉṟu taṉ kuṟi kūṟutalākiya matiyuṭampaṭuttaliṉ tuṟika ḷām.	(kōvi. 50 - 59)
matiyuṭampaṭutal -
talivaṉ taṉṉiṭam kūṟiyavaṟṟiyum taliviyiṉ ceyal kaḷiyum tōḻi taṉittaṉiyākavum cēravum pārttu ārāyntu, talivaṉukkum talivikkumiṭiyē iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷ potuvāyiruppataṉiyum iruvarukkum uyir oṉṟāyiruttaliyum kaṇṭu, iruvar aṟivum oṉṟā yiruttali viyantu kūṟutal. ‘matiyuṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉpatum atu.
itu ‘matiyiṉiṉ avaravar maṉakkaruttu uṇartal’ eṉavum kūṟappeṟum.	(na. a. 141)
itu ‘nāṇa nāṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; ituvē iṟutik kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 71)
matiyuṭampāṭu -
talivaṉtaliviyartam matiyōṭu tōḻiyatumatiyum ottuc cellutal.	(to. kaḷa. 37 ca. pāla.)
matiyuṭaṉ ākkal -
itu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattil tōḻi, talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa iruvar uḷḷamum oruvar oruvarmāṭṭup porunti iruppati uṇartal. ovvoruvarum tattam aṟivi maṟṟavariṭam amittukkoṇṭati aṟivatu matiyuṭam pāṭākum.
itu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam (pakaṟkuṟi) eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi. 17)
matuppoḻi tārōṉ maṇamaṉi vāyil, potut talattiruntuḻip puritīr maṉiviyar, niṉivaṟi kaṇputi nikaḻttal -
talivaṉatu iruppiṭam vantu talivi avaṉkaṇkaḷit taṉ kiyāl pottiyavaḻi, avaṉ ellāt taliviyarkkum potuvāka amiyumāṟu, “cempoṟ koṭi pōlvāy! cintāmaṇiyām teyvika maṇiyē tam kivarappeṟṟavarkaḷ aṟparkaḷākiya ciṟucelvar muṉpu irappārkaḷō? eṉ kaṇkaḷi nī aṉpōṭu pottiyataṉ kāraṇam eṉṉa?” (tiruppatik. 501) eṉṟu kūṟutal.
iḵtu uṇarvatōṭu uṇarā ūṭaṟkuriya tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. aka. 104)
mantimēl vittu varivu kaṭātal -
talivaṉ kaḷavoḻukkatti nīṭṭikkum viruppattuṭaṉ irukka, avaṉi nōkkit tōḻi, “oru kaṭuvaṉ taṉ mantikku māṅkaṉikaḷit tēṉil tōyttuk koṭuttut tāṉ atu nukarvatu kaṇṭu makiḻnta kāṭciyik kaṇṭa talivi, icceyal nam kātalariṭattu illiyē eṉṟu, nī taṉṉi maṇantu illaṟam naṭattāmi kuṟittu āṟṟāḷā yuḷḷāḷ” eṉṟu oru mantiyiṉ ceyalik kuṟippiṭuvāḷ pōlat talivaṉit taliviyi maṇakkumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttal.
itu ‘varivu muṭukkam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 257)
mayakkam (3) -
kaḷavoḻukkattiṟkuriya uṇarvup pakutikaḷ pattaṉuḷ itu oṉṟu.
mayakkamāvatu, ceyvaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ ārāycciyum kaṭippiṭiyu miṉṟi nekiḻtalum viḷivaṟiyātu pēcutalum ceyalpuritalum ām.	(to. kaḷa. 9 ca. pāla.)
mayaṅki urittal -
talivaṉukkuk kuṟi nērnta tōḻi taliviyiṭam kuṟi nayappuk karuti uriyāṭiyavaḻi, talivi uṭaṉpaṭaṟkuk kālam tāḻkkat tāṉ maṉam mayaṅkit taliviyi muṉintu urittal (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)
itu ‘taliviyi muṉital’ (na. a. 148) eṉavumpaṭum.
	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ)
mayiloṭu kūṟi varivu kaṭātal -
pakaṟkuṟi iṟutikkaṇ talivaṉ ceviyiṟpaṭumāṟu tōḻi mayilukkuk kūṟuvāḷāy, “mayilkāḷ! vēṅkikaḷ pūttami yāl tiṉi muṟṟiyatākak karutit tiṉikkatirkaḷi emar koytuviṭṭaṉar. ātaliṉ iṉi emakkut tiṉippuṉaṅ kākkum vāyppilli. iṉi ippakkam vārōm. talivaṉ ippuṉattukku vantāl yāṅkaḷ iṉi iṅku vara vāyp pilli eṉpataṉik kūṟuṅkaḷ” eṉṟāṟpōlak kūṟit talivaṉi varivu kaṭātal.
itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
	(kōvi. 145)
`marīiya maruṅku' -
kaḷavoḻukkam nīṭṭitta iṭam.	(to.po. 208 iḷa.)
naṭuvaṇ aintiṇikku irupakkaṅkaḷilum vittu ōtappaṭum kikkiḷi peruntiṇi eṉṟa akappuṟattiṇikaḷ.			(211 nac.)
maruṅku aṇital (1) -
iravukkuṟiyiṭit taṉṉi aḷaviṉ mikap pukaḻnta talivaṉ coṟ kēṭṭa talivi nāṇattāl tali kaviḻntu niṟpa, anniliyil avaḷic ceṉṟu taḻuva iyalātu, avaḷ nāṇam oṭuṅkutaṟku avaḷ taṉaṅkaḷāl taḷarvuṟṟa iṭiyik kāppavaṉ pōla iṭiyiṉit taḻuvi avaḷic ceṉṟaṇital.
itaṉic ‘cārtal payaṉākap pukaḻtal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.
	(i. vi. 517 uri)
itu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (165) sippeyarttuṟi pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattilum, pakaṟkuṟiyi lum nikaḻkiṟatu. (kōvi. 45, 121) ]
maruṅku aṇital (2) -
talivaṉ vēṟu nōkkuṭaṉ celvāṉ pōlat taliviyi neruṅkic cellutal; ‘vaṇṭōcci maruṅku aṇital’ eṉappaṭum.
“vaṇṭukāḷ! muṉpē ivaḷiṭi nakilpāram tāṅka māṭṭāmal nalikiṉṟatu. nīṅkaḷum ivaḷ kuḻalmītu moyttu ivaḷi māykka niṉittīrō?” eṉṟu viṉaviya vāṟē, vaṇṭu ōccuvāṉ pōlat talivikku mikka aṇmi yil cellutal.
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟāka ilakkaṇa viḷakkattuḷ taḻuvappaṭṭatu. (495 uri); tirukkōviyāruḷ iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇṇatu.
	(kōvi. 45)
maruṇṭu urittal -
maruḷuṟṟu urittal ; atu kāṇka.	(cāmi. 89)
marutak karupporuḷkaḷ - (1)
	1.	teyvam 	-	intiraṉ;
	2.	uyar makkaḷ	-	ūraṉ, makiḻnaṉ; kiḻatti, maṉivi, maṉiyōḷ;
	3.	potumakkaḷ	-	kaḷamar, uḻavar, kaṭiyar; uḻattiyar, kaṭiciyar;
	4.	paṟavi	-	vaṇṭāṉam, makaṉṟil, nāri, aṉ ṉam, pōtā, kampuḷ, kuruku, tārā, nīrkkōḻi;
	5.	vilaṅku	-	erumi, nīrnāy;
	6.	ūr	-	pērūr, mūtūr;
	7.	nīr	-	āṟṟunīr, kiṇaṟṟunīr, poykinīr, kuḷattunīr;
	8.	pū	-	tāmari, kaḻunīr, kuvaḷi, malliki;
	9.	maram	-	kāñci, vañci, marutam;
	10.	uṇavu	-	cennel, veṇṇel;
	11.	paṟi	-	nellarikiṇi, maṇamuḻavu;
	12.	yāḻ	-	marutayāḻ;
	13.	paṇ	-	marutappaṇ;
	14.	toḻil	-	viḻā nikaḻttutal, vayalil kaḷi nīkkutal, nellarital, kaṭāviṭutal, kuḷanīr āṭal, āṟṟil putuppuṉal āṭal mutaliyaṉa;
	15.	koṭi	-	mātavi
			   (to. po. 20 iḷa; 18 nac.; cilap. patikam)		(aṭiyārk; ta. ne.vi. 6 -12; na.a. 23)
marutak karupporuḷkaḷ (2) -
uyarntōr	-	nāṟkulattār;
tāḻntōr	-	vēciyar;
puḷ	-	kuyil, vaṇṭu, mayil;
vilaṅku (ūrvaṉa)	-	caṅku, vaḷi;
maram	-	kamuku, mā, palā, vāḻi, teṉṉi;
uṇavu	-	kaṉi, kiḻaṅku, cēṉi, karuṇi, cēmpu, 					mañcaḷ;
toḻil	-	vētam ōtal, vēḷvi vaḷarttal, 						tamiḻ mutaliya kalvi payilal, pāṭal, 						āṭal, nāṉku varuṇattārkkum
				uriya toḻilkaḷ;
ūr	-	aṅkāṭi, cōli, pūntōṭṭam, 						matil, 	 kūṭakōpuram, mēṭi, māṭam, 					vēli, māḷiki, kōyil, cāli, 						paṭittuṟi 	 mutaliyavaṟṟi uṭiyatu.
					(cāmi. 77,78)
marutattukkum neytaṟkum perumpoḻutu vitantu kūṟāmi -
marutam neytal tiṇikaṭkup perumpoḻutu vitantu kūṟāmiyāṉ, avaṟṟiṟkuriya uripporuḷākiya ūṭalum iraṅkalum ākiya uṇarvukaḷ ciṟupoḻutaḷavil nikaḻum kuṟukiyakāla elliyuṭiyaṉa. ūṭalum iraṅkalum nīṭṭikkumāyiṉ avi akaṉintiṇikkaṇ nikaḻum peruntiṇip pakutiyām.	(to. akat. 12 ca. pāla)
marutattukkuriya poḻutu -
marutattukku āṇṭu muḻutum perum poḻutu; ciṟupoḻutu vikaṟiyum viṭiyalum.
	vikaṟi	-	iravil cūḻnta iruḷ ciṟituciṟitākak kaḻivataṟkuriya iraviṉ iṟutip pattu nāḻiki (2 ya.a. - 6 ya.a)
	viṭiyal	-	iravuppoḻutu nīṅkak katiravaṉ tōṉṟi 	oḷivīcum kāli nēram. itu pakaṟpōtiṉ mutal pattu nāḻiki (6 ya.a - 10 ya.a.)
irāppoḻutiṉkaṇ parattiyari nōkkip pirinta talivaṉ avarkaḷuṭiya āṭalum pāṭalum kaṇṭum kēṭṭum irāppoḻutiṉik kaḻittu maṟṟavarkaḷukkut teriyāmal maṉikku mīḷum kālam vikaṟi. talivikkum iravunēram talivaṉoṭu kūṭik kaḻiyāmiyāl neñ caḻintu varuttam mikkut talivaṉōṭu ūṭal koḷvataṟku vāyppiṉi nalkuvatum vikaṟi. poḻutu viṭitaṟkuc ciṟitu nēramē iruttaliṉ itaṉāl peṟum payaṉ iṉṟu eṉṟu talivi vāyili aṭittu  ūṭali nīṭṭippavē, vikaṟiyi aṭutta viṭiyalil avaṉ uṭampil paratti yar toṭarpāl ēṟpaṭṭa vēṟupāṭukaḷ pulappaṭum; piṟaku vāyilavar vantu camātāṉam kūṟum nili ēṟpaṭum. ātaliṉ vikaṟiyum viṭiyalum marutattiṟkuc ciṟupoḻutāyiṉa.			(to. po. 8 nac.)
iḷampūraṇarum ivvāṟē koḷvar.	(to. po. 9)
vikuṟu viṭiyal eṉṟa toṭari vikuṟuvum viṭiyalum eṉṟu koḷḷāmal, vikaṟiyākiya viṭiyal eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu vikaṟippoḻutē marutattiṟkuriyatu eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar.	(cū. vi. pak. 52)
iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ uriyum (cū.1) nampi akap poruḷum (cū. 12) marutattiṟku vikaṟi eṉṉum ciṟupoḻu toṉṟaṉiyē koḷḷum.
aṭiyārkku nallārum vikaṟiyē koṇṭār.
	(cilap. patikavuri)
ilakkaṇaviḷakkanūlār vikaṟiyum viṭiyalum koṇ ṭār. 	(cū. 385)
maruta naṭiyiyal -
ūṭal eṉṟa oḻukkam paṟṟiya naṭappukkaḷi niralpaṭak kūṟutal.
vīracōḻiyattiṉ uriyil, talivaṉ taliviyoṭu poḻil viḷiyāṭiyum puṉalāṭiyum makiḻtalum, talivaṉ parattiyarmāṭṭup pirintavaḻit talivi ūṭutalum, ūṭal tīrtalum, iraṇṭām maṉiviyiyum kāmakkiḻattiyariyum talivi varavēṟṟu iṉimiyākap paḻakutalum pōṉṟa ceytikaḷ paṟṟiya kiḷavikaḷ palavum ōr āciriyappāvāl tokuttuk kūṟappaṭṭu maruta naṭiyiyal eṉṟa peyariṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.	(vī. cō. 95 uri mēṟ)
marutam -
vayalum vayal cārnta iṭamum ākiya nāṭṭup pakuti.
ippakutiyil maruta maraṅkaḷ palavākak kāṇappaṭutal paṟṟi itaṟku marutam eṉṟa peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉavum, marutam eṉappaṭum talivaṉtaliviyar ūṭal paṟṟiya nikaḻcci mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭutaliṉ innilattiṟku ippeyar ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉavum kūṟuvar.
marutam : nilam, makkaḷ, ūr paṟi, vayal -
	nilam	:	paḻaṉam, tōṭu, paṇi, meṉpāl, kaḻaṉi, paṇṇi (piṅ. 549) akaṇi, pāṉal, vayal, paṇi.		(cūṭā.ni. ē 32)
	makkaḷ 	:	kaḷamar, toḻuvar, maḷḷar, kampaḷar, viṉiñar, uḻavar, kaṭiñar, kiḷiñar; uḻattiyar, kaṭic ciyar, āṟṟukkālāṭṭiyar; ūraṉ, makiḻnaṉ, kiḻavaṉ; maṉivi, kiḻatti, illāḷ, il. (tivā. pak. 41) marutattār, kaḷamar, maḷḷar, viṉiñar, uḻavar, kampaḷar, paḷḷar, kaḷiñar, kaṭiñar. 
	(nā. ni. 159)
	ūr	:	pūkkam, koṭikkāṭu, pūriyam, uṟiyuḷ, pākkam, aruppam, akaluḷ, pati, kōṭṭam, vacati, tāvaḷam, niyamam, vāḻkki, taṇṇaṭi.			(tivā. pak. 110)
paṟi - kiṇi (tivā. pak. 148)
vayal - viḷiyuḷ, paṇṇi, cey, kaḻaṉi, taṭi, pātti, ceṟu.
ciṟuvayal - kuṇṭil, pātti.	(tivā. pak. 119)
maruvutal urittal -
talivi talivaṉōṭu illaṟam naṭattum maṉikkuc ceṉṟu mīṇṭa cevili naṟṟāyiṭam, “talivaṉ aracaṉ kāriyamāka vēṟṟiṭam ceṉṟālum, avviṉi muṭittapiṉ talivaṉ tēr nērāka illiṟku vantutāṉ taṅkumēyaṉṟi veḷiyē evviṭattum taṅkātu. talivi talivaṉiyaṉṟi maṟṟoru teyvatti maṉattālum niṉiyāḷ. iḵtu ivarkaḷ kātal” eṉṟu avviruvar kātalum porunti yirukkum ciṟappiṉik kuṟippiṭutal.
itaṉic ‘cevili poṟṟoṭi kaṟpiyal naṟṟāykku urittal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa (‘ilvāḻkki eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatu)	(na. a. 203)
itu ‘maṇañ ciṟappurittal’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 306)
maruḷuṟṟu urittal -
‘pirivuḻik kalaṅkal’ eṉum kiḷaviyatu oruvaki itu. itaṉkaṇ ‘āya veḷḷam vaḻipaṭak kaṇṭu itu, māyamō eṉṟal’ eṉṟa kiḷavi oṉṟē nikaḻkiṟatu. atu kāṇka.			(na. a. 132)
malivu -
makiḻtal; aḵtāvatu talivaṉum taliviyum kaṟpuk kālattil illaṟoḻukkamum puṇarcciyum mutaliyavaṟṟāl makiḻtal.	(to.cey. 187 pērā.)
“maḻi iṉmiyāl tiṉi uṇaṅkum; viḷiya māṭṭā; puṉam kāppacceṉṟu avari etirppaṭalām eṉṟu eṇṇiyirunta itu kūṭātāyiṟṟu” eṉa veṟuttirunta talivikkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu -
“talivi! talivaṉatu kuṉṟiṉi nōkkik kārmēkam cūḻn tuḷḷatu. virivil maḻi poḻiyalām. nampuṉattut tiṉikkatir vāṭivataṅkum nili irātu. namakkum puṉaṅ kāval mīṇṭum vāykkum” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 207)
maṟṟavaḷ taḷarcci -
tōḻi taliviyiṭam iravukkuṟi nayakkumāṟu kūṟiyavaḻi, talivi iravukkuṟiyiṭattut talivaṉ varum vaḻiyiṭi ētam karuti maṉam taḷartal. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) 			(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
itu ‘nērātu iṟivi neñcoṭu kiḷattal’ eṉavum
paṭum.	(na. a. 158)
maṟṟiyavaḻit talivaṉ kūṟal -
kuṟi etirppaṭṭum kiyuṟi maṟukkappaṭṭum koṭukkap peṟṟum irantu piṉ niṉṟa talivaṉ, aṅṅaṉam kuṟiyiṭat tut taliviyi etirppaṭa iyalātu āṟṟāṉāy, irantu piṉniṟṟali nīkkumiṭattuk kūṟutal. 
“maṉamē! kiḷipōla maḻalimoḻi pēcum civanta vāy, periya kayal pōṉṟa miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷ, mēkam pōla mutukuppuṟam tāḻnta kariya kūntal, miṉṉal pōṉṟa iṭi ivaṟṟiyuṭiya kuṟumakaḷi viṭātu piṉtoṭarum nī, evvi eṉṟa naṇpaṉ nītiyāṉa coṟkaḷi eṭuttuc collavum kēṭkāmal titiyaṉatu kāvalmaramāṉa puṉṉi maratti aḻikkak karuti avaṉoṭu pōriṭṭa aṉṉi eṉpavaṉip pōla iṟantupaṭutalē muṭivāka nikaḻak kūṭiyatu” (akanā. 126) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
neñcē! it taliviyiṉ mārpiṉit taḻuvutal karuti, pāmpu iri tēṭum añcattakka kuṟukiya vaḻiyilē, iraviruḷiyum pārātu taṉittuk kaṇviḻittu naṭantu vantum, taliviyik kāṇap peṟāmiyāl, kāṇpavark kellām perunaki yuṇṭākak kāmam mikutippaṭak kuṟi yāta tuṉpattit tanta nī virivil aḻivāyāka!” 	(akanā. 258) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
maṟala viṉaval -
ceviliyiṉ ceyal kaṇṭu talivi māṟupaṭat tōḻi avaḷi viṉavutal.
kaḷavil talivaṉi nukarntu varum talivi avaṉpirivi niṉittu varuntiyatāl avaṭku ēṟpaṭṭa melivu kuṟittuc cevili kaṭṭuvicciyi viṉavi annōy murukaṉāl ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu avaḷ kūṟiyati nampi murukaṉukku veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttat tuṇintavaḻi, atu kuṟittut talivi maṉam māṟupaṭṭu varuntat tōḻi avaḷi nōkki, “añcattakka kaṭal pōla nam kāmam peruki elli kaṭantuviṭṭa inniliyil, nām itaṉāl vīṇē varunti aḻiyāta vakiyil oruvāṟṟāṉ cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟpatil taṭi yātu uḷḷatu?” eṉṟu viṉavutal. 
itu kaṟpiṉic cārnta ‘aṟattoṭu nili’ eṉum kiḷavik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 38)
maṟavāmi kūṟal -
kaṟpuk kālattil vēntaṟkuṟṟuḻip pirintu mīṇṭu vanta talivaṉ taliviyoṭu paḷḷiyiṭattaṉāka, appoḻutu, “viṉiyiṭattu emmi maṟantīrē!” eṉṟa tōḻikku,  “yāṉ pācaṟikkaṇ taṅkiyiṭattum, kaṇkaḷil kaṇṇīr tuḷikka niṉṟu ivaḷ eṉṉuṭiya neñciṉi viṭṭu nīṅkavilli; ātaliṉ yāṉ maṟakkumāṟu eṉṉō?” eṉat tāṉ taliviyi maṟavāmiyi avaṉ kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘vēntaṟkuṟṟuḻip pirivu’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 331)
maṟuttu etirkōṭal -
talivi nāṇatti akaṟṟit talivaṉi ēṟṟukkoḷ ḷutal.
itu ‘taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
itu tirukkōviyāril ‘nāṇ viṭa varuntal’ (44 eṉappaṭum.	(na. a. 127)
maṟuttuk kūṟal -
kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉ vēntaṟ kuṟṟuḻip piriya, avaṉ mīṇṭu varum muṉṉark kār kālam varavē, atu kuṟittut talivi āṟṟāḷākat tōḻi atu kārpparuvamaṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟa, kāntaḷē yaṉṟit tōṉṟiyum malarntuḷḷatik kāṭṭi atu kārkālamē eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam maṟuttuk kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘vēntaṟ kuṟṟuḻip pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 325)
maṟi -
piṟarāl aṟippaṭāta poruḷuṭiya vētam maṟi eṉappaṭṭatu pōla, piṟarāl aṟiyappaṭāta talivaṉ taliviyariṉ kaḷavukkūṭṭam ‘maṟi’ eṉappaṭṭatu. ‘maṟialar āki maṉṟat taḵtē’ (kuṟun. 97) eṉak kaḷa voḻukkam ‘maṟi’ eṉappaṭṭavāṟu.	(to. po. 92 nac)
maṟittami kūṟi nakitturittal -
talivaṉ koṇarnta kiyuṟiyit tōḻi ēlāmal pala vakik kāraṇaṅkaḷip paṭittu moḻiyavē, talivaṉ, avaḷ kālam tāḻppataṉip poṟātu, avaḷi iṉṟiyē taliviyip peṟak kūṭiya vāyppup paṟṟic cintikkat toṭaṅka, appoḻutu tōḻi avaṉiṭam, “nī eṉṉi maṟitta kāriyam iṉi niṉakku eṉṉiyiṉṟi naṭavātu” eṉṟu nakiyāṭik kūṟutal.
itaṉip ‘pāṅki eṉṉi maṟittapiṉ eḷiteṉa nakutal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.	(na.a. 106)
itu ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
	(kōvi. 105)
maṟinta oḻukkam -
taliviyiṉ peṟṟōr avaḷit talivaṟku muṟiyāka maṇam ceytuvippataṉ muṉṉar, talivaṉ viti kūṭṭi vikkat tōḻiyiṉ utaviyi mēṟkoṇṭu, taliviyoṭu naṭattum kaḷavoḻukkam.	(to. po. 135 nac.)
maṟintu taliviyik kaṇṭu niṉṟa talivaṉ avaḷoṭu	nikaḻntatu niṉii neñciṟkuc collutal (allatu) iyaṟkip	puṇarcci puṇarntu nīṅkiya talivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc collutal-
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkup piṉ āyattoṭu pōkiṉṟa taliviyit tāṉ maṟintu kaṇṭa talivaṉ, ataṟku muṉ nikaḻntaṉavaṟṟi yellām uṭkoṇṭu makiḻcciyuṭaṉ taṉ maṉatti nōkki, “neñcē! yāṉ taṉṉik kāṇun tōṟum taṉakku ēṟpaṭum makiḻcciyip peritāka veḷik kāṭṭātu, taṉ neṟṟiyil viyarvi tōṉṟa nāṇattāl talikaviḻntu, taṉakku vanta puṉcirippi veḷippaṭuttātu, mukattiṉ polivukkuḷḷēyē maṟittukkoṇṭa taliviyiṉ maṉam eṉṉi nōkkic citinta taṉmiyi nī uḷḷapaṭi aṟintāyalli. ataṉi yāṉē uṇmiyāka aṟintu iyaṟkip puṇarcciyi mēṟkoṇṭēṉ” eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.	(to. po. 101 nac.)
maṟiyōr āṟu -
kantiruva vaḻakkam; kantiruvarukku maṟiyōr ōtiya neṟi atuvākaliṉ ‘ maṟiyōr āṟu’ eṉṟār. eṉavē, pāṅka ṉum tōḻiyum uṇarntuḻiyum kaḷavu eṉṟal maṟiyōr vaḻakku.	(to.cey. 186 nac.)
`maṟiyōr tēettu maṉṟal eṭṭu' -
aṟanili, oppu, poruḷkōḷ, teyvam, yāḻōr kūṭṭam, arumporuḷ viṉi, irākkatam, pēynili eṉa maṟiyuḷ kūṟappaṭṭa maṉṟal eṭṭām. avi piramam, pirācāpattiyam, āriṭam, teyvam, kāntaruvam, acuram, irākkatam, picācam eṉavum vaḻaṅkappeṟum.
maṟiyil kūṟappaṭum maṉṟal eṉṉātu maṟiyōrāl koḷḷappaṭum maṉṟal ivi eṉpatu. ikkālattu im maṇaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippu vētattuḷ kālappaṭṭilatu; taruma cāttiraṅkaḷilum maṉusmirutiyilumē uḷḷatu.			(iṟi. a. 1; to. po. 92 nac.)
kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik koṭikkuri marapiṉōr karaṇamoṭu puṇarak koṭuppavē varintu koḷvatu tirumaṇa maṉṟal.
kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik koṭuppōriṉṟik karaṇamoṭu puṇarat taṉmaṉi nōkkik kaḻintaviṭattu iṭiccurattuk kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavviyāṉ nikaḻvatu kaṭimaṇamaṉṟal.
pālatu āṇiyāṉ nikaḻvatu teyvamaṇamaṉṟal. maṟṟa tuṇikkāraṇamum nimitta kāraṇamumiṉṟi ūḻākiya mutaṟkāraṇam paṟṟiyē nikaḻumitu yāḻōr kūṭṭam eṉac ciṟappikkappaṭṭatu.
kulavaḻakkum kuṭimarapum paṟṟip piṟappurimiyāṉ varintu koḷvatu muṟimaṇamaṉṟal.
ēṟu taḻuval mutalāya vīṟupaṟṟik kiḻattiyip paricākap peṟṟu varivatu  arumaṇamaṉṟal.
puṟattiṇi oḻukalāṟṟiṉkaṇ pakivari veṉṟu avartam makaḷiri urimi pūṇṭu variyumatu perumaṇamaṉṟal.
aṭiyōr pāṅkiṉum viṉivalar pāṅkiṉum karaṇamiṉṟi nikaḻvatu ciṟumaṇamaṉṟal.
ivaṟṟi muṟiyē kaṟ. 1, kaṟ. 2, kaḷavu. 50, kaḷa-1, kaṟ. 32, mullikkali - 4, puṟat. 19, mullikkali. 12 ivaṟṟuḷ kāṇka.
	(to. kaḷa. 1 ca. pāla.)
maṉṟal eṭṭu -
aṟanili, oppu, poruḷkōḷ, teyvam, yāḻōr kūṭṭam arumporuḷviṉi, valinili, pēynili eṉpaṉa.	(iṟi. a. uri (1)
ivaṟṟi muṟiyē pira(hm)mam, pirācāpattiyam, āriṭam teyvam, kāntaruvam, ācuram, irākkatam, picācam, eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar.	(to. po. 92 nac.)
maṉṟal maṉivaru cevilikku ikuḷi aṉpuṟavu uṇarttal -
‘maṇaviṉikku vanta cevilikku...........’ kūṟutal kāṇka.
	(na. a. 203)
maṉṉar pāṅkiṉ piṉṉōr -
aracariccārntu vāḻum iyalpiṉiyuṭiya uḻuvit tuṇṇum vēḷāḷar. taṉṉic cārntu vāḻum avarkaḷi aracaṉ pakivarmēlum, nāṭu kāttalmēlum cantu ceyvittalmēlum, poruḷvaruvāymēlum ēvuvāṉ.
avarkaḷ maṇṭilaṅkaḷi āḷvōrum tāṉit talivaru māy, cōḻanāṭṭup piṭavūrum aḻuntūrum nāṅkūrum nāvūrum ālañcēriyum peruñcikkalum vallamum kiḻārum mutaliya pakutikaḷil tōṉṟi, vēḷ eṉavum aracu eṉavum ciṟappup paṭṭam pūṇṭōrum kuṟunila maṉṉar kuṭikaḷil piṟantōrum ākiya palarāy, muṭiyuṭi maṉṉaṉukkut tam makaḷi maṇamceytu koṭuttaṟkut takutiyuṭiya vēḷāḷarkaḷ.	(to. po. 30 nac.)
maṉṉaṉi niṉintu miṉṉiṭi melital -
talivi talivaṉi niṉintu ēṅkutal.
“uṉṉi viṭṭup piriyēṉ” eṉṟu collip pōṉa eṉ uyir pōṉṟa kātalaṉ, eṉṉik kūṭi iṉpuṟa viḻintu mīṇṭum ivviṭam varuvāṉō?” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘iṭantalip pāṭu’ eṉum tokutikaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 37; i.vi. 503 uri)
maṉṉiya vakuttal -
talivaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattut taliviyik kāṇac ceṉṟa pāṅkaṉ avaḷik kaṇṭu talivaṉiṭam nilipeṟṟa āṟṟali viyantu kūṟal.
itu ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi. 16)
maṉṉiya viṉi -
talivaṉum taliviyum, niliyāk kāmap poyyoḻuk katti vilakki, eṉṟum kuṉṟātu cantittu iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻttik kaḷavil toṭarum kātaṟ kūṭṭam, kantaruvam pōla nērntavaḻip puṇarntu tīrntavaḻi maṟakkum tiṟattatu aṉṟām. “iruvayiṉ ottup piriyātu kūṭi vāḻtal, aṉṟēl tariyātu iṟantu muṭital” eṉum tuṇi vuṭiyār irupālavarum ākiya oruvari oruvar iṉṟi yamiyāk kātaluṭiyārkkē uriyatātaliṉ, attalivaṉ taliviyaratu kātaṟ kaḷavoḻukkam ‘maṉṉiya viṉi’ eṉap paṭṭatu. 	
aḵtāvatu maṉpati maṟiyātu nilipeṟutaṟkuk kāraṇa māṉa kāma oḻukkam.	(to. meyp. 19 pārati)
maṉattoṭu nērtal -
kaḷavukkālattil tōḻiyik kuṟinayanta talivaṉ āṟṟāmiyāl maṭalēṟat tuṇinta ceytiyit tōḻi vaṉmoḻiyākak kūṟak kēṭṭa talivi, ataṟkuttāṉ āṟṟāḷāyt talivaṉikkāṇa vēṇṭit taṉ maṉat toṭu kūṟit talivaṉuṭiya kuṟi nayappi ēṟṟatu. 
itu ‘kuṟi nayappuk kūṟal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 89)
maṉikkaṇ ceṉṟa talivaṉ avvaḻip piriyātu uṟital kēṭṭa paratti kūṟiyatu -
“tōḻi! keṇṭimīṉ orumuṟi pāynta tuṇiyāṉē āmpalitaḻ aviḻntu malarum nāṭaṉākiya nam talivaṉ, ētō orumuṟi etirpārāta vakiyil taṉ maṉikkaṇ cella, avaṉ varukiyāl talivi makiḻntati  nili yāṉatoru ceyal eṉṟu niṉittu, uṇmi aṟiyāta varkaḷ, nām aḻumpaṭi nammi nīttut talivaṉ talivi yillattilēyē niliyākat taṅkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭāṉ eṉṟa kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷatu maṭamiyi eṉeṉpatu?” eṉṟa paratti kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 40)
maṉiccel parattiyi makiḻntu etirkōṭal -
taṉ illattiṟku varum talivaṉuṭiya kāmakkiḻattiyākiya parattiyit talivi makiḻvoṭu varavēṟṟal. (maruta naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 95 uri mēṟ.)
maṉipukal kaṇṭa vāyilavar kūṟal -
kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu paratti illattāṉāka, appoḻutu talivi viṭutta cevvaṇi kaṇṭu, parattiyillam nīttut talivimaṉikkaṇ avaṉ pukavē, “muṉpellām iravum pakalum vāyil peṟātu niṉṟu vāṭum ikkāvaliyuṭiya vāyili vāyilē iṉṟit talivaṉ ivvaḷavu eḷimiyāka aṭintu viṭṭāṉ. kaṟpukkaṭaṉ pūṇṭa taliviyaruḷ tam kaṭamiyi niṉittu aṭaṅkātavar oruvarum ilar!” eṉat taliviyiṉ vāyilōr tammuḷ kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 362)
maṉimaruṭci -
talivaṉ taliviyi uṭaṉpōkkil aḻittuc cella, avvuṭaṉpōkkiṉi aṟinta naṟṟāy maṉamakiḻvōṭu aḵtu aṟam eṉṟu karutiyum, taṉiyē taṉ makaḷ koṭiya pālivaḻiyil ceṉṟatu kaṇṭu āṟṟāḷāyt taṉ makaḷuṭiya kaṇkaḷ pāliyiṉ veppattāl civantu oḷi maḻuṅkic cuḻalumē eṉṟum,
taliviyiṉ āyamum payiliṭamum kiḷi mutaliyaṉavum kaṇṭu tuyaram tāṅkātu, “eṉ makaḷ kuṟṟa maṟṟavaḷ; avaḷ koṇṭa kāmam viyappuṭiyatu; avaḷ kāraṇamāka muṉṉamēyē ayalār kūṟum paḻimoḻiyi aṟintiruntum aṟiyātavaḷip pōla iruntoḻinta yāṉē vaḻuvuṭi yēṉ” eṉṟum varuntiyum maṉiyiṉkaṇ iruntu maruṇṭu kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.	(naṟ. 66, 143)
maṉiyavar makiḻtal -
kaṟpukkālattil parattiyiṟ pirinta talivaṉ mīṇṭu illam vara, avaṉukkākat tōḻi taliviyiṭam vāyil vēṇṭa, talivi makiḻcciyuṟutalukku māṟākak kaṭuñciṉaṅ koḷḷa, avaḷ kuvaḷi pōṉṟa kaṇkaḷ vekuḷiyāl ceṅkaḻunīr malarkaḷ pōlac civakka, atu kaṇṭu maṉiyavar, “ittalivi yiṉ vekuṭci talivaṉuṭiya puṟattoḻukkatti iṉi nīkkum” eṉat tammuḷ makiḻntu kūṟuvatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 384)
`maṉivi uyarvum kiḻavōṉ paṇivum' -
kāmam talivaṟku mikuntiruppat talivi avaṉiṭam appōtu ūṭal koṇṭavaḻiyum, pulavik kālattum, taliviyiṉ  uyarvum talivaṉ avaḷip paṇintu avaḷ kūṭṭattiṟku uṭaṉpaṭutali vēṇṭi ēṅki niṟṟalum nikaḻum.	(to. po. 227 nac.)
maṉivi pōla allavi moḻital -
pāṅki mati yuṭaṉpāṭṭiṉkaṇ talivaṉ taṉakkut talivi urimiyuṭiyavaḷ pōlap putitāka vantavar kūṟattakāta coṟkaḷit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟi uriyāṭutal. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal).	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
maṉiviyum tāṉum vaṉamaṭintu nōṟṟal -
kaḷavu oḻukit taliviyi varintu kaṟpukkaṭam pūṇum illaṟattip pallāṇṭu nallaṟamāka nikaḻtti naṉmak kaḷip payantu avarkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭuttapiṉ aintiṇi oḻukkamākiya kāmiṉpattil paṟṟaṟṟut taliviyum talivaṉum oru kāṭṭiṉi aṭintu tammuyirkku uṟuti tarum nalla ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭṭut tavamceytu vīṭu peṟa muyalutal, aintiṇik kāmattukku appāṟpaṭṭa uyirk kāmam ātaliṉ aḵtu aintiṇikaḷuḷ aṭaṅkātu, uṭaṟkāmatti veṟuttamiyāl poruntāk kāmam eṉṟataṉ kūṟṟākiya peruntiṇit tuṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟākum eṉṟu oru cār āciriyar kūṟuvar. ituvum akapporuṭ peruntiṇi.	(na. a. 243)
`mātarik koṇṭu tam vāḻpatip pukutal' -
uṭaṉpōyt talivaṉuṭaṉ illaṟam naṭatti naṉmakap peṟṟa piṉ ceyti colli yaṉuppiya taliviyit talivi tamar ceṉṟu tammūrkku aḻittu varutal (pāli naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 93 uri mēṟ.)
māyappuṇarcci -
‘kaḷaveṉap paṭuvatu.............. māyap puṇarcci eṉmaṉār pulavar’; kāmaṉ pōṉṟa talivaṉ, aḻakiya maṭa nallāḷoṭu vēṟu maliccāral mutaliya maṟivāṉa iṭameyti, āyamum tōḻiyum poruntiyaṟiyātavāṟāka, uḷḷamum meyyum uṟumāṟu kaḷaviṉāl kūṭum kūṭṭam.			(to. po. 92 nac. uri)
māyavaṉ piṉ ceṉṟa makaḷit tāy niṉintu pulampal -
“tirumakaḷip pōlap pēṇi nāṉ vaḷartta eṉ orē oru nalla makaḷit tirumāl uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōyviṭṭāṉ! eṉ illamē veṟumeṉak kiṭakkiṟatu. yacōti eṉ makaḷākiya taṉ marumakaḷi aṉpoṭu naṭattuvāḷō? oṉṟum aṟiyēṉāyuḷḷēṉ” eṉṟa tāy kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(periyāḻ. 3 : 8 : 1, 4)
māyaṉmēl makaḷ māluṟukōlam tāy kūṟal -
oru tāy taṉ makaḷ kaṇṇaṉmītu koṇṭa kātalāl paṭum pāṭṭiṉi eṭutturittal.
“iṉṉum peṇmi muḻumalarcciyum peṟāta eṉpētip peṇ taṉ uṭampil puḻuti paṭiya, kaṇṇaṉaḻakil īṭupaṭṭu avaṉpāl kātalum mikakkoṇṭu, taṉ viḷi yāṭṭukkaḷilum avaṉpeyarik kūvi kūvik kalaṅkuki ṟāḷ; eṉ kaṭṭuppāṭṭiyum mīṟik kaṇṇaṉik kāṇap puṟappaṭukiṟāḷ” eṉṟa kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(periyāḻ. 3 : 7)
māyōṉ -
karuniṟak kaṭavuḷām tirumālākiya mullinilak kaṭavuḷ. kāṭum kāṭu cārnta iṭamum ākiya mullinilattē vāḻum iṭiyarkaḷ, palavakip pacukkaḷum naṉku pālcurattaṟkākat “tirumāl, pālum neyyum payakkum pacukkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum kāppāṉāka!” eṉṟu kuravik kūttu nikaḻttip palavaki yuṇavukaḷi māyōṉukkup paṭittaliṉ, mullinila makkaḷukku apperumāṉatu aruḷ veḷippaṭum.
māyōṉ makaṉākiya kāmaṉum annilak kaṭavuḷākak koḷḷappaṭum.
tirumāl aruḷāl pacuvum erumiyum āṭum vaḷamāka vāḻum eṉpatu. (im mūṉṟiṉattiyum kuṟikkum col ‘ā’ eṉpatu.)	(to. po. 5 nac.)
māyōṉukkuk kāṭu urimiyātal -
mullinilamākiya kāṭṭup pakutiyil āṭumāṭu mēykkum  āyarkaḷ pacukkaḷ palavum pālvaḷam curattaṟku, “ākuti payakkum āpala kākka!” eṉṟu kuravik kūttāṭit tirumā lukku nivētaṉam koṭuttaliṉ, kāṭṭunilamākiya mulli nilamakkaḷukkut tirumāl aruḷceyvāṉ eṉpatu.			(to. po. 5 nac.)
māviratiyari viṉātal -
uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa taliviyi mīṭṭuvarap pālip pakutikkuc ceṉṟa cevili vaḻiyil civavēṭattuṭaṉ varuvōri viṉavutal.
“tūymiyāṉa tirunīṟṟup piyiṉiyum, elumpuaṇiyi yum, caṭiyiṉiyum, veṇṇīṟupūciya mēṉiyiyum uṭiya cāṉṟōrkaḷē! oru peṇ taṉ piṉṉē varat taliva ṉoruvaṉ muṉṉē cellutalik kaṇṭīrō?” (kōvi. 242) eṉṟāṟpōlac cevili curattiṭi viṉavutal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘viratiyari viṉāvutal’ 
eṉṉum (242)
itu variviyalil, ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu!’	(i. vi. 538)
`māṟil āṇmiyil colliya moḻi' -
oruvaṉic ‘ciṅkam vantatu’ eṉṟāṟpōlak kūṟuvatu.
	(to. po. 240 iḷa.)
talivaṉuṭiya, kuṟicollamuṭiyāta āṇmit taṉmiyiṭattē paḻiyuṇṭākiviṭṭatu eṉa,
‘ituvum ōr ūrāṇmik(ku) otta paṭiṟuṭittu’ (kali. 89) “ūriyē āḷum taṉmi eṉṟa pōrviyil nikaḻttum vañcakac ceyal iḵtu” eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟuvatu.
	(to. po. 244 nac.)
māṟukōḷillāk kuṟippu -
taliviyiṉ uṭal vēṟupāṭum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭum kaṇṭa tāy periyōri viṉava, avarkaḷ atu teyvak kuṟṟamāka irukkalām eṉṟu kūṟa, akkuṟṟam pōkkum kaḻuvāyāka murukaṉukku veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttuvataṟkāka vēlaṉi aḻikka, avaṉ veṟiyāṭalit toṭaṅku miṭattē tōḻi avaṉiṭam, “nī iṭum ippalikaḷi eṉtalivik kuriya talivaṉ ēṟṟukkoḷvāṉāyiṉ takkatu“(kuṟun. 362) eṉṟum, āvēcittu vanta murukaṉākiya teyvam kēṭpa, “murukaṉē! ittaliviyiṉatu nōy talivaṉatu mārpi ṉāl vantatu eṉpati aṟintum, vēlaṉit tiruptippaṭutta veṟiyāṭṭiṭattukku vanta nī orutaliyāka aṟiyāmi yuṭiyāy!” (naṟ. 34) eṉṟum kūṟak kēṭṭa tāy, tōḻi taliviyiṉ nōykkāraṇattik kuṟippāl aṟivittali uṇarntu, tōḻiyi viṉava, tōḻi muṟippaṭi aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ. tōḻi tāṉ aṟattoṭu niṟpataṟkut tāy taṉṉi viṉavumāṟu mutaṟkaṇ kūṟiya kuṟippumoḻi māṟu kōḷillāk kuṟippākum.	(iṟi. a. 14)
māṟukōḷillā moḻi -
māṟupāṭillāta coṟkaḷ.
tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟkumpōtu, tāy aṟiviṉoṭum talimakaṉ perumiyoṭum talimakaḷ kaṟpiṉoṭum taṉatu kāvaloṭum nāṇiṉoṭum ulakiyaloṭum māṟukōḷ illāta coṟkaḷic colli aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ eṉpatu.
taliviyiṉ uṭal vēṟupāṭu teyvattāṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟu karutit tāy veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttat toṭaṅka, atu kaṇṭa tōḻi tāykkuk kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ.
“tāyē! yāṅkaḷ pētiyarāyirunta kālattu oru nāḷ eṅkaḷukku aḻaku ceytu eṅkaḷi viḷiyāṭi varumāṟu colli veḷiyē aṉuppiṉāy. appoḻutu iḷiñaṉ oruvaṉ iraṇṭu cuṉikkuvaḷippūkkaḷik koṇṭu avvaḻiyē vantāṉ. avaṉi nōkkit talivi ap pūkkaḷit taṉpāvikku aṇiyumāṟu taravēṇṭa, avaṉum piṟitu cintiyātu koṭuttu nīṅkiṉāṉ. aṉṟu nikaḻnta ceytiyi iṉṟu maṉaṅkoṇṭu taṉakkup pūk koṭutta vaṉit tavira vēṟoruttaṉit tāṉ maṇantāl taṉ kuṭumpattukku mācu nikaḻntuviṭumē eṉṟu niṉittut talivi maṉamum uṭalum vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷāḷ” eṉpāḷ.
viḷiyāṭi varumāṟu iruvariyum tāy aṉuppiyataṉāl, itu tāy aṟiviṉoṭu māṟukoḷḷātu.
akkālattu nikaḻntataṉi ikkālattum niṉintamiyiṉ talivi perumiyoṭu māṟukoḷḷātu;
pūkkoṭuttavaṉit tavira maṟṟoruvaṉi maṇattal kuṭumpattukku mācu eṉṟu talivi karutukiṉṟamiyiṉ, avaḷ kaṟpiṉoṭu māṟu koḷḷātu;
iruvarum irunta nilimikkaṇ talivi pūkkoṇṭāḷ eṉṟamiyiṉ tōḻi kāvaloṭu māṟukoḷḷātu;
itu pētipparuvattu nikaḻntatu eṉṟamiyiṉ, talivi nāṇattoṭu māṟukoḷḷātu;
‘uṟṟārkku uriyār poṟṟoṭi makaḷir’ eṉavum, ‘koṭut tārkku uriyār koṇṭār’ eṉavum talivi karututaliṉ, iḵtu ulakiyaloṭu māṟukoḷḷātatāyiṟṟu.			(iṟi. a. 14 uri)
māṉiṭa makaḷir nayanta kaṭavuṭ pakkam -
innilavulakattu makaḷir tēvarkaḷik kātalittuk kūṟiya ceyti.
aḵtāvatu  “karuvari paṭarnta eṉ kaṇkaḷ civappēṟa irāp poḻutil eṉmārpil tāṉ aṇinta pūṇūliṉatu vaṭup pulaṉākumāṟu taḻuvi eṉ neñcattiṉ aḻakiṉic ciṟitum oḻiyāmal nukarnta iṟivaṉuṭiya ūr pācūrām” eṉṟal pōṉṟu, talivi tāṉ maṉattāṉ iṟivaṉik kūṭiya iṉpanukarcciyi eṭuttiyampum pāṭāṇtuṟi.
itu ‘pūtalattu ariviyar poruvil vāṉavarik, kātaliṉ nayanta kaṭavuṭ pakkam’ eṉṟu ilakkaṇaviḷakkattuḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.	(i. vi. 617 - 47)
`mikka kāmattu miṭaṟ'kaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu -
kari kaṭanta kāmattāl taṉṉi virumpātavaḷi valiyap paṟṟik kūṭumiṭattē talivaṉ kūṟutal. itu perumpālum talimakkaḷiṭattē nikaḻatu; oruvariṭam paṇiyāṟṟi ūtiyam peṟṟu vāḻum aṭiyavarkaḷiṭamē nikaḻum.
aṭiyavaṉoruvaṉ aṭittiyoruttiyi valiyap paṟṟiya pōtu avarkaḷiṭiyē kīḻkkāṇum uriyāṭal nikaḻ kiṟatu.
“ē! ivaṉukku nāṇam eṉpatē illiyē. taṉṉik kūṭa virumpātavariyum puṇarccivēṭkiyāl valitiṟ paṟṟi yiḻukkiṉṟāṉē!“
“pūṅkottukkaḷ mikka koṭi pōlvāy! uṉakku eṉ ṉoṭu kūṭutaṟkuriya uṇarcci poruntiyuḷḷa teṉṟō, poruntavilliyeṉṟō nītāṉ aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇ ṭumēyaṉṟi yāṉ aṟiyamāṭṭēṉ. uṉṉit taḻuvutal eṉakku iṉitāy iruntatu; ātaliṉ taḻuviṉēṉ!“
“tōḻa! taṉakku iṉitāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu piṟarukkut tīṅku ceytal nalla ceyalākumā?“
“peṇṇē! uṉatu aṟivuṭiya viṉāviṉi maṟantu yāṉ kūṟuvataṉik kēḷ : nīr vēṭkiyuṟṟavarkaḷ tamakku nīr kuṭittal iṉitāyirukkiṉṟatu eṉṟu karuti nīr parukukiṉ ṟārkaḷā? allatu, tām parukuvatu nīrukku iṉitāyirukkum eṉṟa karutip parukukiṉṟārkaḷā?” 
ivvāṟu viṉavi avaḷit taṉ vayamākkiṉāṉ 
avaṉ 	(kali. 62) 
`mikka kāmattu miṭaṟ'kaṇ piṟar kūṟuvatu -
kaḻikāmattāl, taṉṉi virumpātavaḷi valiyak kūṭum talivaṉik kaṇṭār kūṟuvatu.	(to. po. 51 nac.)
irācamāpurattil kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉ āṭci ceytapōtu, vēttiyal nāṭakaaraṅkil nāṭṭiyam āṭuvataṟkuc cīvaka ṉāl naṉku alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa aṉaṅkamāli eṉṟa melliyalnallāḷ nāṭṭiyamāṭi muṭinta piṉṉar, avaḷ cīvakaṉiṭattuk kātal koṇṭiruppataṉi aṟinta kaṭṭiyaṅ kāraṉ avaḷi valitiṉ kippaṟṟi, “tēṉ uṭintu oḻukum aḻakiya tāmarippūviṭattē, piṇattil amarum kākki, vantu ataṉ itaḻkaḷ oṭiya ataṉic citittāṟ pōlat taḻuvi avaḷ mulliyiṉ tātu utirumāṟu ataṉip paṟittiṭṭāṉ (mulli - kaṟpu) eṉṟa cīvakacintāmaṇip pāṭal (686) mikka kāmattu miṭalpaṟṟip piṟar kūṟum kūṟṟāka amivatu.
mikka poruḷ -
aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉpaṉavaṟṟil talivikku nāṇam aḻiyāta vakiyil nalvaḻikkaṇ amiyum ceytikaḷ.
	(to. po. 215 iḷa.)
akapporuḷukku, aṟattiṉ māṟupaṭṭatēṉum payaṉpaṭa varum mēmpaṭṭa ceyti.	(219 nac.)
mikkōr ētukkāṭṭal -
curattiṭi uṭaṉpōkkil ceṉṟa talivaṉiyum talivi yiyum kaṇṭatuṇṭō eṉṟa viṉaviya cevilikku, muk kōṟpakavar kāraṇam kāṭṭi avaḷit teruṭṭutal.
“tāyē! otta kātaliyuṭiya talivaṉum taliviyum avvāṟu celvatum piṉ varintu koḷvatum ulakiyalē kāṇ. cantaṉamum muttum caṅkum tām piṟanta iṭattil payaṉpaṭātu, virumpiyavariṭattēyē ceṉṟu avaruṭaṟku aḻaku tarum. nī ataṟkāka varuntutal vēṇṭā” eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 248)
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘ulakiyalpu urittal’ eṉṉum (248).	(na. a. 188)
mikutturittu ūṭal -
kaṟpukkālattil parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉiṭam kalavi karutip pulanta talivi, “nīr viḻumiya nāṭṭil viḻumiya kuṭiyiluḷḷīr! em pōlvāriṭattu ivvāṟu kūṭa virumputal numakku viḻumiyataṉṟu” eṉṟa talivaṉi uyarttik kūṟi ūṭutal.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 393)
mikutiyiṟ koṇṭal -
koṇṭal - kōṭal. iravukkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ, talivi nāṭṭavar perumpālum aṇiyum āṭi, cūṭumpū, pūcum cantaṉam ivaṟṟit tāṉ aṇintukoṇṭu taliviyik kāṇa varutal (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) (koṇṭal : pu.ve.mā. 179)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ)
`miṉṉiṭi amaḷiyil venniṭa melital' -
talivi paṭukkiyil tirumpip paṭuttatu kaṇṭu talivaṉ varuntutal.
parattiyiṟ pirintu mīṇṭa talivaṉ ceyal kaṇṭu ūṭal koṇṭa talivi, viruntiṉar varavē taṉ ūṭali veḷik kāṭṭātu viruntiṉari upacarittu viṭutta piṉṉar, pāya lil talivaṉi nērmukamākak kāṇa virumpātu avaṉ mukattetir taṉmutukup puṟam kāṭṭit tirumpip paṭuttuk koṇṭāḷāka, talivaṉ, “pāṟkaṭal kaṭintapōtu viṭam muṉṉiṭa amutam piṉṉiṭṭatu pōla, iṉṟu paṭukkikkaṇ talivikūntal muṉṉiṭṭut tōṉṟa, avaḷ itaḻ mutaliyaṉa piṉṉiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉavē!” (tiruppatik. 490) eṉṟu kūṟi varuntutal.
iḵtu ‘uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(mā. aka. 103)
miṉṉiṭi varuttam pāṅki viṉātal -
taliviyatu varuttattiṉ kāraṇattit tōḻi viṉāvutal. 
pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭukaḷāl talivaṉik kāṇum poḻutiṉum kāṇāppoḻutu mikutiyāyiruttaliṉ, avaṉi maṇantu eṉṟum piriyāta kaṟpaṟavāḻvu vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṟa vēṭkiyāl talivi varunti niṟka, avaḷik kaṇṭa tōḻi, “niṉuṭal vāṭiyuḷḷatē. mukam kaṉṟiyuḷḷatē. niṉmaṉattilum cōrvuṇṭu pōlum. itaṉ kāraṇam eṉṉa? pūppaṟitta kaḷippā? cuṉiyil neṭu nēram kuḷittataṉ viḷivā?” (tiruppatik. 255) eṉṟāṟ pōla viṉavutal.
itu ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. aka. 56)
mīṭci -
kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭiṟkuriya kiḷavi mūṉṟaṉuḷ iṟutik kaṇṇatu; ikkiḷaviyiṉ vaki nāṉkāvaṉa :
1.	teḷittal - (a) cevili naṟṟāykku makaḷ neṭuntūram ceṉṟuviṭṭatik kūṟit teḷivittalum (ā) talivaṉ mīṇṭu vantu taliviyiṉ ūr vantuviṭṭatic collit teḷivittalum ām.
2.	makiḻcci - (a) vaḻiyil talivikku muṉṉē pōvōr ceṉṟu pāṅkiyiṭam talivi varuvatik kūṟa, ataṉāl pāṅkiyar makiḻtalum (ā) pāṅkiyar ceṉṟu naṟṟāykkuk kūṟa avaḷ makiḻtalum ām.
3.	viḷittal - “mīḷum talivaṉ, nammakaḷi nam illattukku aḻittu varuvāṉā allatu taṉmaṉikkē. aḻittup pōvāṉā” eṉa naṟṟāy vēlaṉik kuṟikēṭṭal.
4.	ceppal - muṉ ceṉṟōr taliviyiṉ varukiyik kūṟutal.	(na. a. 190)
`mīṇṭavaḷ, celvaṉum celviyum viyaṉakar āṇṭamin taṉareṉa ārāyn turittal' -
cevili, talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ avaṉuṭiya ūrukkuc ceṉṟuviṭṭa ceytiyi naṟṟāykkuk kūṟal.
talivaṉuṭaṉ uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa taliviyit tēṭik kāṇātu maṉi tirumpiya cevili naṟṟāyiṭam, “nammakaḷum akkatir vēlavaṉum pāliyik kaṭantu (avaṉ) uṟaviṉar ūriṉi aṭintuviṭṭaṉar” (tiruppatik. 437) eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu ‘cevili piṉ tēṭicceṉṟu mīṭal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. aka. 88)
mīṇṭu varuṅkālit talivaṉ pākaṉoṭu collal -
poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ tāṉ ceṉṟa kāriyam muṭiyappeṟṟu mīṇṭu varumpōtu tērppāka ṉiṭam kūṟutal.
“pāka! ik kārkālattiṉ mālippōtiṉik kaṇṭu talivi eṉ pirivit tāṅka muṭiyāmal tuyaruṟuvāḷ. virivil tēriṉic celuttuka” (aiṅ 486) eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 170)
mīṇṭu varuṅkāli talivaṉ mēkattoṭu collal -
variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟu viṉimuṭittu mīṇṭu varumpōtu, vāṉil tiraṇṭeḻunta kārmēkaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu taṉ āṟṟāmiyāl talivaṉ avaṟṟuṭaṉ kūṟuvāṉ pōlak kūṟutal.
” mēkaṅkaḷē! eṉṉik kāṇāmal, kārpparuvamum kaṭuki vantati āṟṟāmal, mēṉiyellām pacali paravattuyaruṟṟu vāṭum eṉ talivikku eṉ varukiyi aṟiviyuṅkaḷ” (tañci. kō. 275) eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘variviṭivittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 170)
mīḷa vurittal -
etirvaruvōriṭam cevili uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttiya taṉmakaḷiyum avaḷ kātalaṉiyum paṟṟi viṉava, avarkaḷ, “avviruvarum tuṉpamiṉṟit tam ūr cērntiruppar. iṉic celvatu takkatu aṉṟu;  niṉ ūr nōkki mīḷka” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itaṉik ‘kalantuṭaṉ varuvōrk kaṇṭu kēṭṭal’(na. a. 188) eṉṟu kūṟupa.
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 247)
mukam kaṇṭu makiḻtal -
iravukkuṟi yiṟutiyil taliviyiṉ mukamalarcci kaṇṭa talivaṉ tāṉum taliviyum cantiraṉum kumutamum pōlum iyalpiṉarāka vuḷḷamiyip pukaḻntu makiḻtal.
itaṉi ēṉōr ‘pukaḻtal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭakkupa.
	(na. a. 158)
iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
	(kōvi. 166)
mukampuku kiḷavi (1) -
pāṅkiyum taliviyum cērntiruntapōtu poḻiliṭic ceṉṟa talivaṉ avarkaḷoṭu vāyppuk karuti uri yāṭalit toṭaṅkutal.
“celviyarkaḷē! nīṅkaḷ eriyaviṭum neruppil uliyil ēṟṟiya maṇaṟcōṟu vēkātu. eṉ kāmakkaṉal iṭṭāl malikūṭa ventu kuḻintuviṭum. nīṅkaḷ immuttukkaḷi uliyiliṭṭuk kuṟṟuvatu, avi uṅkaḷ paṟkaḷukku oppumiyāl pakiyākiya kāraṇattālō eṉavum kūṟuṅkaḷ. uṅkaḷ uṭampiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷiyum taḻuvi um kiyil amarntirukkum vāyppup peṟṟa ippantu mikka nalviṉiyuṭiyatu!“eṉṟāṟpōlat talivaṉ taṉ vēṭkiyip pulappaṭuttum vāyppi uṇṭākkik koṇṭu, talivi tōḻi iruvariṭamum uriyāṭutal.
itu ‘pāṅkimatiyuṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 87-89)
mukampuku kiḷavi (2)
taliviyiṉ uṭaṉpāṭṭi yuṇarnta tōḻi toṭarntu pēcutal. matiyuṭampaṭṭatōḻi talivaṉukkuk kuṟi muṭippa tākak kūṟit talimakaḷ uṭaṉpāṭu kaṇṭu caṟṟu miki yākavē, “talivaṉ kiyiluḷḷa kaṇṇiyākiya kiyuṟi vāṭat taruvataṉṟu”  eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟit talivaṉik kūṭutaṟku avaḷi uṭaṉpaṭuvikkac collum coṟkaḷ.
itu ‘tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattup pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ka. kā.pak. 68)
mukam pukutal -
tōḻi mukam nōkki niṟṟal	(cāmi. 92)
mukamalarcci kūṟal (1) -
talivi talivaṉik kaṇṭa aḷavil avaḷmukam malarn tatik kūṟutal. itu vāyilōr kūṟṟu.
talivi tāṉ pūppu eytiyatit terivikkat tōḻi oruttiyic cevvaṇi aṇivittut talivaṉ taṅkiyirunta parattiyil lattukku viṭuppavē, attōḻiyik kaṇṭu talivaṉ taṉṉip parattiyiṭamiruntu viṭuvittuk koṇṭu maṉikku vara, “taliviyiṉ mayaṅkiya pārvi oḷiyip peṟṟatu; avaḷ mukam katiravaṉ varukiyāl oḷiviṭṭu malarnta tāmari malari ottatu” eṉṟu vāyilōr tammuḷ kūṟikkoḷḷutal.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (ittuṟi māṟaṉalaṅkārattilum (pāṭal 436) uriyuḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.)	(kōvi. 363)
mukamalarcci kūṟal (2) -
kaṟpuk kālattil parattiyiṟ piriviṉi mēṟkoṇṭa talivaṉ talivi viṭutta cevvaṇi kaṇṭu illam vantu cērntāṉāka, atu kaṇṭu talivi mukam malarnta ceytiyi akampuku marapiṉ vāyilōr tammuḷ kūṟutal.
“taliviyip pirintu paratti yillattut taṅkiya talivaṉ, talivi viṭutta cevvaṇi aṇinta cēṭiyik kaṇṭu illam eytiyatāl, paṇṭu tuyarattāl maruṇṭirunta talivi pārvi itupōtu amirtam potinta karuṅ kuvaḷipōla makiḻccik kaṇṇīrāl naṉintatu.“
“cūriyaṉuṭiya utayattil cevvi peṟṟu malarnta centā mari pōl avaḷmukam cevvi peṟṟu alarntatu.” eṉpatu pōṉṟa uḻiyar kūṟṟu.	(mā. pā. pā. 98)
mukiloṭu kūṟal -
kaṟpiṉuḷ pirinta talivaṉ, tāṉ mīṇṭu varuvatākat talivikkuk kuṟitta paruvam vantatu kaṇṭu, taliviyi niṉittu iraṅki virintu tērēṟi varumpōtu, muṟpaṭṭuc cellum mēkaṅkaḷi nōkki, “eṉ taliviyiṉuṭiya ūrukku eṉakku muṉṉē cellā toḻivīrāka!” (kōvi. 329) eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu ‘vēntaṟ kuṟṟuḻip pirivu’, ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ (348) eṉṟa kiḷavikaḷil kāṇappaṭum ceyti.	
muṭinta kālattup pākaṉoṭu pācaṟikkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu -
eṭuttu mēṟkoṇṭa  pōr tampakkam veṟṟiyoṭu muṭin taviṭattut talivaṉ paṭivīṭṭil taṉtērppākaṉiṭam kūṟal.
“tērvalava! vēntaṉum pōriṉi niṟuttiviṭṭāṉ. pakivarum paṇintu tiṟiyaḷittu naṇparāyiṉar. irutiṟap paṭiyōrum māṟupāṭoḻintaṉar. nam kaṭami muṭintu viṭṭatu. ēṉiya tērkaḷ piṉṉiṭumāṟu nam tēric celuttu. yāṉ kuṭavāyili otta iyaṟki yaḻakuṭiya eṉ taliviyiṉ aḻakiya mārpil iṉtuyil peṟutali virumpu kiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟa kūṟṟu. (akanā. 44)	(to.  po. 41 nac.)
muṭinta kālattup pākaṉoṭu virumpiya viṉittiṟavakikkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟal -
taṉ aracaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa pōr veṟṟiyoṭu muṭinta viṭattut talivaṉ taṉatu maṉikku mīṇṭu pōtaṟku muṭivu ceytu pākaṉiṭam taṉniliyiyum taṉ talivi niliyi yum paṟṟik kūṟutal.
talivaṉ viṉimuṭintavaḻit tāṉ ūr mīḷutal karutit taṉṉiyē etirpārttu niṟkum taliviyik kāṇap pōvatāl taṉakkut tōṉṟum makiḻccimikutiyit taṉ tērppākaṉiṭam kūṟik koṇṭirukkum pōtu, piṉṉum maṟṟoru pakivaṉmēl virintu cellum nili ēṟpaṭṭa tāka, appoḻutu talivaṉ kātali maṟantu pōr vēṭki mītūrntu pākaṉiṭam aṭuttu nikaḻtta vēṇṭiya pōrcceyti paṟṟik kūṟutal.
‘viṉittiṟavaki’ eṉpataṉi vaki(yiṉ) viṉittiṟam eṉṟu māṟik kūṭṭi iṅṅaṉam poruḷ ceyvār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.	(to. po. 41)
muṭiccēri -
mulli nilattūr (yāḻ. aka.).
`mutaloṭu puṇarnta yāḻōr mēṉa' -
nilattoṭum kālattoṭum poruntiya kantaruvarpāṟ paṭṭaṉa.	(to. po. 104 iḷa.)
naṭuvaṇ aintiṇiyum tamakku aṭippaṭiyāka avaṟṟoṭu porunti varum kantaruvamārkkam aintum.	(106 nac.)
nilamum kālamum ākiya mutaṟporuḷoṭu kūṭiya kaḷa voḻukkam.	 (144 kuḻa.)
mutaṟporuḷ -
ovvoru tiṇikkum urimiyuṭiya nilamum, perum poḻutu ciṟupoḻutu eṉṟa iruvakippaṭṭa poḻutum ākiya iraṇṭum mutaṟporuḷām. (immutaṟporuḷ kaḷuḷ poḻutu iraṇṭu mayaṅki varalām; āyiṉ nilam iraṇṭu mayaṅkal kūṭātu eṉpatu nūlōr karuttu.)			(na. a. 8.)
mutaṟporuḷiṉ iruvaki -
mutalporuḷ nilam eṉavum, poḻutu eṉavum iraṇṭu vakippaṭum.	(to. po. 17 nac.)
mutuvēṉil -
ōr āṇṭiṉi ivviraṇṭu tiṅkaḷākap pakutta perum poḻutu āṟaṉuḷ, āṉiyum āṭiyumākiya ivviru tiṅkaṭ paruvam mutuvēṉil eṉappaṭum; mutirvēṉil eṉpatum atu. perumpoḻutiṉi vaṭanūlār ‘rutu’ eṉpa.
muntuṟak kāṇṭal -
talivaṉ tāṉ etirpārtta vaṇṇamē taliviyi avviṭattilēyē kāṇutal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘uyireṉa viyattal’ eṉṉum (39).	(na. a. 135.)
`munnāḷ allatu............. iṉṟē' : cūttirap poruḷ -
taliviyāl kaḷam cuṭṭappaṭṭuk kūṭum iṭantalip pāṭṭuk kūṭṭam mūṉṟunāḷaḷavallatu pāṅkiyatu tuṇiyiṉṟi nikaḻātu. ammūṉṟu nāḷuḷ piṉ iru nāḷkaḷil pāṅki tuṇiyātal vēṇṭiṉ attuṇi nīkkum nilimittiṉṟu.	(to. kaḷa. 32 ca. pāla.)
munnīr vaḻakkam -
munnīr - ivvulakip paṭittal kāttal aḻittal eṉṟa mūṉṟu taṉmikaḷiyuṭiya kaṭal.
munnīr vaḻakkam - kaṭalik kalattiṉ vāyilākak kaṭantu cellutal. kāliṟpirivu, kalattiṟpirivu eṉṟa iruvakip pirivukaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya kalattiṟ piriviṉkaṇ makaḷiri uṭaṉaḻittuc cellutal kūṭātu. 	(to. po. 37 iḷa.)
ōtalum tūtum poruḷumākiya mūvakippaṭṭa celaviṉ kaṇṇum taliviyoṭu kūṭac cellutal marapaṉṟu. (34 nac.)
muyakkattup putumaṇam kaṇṭu vaṇṭu moykka, ataṉ kāraṇattit tōḻi cevilikku kūṟuvāḷ pōlat talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākak kūṟiyatu -
“tāyē! erumi karuṅkālip pūkkaḷiyum āmpal pūkkaḷi yum tiṉṟataṉāl avaṟṟilirunta vaṇṭukaḷ avaṟṟi nīttut taliviyiṉ kūntaliluḷḷa pūkkaḷil tātu nukarak karuti ivaḷmītu moykkiṉṟaṉa.” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 93)
muyaṅkal -
talivaṉ taliviyit taḻuvik kūṭi iṉpuṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘iṭantalippāṭu’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇa torukūṟṟu. piṟa cila kiḷaviyuḷḷum, puṇartal kūṭal mutalāka varum pariyāyap peyarkaḷuḷḷum immuyaṅkal nikaḻum eṉka.	(na. a. 135)
muyaṅkutal uṟuttal -
taliviyit talivaṉ iṟukat taḻuvik kūṭutal.
taliviyatu puṉṉaki tanta kuṟippiṉāl avaḷicivu uṇarnta talivaṉ taliviyip pullutal.
itu ‘taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatākiya ceyti.	(na. a. 127)
muracaṟital yārkkeṉṟu ikuḷi viṉātal -
‘ayalavar maṇamuracu āyiṭi vilakkal’ eṉṉum ceytik kaṇṇatu ikkūṟṟu. tōḻi, talivinili kuṟittuc ceviliyiṭam aṟattoṭu niṉṟapiṉ, tirumaṇat toṭar pāka illattil muracu aṟitalik kēṭṭu, “immuracu, nīr niliyiṉ āḻattiṉiṉṟum yāṉiyiṉ tākkutaliṉiṉṟum taliviyik kātta talivaṉukkut taliviyi makaṭ koṭuttaṟkā, aṉṟip piṟarkku avaḷi makaṭ koṭut taṟkā olikkiṉṟatu?” eṉṟu arukiluḷḷāri viṉavutal. (itaṉ payaṉ : ayalavar maṇam pēci varutalit tavirttal) 		(tiruppatik. 364).
iḵtu ‘aṟattoṭu nili’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
‘ayalavar maṇamuracu āyiṭi vilakkal’ eṉṉum ceytik kaṇ aminta eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉatu karuttu nōkkit talip piṭap peṟṟatu.	(mā. aka. 74.)
murukaṉ -
1. kuṟiñci nilat teyvam 2) veṟiyāṭṭāḷaṉ 	(piṅ. 828) (ṭu)
murukiyam -
kuṟiñci nilattil murukaṉukkuriya veṟiyāṭṭuppaṟi.			(to. po. 18 nac.)
mulli (1) -
kāṭum kāṭu cārnta iṭamum.
kāṭṭil kārkālattil mullikkoṭikaḷ palavākap pūttal ciṟappup paṟṟik kāṭum kāṭu cārnta iṭamum mulli eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kaṟpuk kālattil kāval tūtu mutaliya kuṟittup pirinta talivaṉ pirintu varumtuṇiyum talivi āṟṟiyiruntu illiruntu nallaṟam ceytal makaḷirkkuriya iyaṟki mulli ātaliṉ, iruttal oḻukkam paṟṟi, iruttal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ‘mulli’ eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa eṉavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.	(to. po. 5 iḷa. nac.)
mulli (2) -
nilappeyar - puṟavam, puṟampaṇi, puṟavaṇi, mutippuṉam, itippuṉam.
nīr - kāṉāṟu, kaluḻi, uyavi
ūr - pāṭi
paṟi - ēṟukōḷ, pampi	(piṅ. 536, 539, 540, 541, 543, 544.)
makkaḷ - mulliyāḷar, kōvalar, iṭiyar, aṇṭar, potuvar, āṉvallōr, kuṭavar, pālar, toṟuvar, kōvintar, aṇṭar, kōpālar, āyar, amutar.
āycciyar, toṟuviyar, potuviyar, kuṭattiyar, iṭic ciyar; kāṉakanāṭaṉ, kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ, tōṉṟal, aṇṇal (tivā.) 	(tivā. pak. 40 ; piṅ. 546, 547)
mullik karupporuḷ (1) -
1.	teyvam	-	māyōṉ (neṭumāl, kaṇṇaṉ)
2.	uyarmakkaḷ	-	kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ, aṇṇal, tōṉṟal; maṉivi, kiḻatti.
3.	potumakkaḷ	-	āyar, iṭiyar, kuṭavar, kōvalar, potuvar; āycciyar, iṭicciyar, kōvittiyar, potuviyar.
4.	paṟavi	-	kāṭṭuk kōḻi, cival.
5.	vilaṅku	-	māṉ, uḻi, pulvāy, muyal.
6.	ūr	-	pāṭi, cēri, paḷḷi.
7.	nīr	-	kāṭṭāṟṟu nīr, kuṟuñcuṉi nīr.
8.	pū	-	kulli, mulli, tōṉṟi, piṭā.
9.	maram	-	koṉṟi, kāyā, kuruntam, putal.
10.	uṇavu 	-	varaku, cāmi, mutiri.
11.	paṟi	-	ēṟukōṭ paṟi
12.	yāḻ	-	mulliyāḻ.
13.	paṇ	-	cātāri.
14.	toḻil	-	cāmi varaku ivaṟṟi vitittal, kaḷi nīkkutal, arital, kaṭāviṭutal, kuḻal ūtutal, ā erumi āṭu ākiya mūviṉaṅkaḷi mēyttal, kol lēṟu taḻuvutal, kuravik kūttāṭal, kāṭṭāṟṟil nīr viḷiyāṭutal - pōlvaṉa.
15. koṭi	-	kavali (to. po. 20 iḷa., 18 nac., cilap. patikam aṭiyārk., ta.ne.vi. 6-12, na.a.22)
mullik karupporuḷ (2) - 
pū - malliki maram - piṭavam.	(cāmi. 76)
mullikkuriya poḻutu -
mullikkuriya perumpoḻutu āvaṇi puraṭṭācit tiṅkaḷākiya kārkālam; ciṟupoḻutu māli; aḵtāvatu iraviṉ muppatu nāḻikikaḷuḷ mutal pattu nāḻiki (6 yī.a. - 10 yī.a ) 
mullikkuk kārum māliyum uriyavātaṟkuriya kāraṇaṅ kaḷi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkukiṟār.
pirintu mīḷum talivaṉ tēr ēṟip pācaṟiyiṉiṉṟu mālik kālattu ūrvayiṉ varum kālam āvaṇiyum  puraṭṭāciyum ātaliṉ, avi, mikka veppamō mikka taṭpamō iṉṟi iṭinikaravāki, ēval ceytu varum iḷiyarkku nīrum niḻalum payattalāṉum, uṇavu taṭṭup pāṭiṉṟi nīrum niḻalum peṟutaliṉ makiḻcci mikkup paṟavikaḷum vilaṅkukaḷum tuṇiyōṭu iṉpuṟṟu viḷi yāṭuvaṉa kaṇṭu talivaṟkum talivikkum kāmakkuṟippu mikutalāṉum, mullikkup perumpoḻutu āyiṉa.
pulli mēyntu ēṟṟōṭu pacu taṉ kaṉṟi niṉittuk koṇṭu mēyum iṭatti viṭṭu ūrppotuviṭattukku mīṇṭu varavum, kōvalar vēyṅkuḻal ūtavum, pantariluḷḷa mullikkoṭi pūttu maṇam vīcavum, varukiṉṟa taliva ṉukkum irukkiṉṟa talivikkum kāmak kuṟippuc ciṟattaliṉ, kār kālattu māli ciṟupoḻutāyiṟṟu.(to. po. 6 nac.)
mulli, kuṟiñci, marutam, neytal : peyarkkāraṇam -
mulli, kuṟiñci mutaliyaṉa kāraṇam paṟṟi mutalāciriyar iṭṭa kuṟi eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. kāṭuṟi ulakiṟku mullippūc ciṟantatu ākalāṉum (mullip, 8-10), mivari ulakiṟkuk kuṟiñcippūc ciṟantatu ākalāṉum (kuṟun. 3 : 3), tīmpuṉal ulakiṟku marutu ciṟantamiyāṉum (akanā. 286 : 6,7), perumaṇal ulakiṟku neytaṟpūc ciṟantamiyāṉum (kuṟun. 9 : 4 - 6)  innilaṅkaḷi ivvāṟu kuṟippiṭṭār.			(to. po. 5 iḷa.)
iruttal (illiliruntu nallaṟam ceytal) eṉpatu mulli eṉpataṉ poruḷ (ciṟupāṇ. 169); puṇarcci eṉap poruḷ paṭuvatu kuṟiñci (maturik. 300); ūṭiyum kūṭiyum pōkam nukartal marutamām (ciṟupāṇ. 186); iraṅkutal eṉṉum poruḷuṭiyatu neytal, neytaṟpaṟi iraṅkaṟpaṟiyāta liṉ (puṟanā. 389-17). ivvāṟṟāl uripporuḷ paṟṟik kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉpaṉa peyarpeṟṟaṉa.		(to. po. nac.)
mulli, kuṟiñci, marutam, neytal : muṟi vippu -
aṉpiṉ aintiṇi illaṟam paṟṟiya oḻukkamātaliṉ, kaṟpoṭu poruntik kaṇavaṉ collit tavaṟāmal illiliruntu nallaṟam ceytal makaḷiratu iyaṟki mulliyātaliṉ mulli muṟkūṟappaṭṭatu; mulliyāvatu iruttal. puṇartaliṉṟi illaṟam nikaḻāmiyiṉ, puṇartaṟ poruṭṭākiya kuṟiñci ataṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. puṇarccikkup piṉ ūṭal nikaḻtaliṉ ūṭal poruṭṭā kiya marutam aṭuttu vikkappaṭṭatu. parattiyiṟ pirivu paṟṟi ūṭal nikaḻum; appirivu pōlātu ēṉiya pirivu kaḷil talivaṉ kuṟittupapōṉa paruvam toṭaṅkiya aḷavil mīṇṭu varavilliyē eṉṟu talivi iraṅkutal paṟṟi, iraṅkutal poruṭṭākiya neytal īṟṟil vikkap paṭṭatu.	(to. po. 5 nac.)
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal eṉṟu muṟivippu, māli yāmam vikaṟi kāli eṉṟa ciṟupoḻutiṉ kiṭakki muṟiyi oṭṭi amintatē eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar.	(cū. vi. pak. 53)
mulli naṭiyiyal -
vīracōḻiyattiṉ uriyil, talivaṉ pirintavaḻi avaṉ coṟkaḷi uṭkoṇṭu talivi illattiliruntu nallaṟam pēṇi irukkum iruttal paṟṟiya ceytikaḷākiya talivi paruvam varuntuṇi āṟṟiyiruttalum, paruva varukiyik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ caṟṟu varuntalum, talivaṉ viṉi muṟṟik kuṟitta kālattu vara iyalāmikku varuntalum, piṉ tēr ēṟi virintu vantu taliviyik kūṭi makiḻtalum ākiya ceytikaḷ paṟṟiya kiḷavikaḷ palavum tokuttuk kūṟappaṭṭu ‘mulli naṭiyiyal’ eṉṟu peyariṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
mulli naṭi iyal - iruttal eṉṟa oḻukkam paṟṟiya naṭappukkaḷi variciyākak kūṟal.	(vī. cō. 94 uri mēṟ.)
mulivili kūṟal -
tōḻiyāl varivu muṭukkappaṭṭa talivaṉ avaḷiṭam taliviyiṉ tirumaṇattiṟku avaḷ uṟaviṉar vēṇṭum paricapporuḷ paṟṟi viṉava, “niṉ varavi emar ēṟṟuk koṇṭāl nallatu; ataṉi viṭuttup paricapporuḷ kūṟuvārāyiṉ, avaḷukku ēḻulakum vili pōtātu” eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam taliviyi maṇattaṟkuriya paricapporuḷ paṟṟik kūṟutal.
itu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 266)
`muṟpaṭak kiḷanta eḻutiṇi' -
poruḷatikārattil mutaṟkaṇ kūṟappaṭṭa ēḻutiṇikaḷ. avi kikkiḷi, mulli, kuṟiñci, marutam, neytal, pāli, peruntiṇi eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mulli mutaliya aintum akaṉintiṇikaḷ eṉavum, kikkiḷi perun tiṇi eṉṟa iraṇṭum akappuṟattiṇi eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
akamum akappuṟamumākiya ivvēḻum muṟpaṭak kiḷanta eḻutiṇi eṉavē, veṭci mutal pāṭāṇ iṟuvāya puṟattiṇi ēḻum piṟpaṭak kiḷanta eḻutiṇiyām.			(to. po. 1 nac.)
`muṟpaṭa vakutta iraṇṭu' -
muṉṉar akattiṇiyiyaluḷ vakuttōtiya iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷ. aviyāvaṉa parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ irattalum teḷittalumākak kūṟumiṭaṅkaḷ. ivvīriṭaṅkaḷ allāta iṭattu, talivaṉmuṉṉart taṉṉip pukaḻntu kūṟutal talivikku ettakiya niliyilum illi.			(to. kaṟ. 39 ca. pāla.)
muṟuvaṟ kuṟippu uṇartal -
taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcciyil, talivaṉ taliviyiṉ puṉmuṟuvalik kaṇṭu ataṉ vāyilāka avaḷ puṇarccikku uṭaṉpaṭṭa tiṟattiṉi uṇartal.
mukippatam pārkkum vaṇṭu pōla nakippatam pārkkum aṟivuṭimi naṟkāmattukku iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ātaliṉ, talivaṉ taṉ aṟiviṉāl taliviyatu maṉakkuṟippi uṇarntāṉ eṉpatu. 	(kuṟaḷ 1274).
itu ‘taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 127)
muṟi ciṟanta poruḷ -
pāṭaluḷ payiṉṟu varuvaṉa mutaṟporuḷ, karupporuḷ, uripporuḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟumām; immūṉṟaṉuḷ muṉṉati viṭa iraṇṭāvatum, iraṇṭāvativiṭa mūṉṟāvatum ciṟantaṉavām eṉpatu.
cila pāṭalkaḷil immūṉṟu poruḷum varum. cilavaṟṟil mutaṟporuḷiṉṟik karupporuḷum uripporuḷum varum. cilavaṟṟil uripporuḷ māttiramē varum. ākavē, mutaṟporuḷiviṭak karupporuḷum, ivviraṇṭaṉiyum viṭa uripporuḷum pāṭalukkuc ciṟantuḷḷami pōtarum. uripporuḷ iṉṟēl, pāṭalukkup poruṭpayaṉ iṉṟu.
	(to. po. 3 nac.)
`muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ keḻutakip potuccol' -
iyaṟpeyari viṭuttu, viḷippataṟkut toṉṟutoṭṭu vanta muṟiyāṉ aminta col ellā eṉpatu; elā, ella, eluva eṉpaṉavum koḷḷappaṭum. iccoṟkaḷ iru pālukkum potuvāṉa viḷippeyarkaḷ. talivaṉit tōḻiyum, taliviyit talivaṉum (kali. 61, 81) viḷit taṟkaṇ ‘ellā’ eṉṟa potuccollip payaṉpaṭuttutal kāṇka.	(to. po. 220 nac.)
muṟippeyar allātatāy ataṉ maruṅkākap poruntiya takavuṭiyatoru potuccol. aḵtu ellā eṉṉum viḷippeyar. iccol pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkil poruntiya marapiṉāṉē, āṭūu makaṭūu ākiya irupāṟkum urittāka varuvatu. atu talivaṉ pāṅkaṉ talivi tōḻi eṉṉum nālvarkkum urittāka varutaliṉ ‘keḻutakip’ potuc col eṉappaṭṭatu. ēṭā, ēṭī, eluvaṉ, eluvi, eṉpavi pāluṇara niṉṟaṉa. irupāṟkum potuccollām ellā eṉṟa viḷippeyar elā, eluva eṉac ciṟitu tirintum niṟkum.
	(to. poru. 25 ca. pāla)
muṉcelvōr pāṅkiyarkku uṇarttal -
uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟu talivaṉuṭaṉ taṉṉūrkku mīṇṭu vanta talivi, vaḻiyil taṉ muṉṉē celpavar vāyilākat taṉ varukiyip pāṅkimārkkuc colliyaṉuppa, avarka ḷum ac ceytiyi avarkaḷukkuk kūṟal.
“makaḷirē! num talivi taṉ talivaṉoṭu mīṇṭu varum kāṭciyi yāṅkaḷ kaṇṭōm. ‘nāḷi viruppattōṭu iṉiyatoru nalvirunti nīṅkaḷ peṟuvīr’ eṉpati maṉaṅ koḷḷumiṉ!” (ampikā. 412) eṉṟāṟ pōla, muṉ celvōr pāṅkiyarkku uṇarttutal.
kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭiṟkuriya tokuti mūṉṟaṉuḷ iṟutiya tākiya ‘mīṭci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu itu.			(na. a. 191)
muṉ nīṅkiyavaḻit taṉ varuttamum vēṭkiyum avaṭkuk kūṟutal -
pirintiruntapōtu tāṉ uṟṟa tuṉpattit talivaṉ talivikkuk kūṟutal.
“ulakattu ēṉiya neruppu neruṅkiṉāl cuṭum; akaṉṟāl cuṭātu. āyiṉ uṉṉāl viḷinta ikkāmat tī, uṉṉi nīṅkiṉāl cuṭukiṟatu; aṇukiṉāl kuḷircciyāka iruk kiṟatu; uṉṉiṭamuḷḷa ittī ulakiyal tīyiṉ māṟupaṭ ṭuḷḷatē!” (kuṟaḷ 1104) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘iṭantalippāṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(i. vi. 503 uri)
muṉṉattāṉ aṟattoṭu nili -
taliviyiṉ naṟṟāyāṉavaḷ tantitaṉṉiyariṭam, “kulattā lum celvattālum mikkāṉ oruvaṉ nam kuṭumpat toṭarpu koḷḷa virumpukiṟāṉ. avaṉi nām uṟaviṉaṉ ākkik koḷvatil taṭiyoṉṟum illiyē” eṉṟu kūṟa avarkaḷ, “ivaḷ karutic colvatu taṉ makaḷ maṇam karutip pōlum” eṉṟu kuṟippāl uṇarvar.
talivaṉ pārppāri muṉṉiṭṭu aruṅkalaṉkaḷoṭu taṉ kuṭumpattu mūttārit taliviyi makaṭpēci vara viṭuppa, talivitamar makaṭ koṭukka uṭaṉē iciyātu maṟuppar.
“tirumaṇam eṉpatu naṉku ārāyntu muṭivu ceyyap paṭuvatākum. nalla aṉupavamuṭiya mūttavarkaḷic cūḻntē muṭivu ceyya vēṇṭum. ivarkaḷ illattil peṇ kēṭpatē periya ceyal. etaṉiyum uṭaṉē muṭivuceytu collamāṭṭārkaḷ” eṉṟu maṟṟavar niṉikkumāṟu naṭantukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. “peṇ kēṭpavaruṭiya uṇmi niliyi ārāyāmal uṭaṉē peṇ koṭukka ottukkoṇṭu viṭṭārkaḷē! periya avacarakkārarkaḷ ivar!” eṉṟa ūrār pēccukku añciyum peṇkoṭuppa icivataṟkuk kālatāmatam ceyya vēṇṭum. “eppaṭiyō peṇṇit tirumaṇam ceytukoṭuttāṟ pōtum eṉṟiruntār pōlum!” eṉṟa ūrār kūṟṟukkum añcutal vēṇṭum; iṟappap periyavar tammiṭam  makaḷ kēṭṭavaḻit tam niliyi niṉittu muṭivu ceyyavum ciṟitu kālam tāḻttal vēṇṭum. potuvāka ulakiyal paṟṟiyum uṭaṉē icivu tarutal kūṭātu.
ivaṟṟāl taliviyiṉ tanti makaṭ koṭukka maṟutta vaḻiyum, naṟṟāy mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu kuṟippiṉāl aṟattoṭu niṉṟu varivu māṭciyamippaṭa muyalvāḷ.			(iṟi. a. 28 uri.)
muṉṉikaḻvurittu ūṭal tīrttal -
kaṟpukkālattil parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talimakaṉiṭattuk kalavi karuti ūṭiya taliviyiṭam, “nām uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ iravu oru ciṟṟūril taṅki, ciṟiya māṉtōlpaṭukkiyil uṉ mārpakam eṉṉit taḻuvat taṅkiya ciṟu toḻiṟku oppu etuvum kaṇṭilēṉ. annikaḻcciyi nī niṉittuppārt tāyā?” eṉṟāṟpōlap paḻiya nikaḻcciyi eṭutturittu avaḷatu ūṭalit talivaṉ nīkkiyatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 398)
‘muṉṉiya mūṉṟu’, `piṉṉiya nāṉku' -
pāṅkar nimittam paṉṉiraṇṭaṉuḷ, muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa pirintavaḻik kalaṅkal, niṟpavi niṉii nikaḻpavi urittal. kuṟṟaṅkāṭṭiya vāyil peṭpuṟal ākiya mūṉṟum akaṉintiṇikkaṇ kikkiḷi oḻukkamāka amiyum; piṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa col avaṭcārttaliṉ pulliya vaki, aṟintōṉ ayarppiṉ avvaḻi maruṅkiṉ kēṭum pīṭum kūṟal, tōḻi nīkkaliṉ ākiya nilimi, maṭal māk kūṟutal ākiya nāṉkum akaṉintiṇikkaṇ perun tiṇi oḻukkamāka amiyum.	(to. kaḷa. 14, 15 ca. pāla.)
muṉṉilip puṟamoḻi -
vāyilkaḷāvār talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa ivarkaḷuṭiya ūṭalit taṇikkumpōtu tammuṉ niṟkum talivaṉi nōkki nērākap pēcāmal piṟarik kūṟumāṟupōlat tam oṟṟumippaṭuttum coṟkaḷip pēci ūṭal tīrttu vippar. muṉṉilip puṟamoḻi - muṉ niṉṟāṉiṭam nērākap pēcāmal piṟarik kūṟumāṟu pōlak kūṟutal.			(to. po. 167 nac.)
muṉṉili yākkal (1) -
talivaṉ taliviyi muṉṉilippaṭuttuvatu.
taṉvayamiḻantu aciyāmal niṟkum taliviyi muṉṉilip paṭutti aḻittu, “pāṅkiyaruṭaṉ kūṭic cuṉiyāṭutal pōṉṟa viḷiyāṭṭil īṭupaṭāmal, patumi pōla niṟki ṟāyē! itaṉ kāraṇam eṉ?” (tañci. kō. 7) eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 127)
muṉṉili yākkal (2) -
taṉiyiṭattut taliviyi etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ, taṉ perumiyum aṟivum nīṅki vēṭki mītūrap puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉāṉāyiṉum, taliviyiṉuṭiya accam maṭam nāṇ ivaṟṟi nīkkuvataṟkuk kaṇṇāl kūṟiyati nīkki moḻiyālum kuṟippiṭum eḻuvakic ceytikaḷuḷ mutalāvatu.
talivi vēṭkikkuṟippu uṭiyaḷāyiṉum, kulattiṉvaḻi vanta iyaṟki āṟṟalāl nāṇamum accamum mika, akkuṟip pillātavaḷip pōla niṉṟavaḻi avaḷi muṉṉiliyāka vittut talivaṉ cila kūṟutal.
“‘niṉ tōḻimāroṭu kaṭaṟkari maṇal viḷiyāṭṭiyum mēvāy; neytalmāliyiyum toṭukkāy; ikkaṭaṟ karic cōliyil orupakkamākat taṉittu niṉṟāy nī yār? niṉṉit toḻutavāṟu viṉavukiṟēṉ. kaṭaṟparappil mēviya aṇaṅkō? ciṟiya uppaṅkaḻi maruṅkil nilipeṟ ṟuḷḷa teyvamakaḷō? yāreṉac colluka’ eṉṟēṉ. eṉ coṟku maṟumāṟṟam avaḷ tantilaḷēṉum, avaḷuṭiya muḷpōlum eyiṟukaḷ ciṟitē arumpit tōṉṟiṉa; 
(-ciṟitē muṟuvalittāḷ.) kaṇkaḷil uvakikkaṇṇīr paraviyatu” (naṟ. 155) eṉa varum talivaṉ kūṟṟuḷ, ‘muṉṉili yākkal’ pulappaṭṭavāṟu.	(to. po. 98 iḷa.)
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉarp piritalum pirital nimittamumāyp poruntum vakiyāṉ talivaṟkuriya eṉappaṭṭa ēḻaṉuḷ, mutal mūṉṟu nayappiṉ kūṟu. avaṟṟuḷ mutalāvatu muṉṉiliyākkal. aḵtāvatu muṉ niṉṟu tāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟik kēṭṭu maṟumoḻi kūṟum iyalpiṉa allāta vaṇṭu, neñcu mutaliyavaṟṟi muṉ niṟutti urippatu. aṟivu taṭumāṟik kūṟāmal tāṉ kūṟuvataṉit talivi kēṭka vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavē vaṇṭu mutaliyavaṟṟukkut taṉ maṉamakiḻcci tōṉṟa urippatu.
“pūkkaḷil makarantaṅkaḷi āyum tumpiyiṉattu vaṇṭē! nī eṉ nilattukkuriyāyātaliṉ eṉ viruppattiṟkiciyak kūṟātu, nī uṇmi eṉak kaṇṭataṉik kūṟuvāyāka. ittalivi kūntaliviṭa, nī aṟintuḷḷa pūkkaḷil naṟu maṇam mikkaṉavum uḷavō?” (kuṟun. 2) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(101 nac.)
muṉṉuṟa uṇarttal (1) -
muṉṉam mika uṇartal; aḵtāvatu kuṟippu mikutiyāka uṇartal. taliviyoṭu taṉakkuḷḷa nerukkattāṉ kuṟippiṉṟiyē oru pātiyi uṇarnta tōḻi, ippoḻutu kuṟippup peṟṟavaḻit taliviyiṉ kaḷavoḻukkam paṟṟi mika uṇarum eṉpatu. itu matiyuṭampaṭuttaliṉ mūvakiyuḷ iraṇṭāvatu.	(to. po. 127 nac.)
iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarnta piṉṟit taliviyiṉ vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu, “iḵtu etaṉāṉ āyiṟṟō?” eṉṟu tōḻi aiyuṟṟu niṟpatu.
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ piṉ taliviyiṉ kaṇcivappum nutal vēṟupāṭum kaṇṭu, avaḷ uḷḷattiluḷḷa maṟiyi avaḷoṭu palavāṟu uriyāṭit tōḻi uṇarvatu.			(iṟi. a. 7 uri)
muṉṉuṟa uṇartal (2) -
talivi uṭalvēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu, tōḻi aiyuṟṟu mutaṟkaṇ taliviyi viṉāvutal ākiya ‘muṉṉuṟa uṇartal’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇ ‘vāṭṭam viṉātal’ eṉṟa oru kūṟṟē uḷḷatu.	(kōvi. 62)
muṉṉuṟu puṇarcciyil tōḻi kūṟṟu -
muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci - tōḻi muṟpaṭṭut talivaṉ talivi yarik kūṭṭuvikkum kūṭṭam.
irattalum kuṟiyuṟutalum ceytu āṟṟāṉākic ceṉṟa talivaṉit taṉṉiṉām kūṭṭam muṭiyātuviṭiṉ peritum tuṉpuṟuvāṉ eṉpataṉit tiripiṉṟi uṇarnta tōḻi kāppu mikuti collaluṟṟu, “ivviṭam kāppu mikuti uṭiyatu; ātaliṉ nī varutal kūṭātu” eṉṟu kūṟi vayap paṭuttiyum, avaṉiṭam, “nī ivvāṟu cuṟṟit tiritaṟkuk kāraṇam yātu?” eṉṟu avaṉ vanta kāraṇatti viṉaviyum, “eṉṉi maṟiyātu kūṟiṉ, niṉ viruppiṉi muṭittut taruval” eṉak kūṟiyum, “yāṉ kuṟṟēval makaḷ; eṉ col ēlātu pōkalām; nīyē ceṉṟu taliviyiṭam niṉ viruppattik kūṟu” eṉṟu colliyum, “nī virumpum makaḷi yāṉ aṟiyēṉē!” eṉṟu kūṟiyum, taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum ēnti vanta vaṉiṭam avaṉ viruppi ēṟṟuk koḷḷātu maṟṟoṉṟu kūṟiyum, “ivviṭattil eṉṉiyar ētam ceyvar; num viruppi akaṉṟupōy muṭittukkoḷka” eṉṟu colliyum, “taliviyiṉ maṉanili ēṟpa illi; avaḷiṭam etuvum uriyāṭal muṭiyātu” eṉṟu colli yum, “talivi eṅkaṭkut teyvam pōlvāḷ; avaḷiṭam takātaṉa pēcutal kūṭātu” eṉṟu colliyum, “periyō ṉākiya niṉakku emmaṉōr toṭarpu takkataṉṟu” eṉṟu colliyum ivvāṟākat tōḻi tāṉ kūṭṭuvikkum puṇarccikkaṇ talivaṉiṭam pala vakiyāka uriyāṭuvāḷ.		(iṟi. a. 12 uri)
‘muṉṉiya nāṉku’ -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikku muṉ nikaḻnta kāṭci, aiyam, terital, tēṟal eṉṟa kuṟippu nāṉkum.
ivi naṟkāmattukku iṉṟiyamiyātu varutaliṉ ciṟap puṭik kikkiḷiyātaṟkuriya.	(to. po. 52 nac.)
ēṟā maṭal tiṟam eṉṉum veḷippaṭa irattalum, iḷami tīrāta tiṟam eṉṉum nalam pārāṭṭalum, teḷivu nīṅkiya kāmattu mikāt tiṟam eṉṉum puṇarā irakkamum, mikka kāmattiṉ māṟākāt tiṟam eṉṉum nayappuṟuttalum, iṉpam payappa varum kikkiḷiyiṉkaṇ nikaḻvaṉavām.	(55 iḷa.)
muṉṉar nikaḻntaṉavaṟṟik kātalar piṉṉar niṉittup pārkkum nikaḻntatu niṉittal, kātalar tammuḷ nikaḻnta ceytiyi eṭuttuk kūṟi ataṉkaṇ īṭupaṭutal, akattuṟi kaḷil muṉpu kūṟappaṭāta piṟa tuṟikaḷum vantu kalattal, aintiṇi uṇarttum uripporuḷ pakutikaḷil uḷḷuṟi uvamam mikutiyāka varutal eṉṟa nāṉkum akaṉintiṇi kaḷukkum ciṟantu uriyavātal pōlavē, kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟanta kikkiḷikkaṇ varum. (e-ṭu : kuṟun. 25, 30, 101, 90).			(to. po. 51 pārati)
‘muṉṉiya mūṉṟu’ -
piramam piracāpattiyam āriṭam teyvam kāntaruvam ācuram irākkatam picācam eṉṟa eṇvaki maṇaṅkaḷuḷ, iṭa vakiyāṉ muṟpaṭṭaṉavāka amiyum ācuram irākkatam picācam eṉṟa mūṉṟu maṇaṅkaḷum, taliviyiṉ kuṟip paṟital vēṇṭātu talivaṉ taṉakkut talivimāṭṭu ēṟpaṭum mikka kāmattāl nikaḻum maṇaṅkaḷ ātaliṉ ivi kikkiḷi maṇaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.	(to. po. 105 nac.)
‘muṉṉiya mūṉṟu’, ‘piṉṉiya nāṉku’ -
ācuram, irākkatam, picācam eṉpaṉa muṉṉiya mūṉṟu. piramam, piracāpattiyam, āriyam, teyvam eṉpaṉa piṉṉi nāṉku. ivi muṟiyē kikkiḷiyum peruntiṇiyum ām.	(to. po. 105 nac.)
muṉṉorukāl pirintu vanta talivaṉ piṉṉum pirintu vantuḻit talivi avaṉi muyaṅkāḷāka, tōḻi, “nī ivvāṟu ceytaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉ?” eṉa viṉaviyavaḻit talivi tōḻikkut talivaṉ kēṭpac colliyatu -
“naṟumalariyuṭiya curapuṉṉiyiṉ kiḷi varuntak kaṭal alikaḷ acikkum tuṟivaṉ ātaliṉ, niliyāka nam miṭattut taṅkātu, vantum peyarntum nammi varuttu kiṟāṉ. avaṉ eppoḻutō orumuṟi nammi aṇuka varutaliṉ, avaṉ nammik kūṭum kālattum, namakku avaṉ pirivu paṟṟiya niṉivē iruttaliṉ, nammit tuṉpuṟuttik koṇṭē irukkiṟāṉ. attakiyavaṉuṭiya puṇarvum pirivum namakku orē niliyaṉa” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.			(aiṅ. 150)
muṉṉoru kālattup poruḷvayiṉ pirinta talivaṉ, piṉṉum poruḷvayiṟ pirivi valitta neñciṟku, nikaḻntatu niṉittuk kūṟiyatu -
mutalil poruḷvayiṉ pirintu mīṇṭa talivaṉ mīṇṭum poruḷvayiṉ piritali niṉittaviṭattu, “muṉpu yāṉ ceṉṟa mūṅkil niṟinta kāṭṭil malivāyc cellum muḻumatiyi nōkkiya aḷavil, ili utirnta maraṅkaḷi yuṭiya malimītu amikkappaṭṭa eṉatu illattil vāḻum muḷ pōṉṟa paṟkaḷiyum tilakamiṭṭa naṟiya neṟṟi yiyum koṇṭa muḻumatiyam pōṉṟa eṉ taliviyiṉ niṉippu vara, yāṉ kāṭṭik kaṭantu ceṉṟu poruḷīṭ ṭutal aritāya ceyalāy muṭintatu. ātaliṉ aḻakiya taliviyip pirintu ceyyum ceyal iṉi vēṇṭā” eṉṟu neñciṭam collic celavu tavirtal (naṟ. 62) pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 43 nac.)
‘muṉivu takavu iṉmi muyaṉṟu paṇpu eytal’ -
talivaṉāl maṇantukoḷḷappaṭṭa iraṇṭām maṉivi, kāmakkiḻatti ākiyōriṭam veṟuppukkāṭṭutalit tavirttu viruppuṭaṉ avarkaḷi varavēṟṟu mēmpaṭṭa illaṟat talivikkuriya  paṇpiṉit talivi peṟutal. (maruta naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 95 urimēṟ.)
muṉivaṉ -
1. iṟivaṉ (to. po. 649 pērā.) 2. talivaṉ 			(kōvi koḷu 329) (ṭu)
mūṉṟaṉ pakuti -
nālvaki valiyuḷḷum taṉ valiyum tuṇi valiyum viṉi valiyum eṉpaṉa.	(to. po. 44 iḷa. 225 kuḻa.)
“aṟattiṉāṉ poruḷākki apporuḷāṉ kāmam nukarval” eṉal.	(to.  po. 41 nac.)
kaṟpuk kālattiṟkuriya poruḷ, ōtal, tūtu eṉṟa piriviṉ mūṉṟu pakuti.	(akat. 40 pārati.)
tāṉi, yāṉi, kutiri eṉṟa muttiṟak kūṟupāṭu.			(44, aruṇā.)
mūṉṟaṉ pakutiyum maṇṭalat tarumiyum tōṉṟal cāṉṟa māṟṟōr mēṉmi’ -
tāṉi yāṉi kutiri eṉṟa muttiṟattup paṭippakuti yāṉum, tam āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa araṇ aminta nilapparap piṉ perumiyāṉum viḷakkam mikkavarāy uṟiyum māṟṟāriṉ cerukkuṟṟa mēṉmi. itaṉkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum.	(to. akat. 43 ca. pāla)
mūṉṟām kāmakkiḻatti, maṟṟa kāmakkiḻattiyar parattiyar ākiyōrtam illiṟku aḻittuc cellappaṭṭup paricup poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ taṉmakaṉ mīṇṭavaḻit talivi taṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal -
‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ik kūṟṟiṉi iḷampūraṇar cuṭṭuvār.
“eṉ makaṉ, mutaṟkaṇ, taṉ tanti aṭakkamillāta pōtil kāmuṟṟa attoṭakkattut tāy illiṟ pukkāṉ; aṭuttu vaḻimuṟit tāy maṉikkaṇ pukkāṉ; aṭuttu, talik koṇṭu nammoṭu kāyum pulattakip putiyavaḷ illilē ceṉṟāṉ; piṟaku nammiṭattē pukkāṉ” (kali. 82) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 149 iḷa.)
meykkuṟi urittal -
iravukkuṟi varutaṟku muṉ tōḻi talivaṉiṭam tam nāṭṭiṉar iṉṉa āṭi uṭuttu iṉṉa pūccūṭi iṉṉa cāntu aṇintu iṉṉa marattiṉ niḻalil viḷiyāṭuvar eṉṟu tamnāṭṭu aṇiyiyal kūṟutal. (itu ‘taṉ nāṭṭu aṇiyiyal pāṅki cāṟṟal’ eṉavumpaṭum. (na.a. 158)) (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 urimēṟ.)
meytīṇṭal -
meytoṭṭup payiṟal. atu kāṇka.	(cāmi. 87)
meytoṭṭup payiṟal (1) -
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ meyyit toṭṭu neruṅkutal.
“uṉ aḻakiya uṟuppukkaḷiyum, melliya kariya kūntaliyum toṭum  vāyppu eṉkikku nērntati yāṉ eṉṉeṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ!” (ampikā. 31) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu ‘taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 127)
meytoṭṭup payiṟal (2) -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṭattup perumiyum uraṉum uṭiya talivaṉ taṉ uḷḷamum talivi uḷḷamum otta paṇpiṉik kūṟiyapiṉ atu kāraṇamākak kātalveḷḷam puraṇṭōṭat taliviyiṉ uṭalit tīṇṭip paḻakutal.			(to. po. 99 iḷa.)
iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇ talivaṉ talivi meyyit tīṇṭip paḻakum nilimi.
talivaṉ meytoṭṭup payiṉṟataṉkaṇ kūṟiyatu - nōkkuka.
meyyuṟu puṇarcci -
otta aṉpāṉ iyinta talivaṉum taliviyum, tammiṭam tōṉṟiya kāmam mikutaliṉālē, talivaṉ taṉakkuriya aṟivu niṟi ōrppu kaṭippiṭi eṉṟa nāṉku paṇpukaḷum, talivi taṉakkuriya nāṇ maṭam accam payirppu eṉṟa nāṉku paṇpukaḷum puṉal ōṭiyavaḻip pul cāyntāṟ pōlak kāmattāl cāykkappaṭavē, meyyuṟṟup puṇarum kūṭṭam.		(na. a. 36)
meyyuṟupuṇarcci nikaḻum kālam -
kāṭci, vēṭki, uḷḷutal, melital, ākkam ceppal, nāṇu vari iṟattal, nōkkuva ellām aviyē pōṟal, maṟattal, mayakkam, cākkāṭu eṉṟa pattu avattikaḷiyum talimakaṉum, kāṭci mutaliya mūṉṟum paṟṟi ovvoṉṟiṟ kum nannāṉkāka pukumukam purital, poṟinutal viyarttal, naku nayam maṟittal, citivu piṟark kiṉmi, kūḻivirittal, kātoṉṟu kaḷital, ūḻaṇi tivaral, uṭipeyart tuṭuttal, alkul tivaral, aṇintavi tiruttal, ilvaliyuṟuttal, irukiyum eṭuttal eṉṟa paṉṉiraṇṭu meyppāṭukaḷiyum taliviyum peṟuvārāyiṉ, avar iruvariṭiyē meyyuṟupuṇarcci nikaḻum.	(i. vi. 405)
mellak koṇṭēkal -
taliviyi uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ aḻittuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ, “uṉṉoṭu celvatāl, ik koṭiya kāṭu eṉakkuk kuḷircciyāka uḷḷatu. niṉ cīṟaṭi varuntāmal mella naṭantu varuvāyāka” eṉṟu talivikkuc cōrvu ēṟpaṭātavakiyil avaḷi mella naṭatti aḻittuc cellutal.
itaṉit ‘taliviyit talivaṉ curattu uyttal’ eṉṟum 
kūṟupa.	(na. a. 182)
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
	(kōvi. 215)
mellam pulampaṉ -
neytalnilat talivaṉ. neytalnilam meṉṉila mātaliṉ neytaṟ pakuti ‘mellam pulampu’ eṉappaṭṭatu. mellaṉ, pulampaṉ eṉa neytalnilat talivaṉ peyarkaḷic cuṭṭuvar.	(piṅ. 602) (kuṟun. 5)
mellam pulampu -
meṉṉilaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya neytalnilam.			(kōvi. 379 uri)
melital -
kikkiḷit talivi ūrār kūṟum alarāl nāṇi melital.
“kāmamikutiyāl eṉṉuṭal kaṉalāyk kāyntuviṭukiṟatu; ivaṉik kūṭappeṟāmal eṉ kaṇkaḷum nīr curakkiṉṟaṉa; muṉpiṉ aṟiyāta ayalāṉāṉa ivaṉukkāka nāṉ ippaṭi nalamiḻantu nalikiṟēṉ” eṉpatu pōṉṟa kikkiḷit talivi kūṟṟu.
itu peṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷit tuṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.			(pu. ve. mā. 15-4)
melitākac collik kuṟinayappittal -
talivaṉuṭiya viruppatti niṟivēṟṟi vippatāka avaṉiṭam uṟutimoḻi kūṟiya tōḻi taliviyi aṭintu, “iṅkut tōṉṟal oruvaṉ varukiṟāṉ. piṟar kuṟi muṭikkum apperuntakiyāḷaṉ, eṉṉāl taṉakku niṟivēṟa vēṇṭiya ceytiyoṉṟu uṭiyāṉpōlum. yāṉ ataṉi muṭittuvippatāka avaṉatu āṟutalukkākak kūṟiya ceytiyi uṇmiyākak koṇṭu avaṉ makiḻvoṭu pōyviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ iṉṟu variṉ, avaṉ kuṟiyi yāṉ muṭittu vikkāmiyāl eṉṉip poyyaḷ eṉṟu muṭivu ceytuviṭuvāṉē!” eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟit taliviyiṉ maṉaniliyi uṇarntu taliva ṉoṭu kūṭṭuvikka āvaṉa ceytal; tōḻi taliviyiṭam meṉmiyāṉa coṟkaḷip pēci, avaḷ tāṉ virumpum ceyali niṟivēṟṟuvataṟku virumpac ceytal.			(iṟi. a. 10 uri)
melivu kaṇṭu cevili kūṟal -
variporuṭkup pirinta talivaṉiṭamiruntu tūtukūṭa varavilliyē eṉṟu varuntiya niliyilirunta taliviyi nōkkiya cevili, “eṉpeṇ paṇṭiya niliyi lilliyē! orukāl immaliyil murukapperumāṉāl tīṇṭappaṭṭu inniliyaḷ ākiyiruttal kūṭumō?” eṉṟu taliviyatu melivu kaṇṭu kūṟip pāṅkiyi viṉavutal.
itaṉic ‘cevili talimakaḷ vēṟṟumikaṇṭu pāṅkiyi viṉātal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa; ‘aṟattoṭunili’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatu.	(i. vi. 533 uri)
itu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 282)
melivu viḷakkuṟuttal -
taṉiyiṭattut taliviyi etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ taṉ perumiyum aṟivum nīṅki vēṭki mītūrap puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉāṉāyiṉum, taliviyiṉuṭiya accam maṭam nāṇ ivaṟṟi nīkkutaṟkuk kaṇṇāl kūṟiyati viṭuttu moḻiyāṉum kuṟippiṭum eḻuvakic ceytikaḷuḷ aintāvatu.
melivu viḷakkuṟuttalāvatu - talivaṉ uḷḷattu niṟinta kāmanōyāṉ taṉakkup puṟattuṟuppukkaḷil nikaḻum taḷarviṉik kuṟippāka eṭuttuk kūṟal.	(to. po. 98 iḷa.)
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ piṉ piritalum piritalnimittamu mākap poruntuvaṉavāyt talivaṟku uriyaṉa ēḻaṉuḷ iḵtu aintāvatu. melivu viḷakkuṟuttalāvatu talivaṉ ippirivāl taṉakkuḷḷa varuttattit talivi maṉam koḷḷak kūṟalum, taliviyiṉ varuttattik kuṟippāṉ uṇarntu atu tīrak kūṟalum ām. taṇṇīr vēṭṭu ataṉi uṇṭu uyir peṟṟavaṉ “itaṉāl uyir peṟṟēṉ” eṉak karuti ataṉmāṭṭu vēṭki nīṅkātavāṟu pōlat talivimāṭṭu vēṭki eyti avaḷi aritiṉ kūṭi uyir peṟṟāṉātaliṉ, “ivaḷāl uyir peṟṟēṉ” eṉ ṟuṇarntu avaḷmēl nikaḻkiṟa aṉpuṭaṉē piriyak karutuvāṉātaliṉ, talivaṟkum pirivaccam uḷatāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu :	‘ivvulakamē peṟiṉum niṉṉuṭiya naṭpiṉi viṭak karutukiṉṟilēṉ” (kuṟun. 300) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉam aṉpu nikaḻavum, “ippuṇarcciyip piṟar aṟital kūṭātu eṉpataṟkākap pirikiṉṟēṉ” eṉpataṉit talivikku maṉaṅkoḷḷak kūṟutalē viḷakkuṟuttalām. taṉ iṭam aṇittu eṉṟāṟpōla mēlum vaṟpuṟuttip piṉṉarp pirivāṉ eṉpatu.	(to. po. 101 nac.)
melivoṭu vikal -
alar nāṇiyum kāmam kimikuntum varuntiya kikkiḷit talivi, attuṉpattoṭu taḷarntirunta nili.
“kāmam varuttak kaṇṇīr coriya eṉ vēṭkiyi aṭak kik koṇṭu, eppaṭiyāvatu ivviraviṉik kaḻippēṉ” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu peṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷit tuṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
	(pu. ve. mā. 15 - 5)
meṉpāl -
marutamum neytalumākiya meṉṉilap pakuti (to. co. 10 cēṉā.) marutam eṉṟu nikaṇṭu kūṟum.			(piṅ. 724; tivā. pak. 108; nā. ni. 539, cūṭā. ē. 33)
meṉpulap potupporuḷ -
‘meṉpulap poruḷ’ kāṇka.
meṉpulap poruḷ -
kīri, vaḻutuṇi, kiṭi, caṇappu, vēri, taṇakku, marutam mutaliyaṉa meṉpulamākiya marutam neytaṟ pakutikaḷukkuriya poruḷkaḷām.	(vī. cō. 96 urimēṟ.)
meṉpulam -
marutamum neytalumākiya meṉṉilap pakuti; meṉpāl.	(ṭu)
meṉmoḻiyāṟ kūṟal -
kaḷavukkālattil talivaṉuṭiya kuṟiyit talivi nayantu niṟivēṟṟa iṇaṅkumāṟu kūṟak karutiya tōḻi, taliviyi aṇuki, “periyōṉ oruvaṉ vāṭiya mēṉiyaṉum vāṭāta taḻiyaṉumāy nam puṉatti viṭṭu nīṅkātu niṟkiṟāṉ; taṉ kuṟi iṉṉateṉṟu veḷippaṭak kūṟuvatum ceykilaṉ. iḵtu eṉṉa māyamō, aṟiyēṉ” eṉa, maṉmataṉ pōlavum murukaṉ pōlavum peruvaṉappiṉaṉākiya talivaṉuṭiya melivukku nontu kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘kuṟi nayappuk kūṟal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 83)
mivari ulakam -
mēkam paṭintirukkum mali cūḻnta kuṟiñcip pakuti. ‘ulaku’ eṉṉum col maṇṇulakam muḻutumēyaṉṟit taṉkaṇ kūṟupaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷiyum uṇarttum eṉpatu. (cin. 2606 nac.)		(to. po. 5 nac.)
moḻicēr taṉmi -
akapporuḷuri irupattēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu (vī.cō. 91). coṟkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu vēṟṟumi, viṉi, paṇpu, uvami, ummi, aṉmoḻittoki paṭumāṟṟāṉ cērntirukkum niliyik kūṟutal. tokiyē aṉṟi, muṟṟu, īreccam, eḻuvāy, viḷi, vēṟṟumi viri, iṭic col, uriccol, aṭukkuccol ākiya tokānilit toṭarkaḷum ‘moḻi cēr taṉmi’yil aṭaṅkum.			(vī. cō. 96 uri)
moḻipporuḷ -
naṟcol nimittam. itu viricci eṉṟu puṟapporuḷil kūṟappaṭum. mullippūkkaḷi nāḻiyil koṇṭu pōṉa nelluṭaṉē tūvi, ūr ellip puṟattu niṉṟu naṟcol nimittam pārttal marapu. (mullip. 8 - 11)(to. po. 36 nac.)
moḻipeṟa varuntal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉar, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattut taliviyip poḻiliṭiyē taṉiyē kaṇṇuṟṟa talivaṉ avaḷatu puṇarcciyuṭaṉpāṭṭi avaḷ colvāyilākap peṟamuyalutal.
itaṉik ‘kūṭaṟku ariteṉa vāṭi yurittal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.	(i. vi. 503 uri)
itu ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 41)
moḻi peṟa virumpal -
itu tirukkōviyāriluḷḷa ‘kiḷavi vēṭṭal’ eṉum tuṟiyi ottatu. aḵtāvatu taliviyiṉ coṟkēṭka virumputal.
iyaṟkippuṇarccikku muṉ talivaṉ taliviyiṉ uḷḷak kuṟippiṉi avaḷatu puṉmuṟuvalāl uṇarntu avaḷ puṉmuṟuvaliṉ aḻakiṉi viyanta piṉṉar, “taṉ kaṇkaḷāl kavarappaṭṭa yāṉ makiḻumāṟu puṉmuṟuval ceyta it talivi vāyit tiṟantu cila coṟkaḷ kūṟiṉ, avi eṉnōykku maruntāka irukkum” (tiruppatik. 22) eṉṟu avaḷ coṟkaḷik kēṭka virumputal.
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. akap. 19)
moḻipeṟātu kūṟal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcci, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam ivaṟṟiṉ piṉ tōḻi yiṉ utaviyiṉālēyē kaḷavoḻukkattit toṭartal vēṇṭum eṉṟu muṭivu ceyta talivaṉ, taliviyum tōḻiyum uṭaṉirukkum iṭattiṉi eyti, vēḻam kali māṉ vaḻi pati peyar ākiyavi paṟṟit tōḻiyi viṉaviya vaḻi, avaḷ maṟumoḻi koṭātatik kaṇṭu, “ippuṉatti luḷḷa makaḷir viruntiṉarōṭu uriyāṭak kūṭātu eṉpatu viratamāka uṭiyavarō?” eṉṟāṟpōla viṉavutal.
itu ‘moḻiyāmi viṉātal’ eṉṟum kūṟuppeṟum.	
	(i. vi. 507 uri)
itu ‘matiyuṭampaṭuttal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 57)
moḻivaki -
iḵtu akapporuḷuri irupattēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu; ip pāṭṭuc ceñcol, tiricol, vaṭacol, ticiccol eṉṉumivaṟṟil iṉṉa colpaṟṟi vantatu eṉpatu.			(vī. cō. 86)
mōkam -
kaḷaviṉuḷ mutaṟkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyariṭi meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻumuṉ talivaṉukku viḷiyakkūṭiya pattu avattikaḷil oṉpatāvatākiya mayakkam eṉṟa nili. itaṉkaṇ moḻipala pitaṟṟalum nikaḻum.
	(vī. cō. 96 urimēṟ.)
ya
yāṇarik kāṇa virumpal -
taliviyiṉ puṉiṟṟukkālattup putiya eḻilit talivaṉ kāṇa virumputal.
“putalvaṉip peṟṟeṭuttup putuppolivuṭaṉ vikum eṉ maṉiviyiṉ kuvaḷi nōkkiṉiyum, maṉamakiḻcci yiṉiyum, kiyiṟ kuḻantiyuṭaṉ irukkum niliyiṉiyum, neyyāṭiya aḻakiṭṭu veḷutta mēṉiyiṉiyum tavamuṭi yēṉ ceṉṟu kāṇpatu eṉṟikkō?” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
yāṇar - putitākat tōṉṟum aḻaku.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru tuṟi.	(ampikā. 465)
“yārē ivar maṉattu eṇṇam yātu” eṉat tērtal” -
taṉṉiṭam vantu ūrum pērum keṭutiyum piṟavum viṉavi niṟkum talivaṉi, “ivar yār? ivar maṉak karuttu yātu?” eṉat tōḻi ārāytal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘aiyuṟutal’ (60)‘aṟivu nāṭal’ (61) eṉac cuṭṭum.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkimatiyuṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 140)
yāḻōr -
yāḻvallōr; ‘yāḻōr marutam paṇṇa’ (maturik. 658)
yāḻōr kūṭṭam -
itu maṉṟal eṭṭaṉuḷ aintāvatu. ituvē kāntaruvam eṉappaṭuvatu. kantaruvar eṉṟa tēvacātiyiṉariṉ iṉpam tuykkum vaki ituvākaliṉ ippeyart tāyiṟṟu. yāḻōr - kantaruvar.
kulattālum piṟanalattālum oppumiyuṭiya talivaṉum taliviyum etirppaṭṭa aḷavil uḷḷap puṇarcciyuṟṟup piṉ meyyuṟupuṇarcciyum koḷḷutalāṉ, kantaruvar kaṇṭa tirumaṇa muṟiyākiya itaṉkaṇ koḷvārum koṭuppārum uṭaṉ iruppārum eṉa yārumē illi; iruvarum taṉittut tāmē kūṭum kūṭṭam itu.
kantaruvarkkuk kaṟpiṉṟi amiyavumpeṟum; īṇṭuk kaṟpiṉṟik kaḷavē amiyātu eṉṟaṟkut ‘tuṟiyami’ eṉṟu ‘nalyāḻt tuṇimiyōr iyalpu’ ākiya kāntaruvam vicēṭikkappaṭṭatu.	(to. po. 92 nac.)
yāḻōr maṇaviṉi -
yāḻōr kūṭṭam; kantaruvam. ‘yāḻōr maṇaviṉikku ottaṉaḷ eṉṟē’ 	(maṇimē. 22 : 86)
yāḻōr vēṇṭum puṇarcci -
kantaruvar eṉṟa tēvacātiyic cērnta āṭavarum kaṉṉiya rum koṭuppārum aṭuppārum iṉṟit tāmē poḻi lakattut taṉiyē etirppaṭṭup puṇarvatu. ippuṇarccik kuppiṉ kantaruvariṭam karaṇattoṭu tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷutal nikaḻāmalum pōkalām. āyiṉ, ikkantaruvar vēṇṭum puṇarccipōl talivaṉum taliviyum tāmē koṭuppārum aṭuppārum iṉṟit taṉiyiṭattu etirp paṭṭuk kūṭum iyaṟkip puṇarcci, karaṇamoṭu puṇarat tirumaṇam ceytukoṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttum kaṟpiya lāka mēl toṭarappaṭuvatākaliṉ, iḵtu yāḻōr vēṇ ṭum puṇarcciyi ottatēyaṉṟi atuvē yākātu.	(na. a. 28)
“yāṉaṟiyēṉ nī yuri” eṉṟal -
talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam taliviyit tāṉ maṇattaṟkuk koṭukkavēṇṭum paricapporuḷ paṟṟi viṉāva, avaḷ atu paṟṟit taṉakkut teriyātu eṉavum, avaṉē avaḷ tamari ucāvi atu paṟṟi aṟital vēṇṭum eṉavum kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku valittal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 35)
va
vakutturittal -
talivaṉukkuk kuṟi nērnta tōḻi avaṉiṭamiruntu kiyuṟiyākap peṟṟuvanta taḻiyāṭiyit talivikku aḷikkumuṉ, “ivvāṭiyi nām ēlāviṭil talivaṉ tuyaruṟuvāṉ; ēṟṟāl, nammip piṟar alartūṟṟum vāyppu nikaḻum. namakkup pērutavi ceyta talivaṟkuk  kimmāṟāka ōr utavi nām ceyyāviṭiṉ, ceynnaṉṟi koṉṟavarā vōm” eṉṟu palapaṭiyākat talimakaḷ taḻiyi ēṟpataṟku uriyavaṟṟi nāṭikkūṟutal.
itu ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.			(kōvi. 113)
vaṅkar -
neytal nilamakkaḷ. vaṅkam ōṭṭunar eṉṟa kāraṇappeyar.
vacati -
marutanilattu nakaram. 	(piṅ. 4009, cūṭā. ē - 40)
vañcit turittal -
talivaṉukkuk kuṟi nērnta pāṅki taliviyiṭam avaṉ niliyik kūṟa, avaḷ aṟiyātavaḷip pōlak kuṟippākak kūṟa, tōḻiyum tāṉ veḷippaṭiyākat talivaṉ avaḷoṭu koṇṭuḷḷa toṭarpik kūṟāmal, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam kūṭit tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattukku muyalum avaṉatu niliyiṉi vēṟoruvarmēl vittu maṟittuk kūṟal.
itu ‘kuṟinayappuk kūṟal’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru tuṟi.	(kōvi. 86)
vaṇṭōcci maruṅku aṇital -
‘maruṅku aṇital’ kāṇka.	(kōvi. 45) (i. vi. 495 uri)
vantavaḻi eḷḷal -
taliviyatu kūṟṟu nikaḻum oḻukalāṟukaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ itu oṉṟu.
pāṅkaṉāl talivi āṭiṭam terintu talivaṉ vanta viṭattu aḵtu āyattārkkup pulaṉākuṅkol eṉṉum accattāṉ talivi kūṭṭatti ikaḻntiruttal.			(to. kaḷa. 21 ca. pāla.)
vantuḻi makiḻcci -
ōtal, kāval, tūtu, tuṇi, poruḷ ākiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kāraṇamākap pirintirunta talivaṉ viṉi muṟṟi mīṇṭu vantapōtu taliviyum tōḻiyum makiḻtal.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ aivakip pirivukaḷil nikaḻum nikaḻcci kaḷiṭi oṉṟu.	(na. a. 209)
vantōṉtaṉṉoṭu nontu viṉātal -
kaḷavoḻukkattiṉiṭiyē taṉṉūr ceṉṟu mīṇṭa talivaṉiṭam tōḻi, nīṭṭittamikku varuntik kāraṇam kēṭṭal.
“taliva! niṉ pirivit tāṅkāmal talivi taṉṉuṭal muḻutum veḷuttup pōka niṉṉi niṉittu viḻuntu eḻuntu vimmi melintuḷḷāḷ” (kōvi. 193) eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi taliviyatu tāṅkoṇāp pirivut tuyarit talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘varuttamikuti kūṟal’ eṉṉum.	 (193)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 168)
vayal -
maruta nilam	(tivā. pak. 108)
varavu uṇarnturittal -
oruvaḻit taṇanta talivaṉ vantu ciṟippuṟattu niṟka, avaṉ varavu aṟinta tōḻi matiyattiṭam, “cantiraṉē! eṉ vaḷikaḷ kaḻalukiṉṟaṉa; neñcam nekiḻnturukukiṉṟatu; kaṇkaḷ tuyiliṉṟik kaluḻkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi nāṉ colla vēṇṭā. nīyē talivaṉiṭam colluka” eṉṟu kūṟuvatu.
iḵtu ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum tokutikaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 192)
varavu etirntiruttal -
peruntiṇit talivi taṉ maṉiyakattut talivaṉi etirpārttiruttal.
“neñcē! pūkkaḷ parappiya paḷḷiyil amarntu, pūvum cāntum aṇintu, nam talivaṉ varukiyi etirpārttu āvaluṭaṉ kāttiruppāy!” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu peruntiṇiyiṉ oru pakutiyāṉa peṇpāṟkūṟṟut tuṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(pu. ve. mā. 16 - 4)
varavu tāḻntu iraṅkal -
talivaṉ, kaṟpukkālattil oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattu mīṇṭuvaruvatākak kūṟip poruḷtēṭal karutip pirintu ceṉṟavaṉ, akkālattu mīḷāta niliyil, talivi avaṉ varuki nikaḻāmi kuṟittu varuntik kūṟal.
“aṉṉam taṉ peṭiyik kuḷattiṉ tāmarippūvil taṅkavittu virivil nīriliruntu iriyik koṇṭu vantu, acciṟupirivum tāṅkāta peṭikku aruttip piṉ ataṉi viṭṭu nīṅkātu eppōtum taṅkiyiruppatip pārkkumpōtu maṉitappiṟavi yeṭutta eṉṉiviṭa aṉṉappēṭu mikunta tavam ceytuḷḷatu eṉpatu uṟuti” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(mā. a. pāṭal. 550)
varavu vilakkal -
kaḷavukkālattil iravukkuṟiyil tōḻi, talivaṉ viṭuppa mīṇṭu vanta taliviyip paḷḷiyiṭattuc cērttic ceṉṟu, “taliva! ittalivi kāraṇamāka ikkoṭiya kalmikka vaḻiyil iṉi vārātē” eṉṟu virivil avaḷi varintu koḷḷumāṟu kuṟippiṭṭu iravukkuṟikku varutalit tavirttatu.
iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
	(kōvi. 168)
varukuvar mīṇṭu eṉap pāṅki valittal -
varitaṟkup poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ virivil mīḷvāṉ eṉṟu tōḻi talivikku vaṟpuṟutti yuṇarttal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘varuttam kaṇṭurittal’ eṉṉum (274).
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 170)
varuṅkāl kalakkam -
‘varuvaḻik kalaṅkal’ atu kāṇka.	(cāmi. 123)
varuṇaṉ -
nīrukkuriya teyvam; neytal nilatteyvam.
kaṭal cārnta nilamākiya neytalnilattil vāḻum nuḷi yarkaḷ taṅkaṭku valiyāṟ piṭikkum mīṉvaḷam kuṟintāl, tam nilattu, makaḷiri uṟaviṉaruṭaṉ aḻittuk kūṭṭic cuṟāmīṉ kompiṉi naṭṭuvittu varuṇaṉām tam nilatteyvam kuṟittu vaḻipāṭukaḷ nikaḻttutaliṉ, avarka ḷukku atteyvattiṉatu aruḷ veḷippaṭum.
varuṇaṉ alikaḷ mōtik kaṭaṟkari karintu pōkāta vāṟu kākkum.	(to. po. 5 nac.)
varuṇaṉ perumaṇaṟpakutikkut teyvamātal -
‘varuṇaṉ’ kāṇka.
varuttam kaṇṭurittal -
talivaṉuṭiya variporuṭpirivu kuṟittut talivi neñcoṭu varuntuvatu kaṇṭa tōḻi, “talivaṉ ceṉ ṟuḷḷa kāṭṭil vēṭarkaḷ tattam maṉiviyartam kaṇkaḷip pōṉṟa kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu māṉkaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. avvāṟu maṉiviyariṭam viruppamikka avarkaḷ vāḻum vaḻiyē ceṉṟa talivaṉ virivil paricapporuḷoṭu mīḷvāṉ. ātaliṉ nī kavalippaṭātē” eṉṟu varuttam tīra vurittal.
itaṉi ‘varukuvar mīṇṭeṉap pāṅki valittal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.	(na. a. 170)
itu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 274)
varuttam kūṟi varivu kaṭātal -
kaḷavukkālattil pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉiṭam uṇmi yurittu varivukaṭāya tōḻi, “iṉṉum nīr taṉṉi maṇantukoḷḷa villiyē eṉṟu talivi varuntukiṟāḷ. varivu eṉpati niṉittu nīr varuntukiṉṟīr. ivvāṟu num iruvaruḷḷamum māṟupaṭa nikaḻtaliṉ, iruvarkku miṭiyē yāṉ varuntukiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟu talivaṉukkut taliviyiṉ varuttattiyum taṉ varuttattiyum kūṟi varivu kaṭāvutal.
itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.			(kōvi. 131)
varuttam taṇittal -
kaṟpukkālattil talivaṉ paki taṇi viṉikkup pirintā ṉāka, avaṉatu pirivu kēṭṭu uḷḷuṭintu taṉimiyuṟṟu varuntiya taliviyi nōkkit tōḻi, “niṉṉi viṭṭu avar piriyār. nī neruppiṉiyuṟṟa veṇṇey pōlavum, nīriṉiyuṟṟa uppup pōlavum ivvāṟu urukit taṉimi yuṟṟu varuntātoḻi” eṉa avaḷatu varuttatti taṇittatu.
itu ‘paki taṇiviṉip pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 315)
varutta mikuti kūṟal -
talivaṉ oruvaḻit taṇantu vantu ciṟippuṟattu niṟkat talivi, aṉṉam matiyam ivaṟṟoṭu talivaṉpirivu paṟṟikkūṟi varuntikkoṇṭirunta niliyil, tōḻi avaṉi aṇukit talivi paṭum piritaltuyaram taṉatu collaḷaviṉik kaṭantatu eṉṟu kūṟi, avaṉ avaḷi virivil varitalvēṇṭum eṉpatu paṭat taliviyiṉ varutta mikutiyi avaṉukkuk kūṟutal.
itaṉi ‘vantōṉ taṉṉoṭu nontu viṉātal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.	(na. a. 168)
itu ‘oruvaḻittaṇattal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatākiya kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 193)
varutta mikuti kūṟi varivu kaṭātal -
talivi alarālum kāval mikutiyālum talivaṉik kuṟiyiṭattu etirppaṭa iyalātavaḷāki aḻutu varunti yirukkum niliyit talivaṉukku eṭuttuk kūṟi, avaṉ avaḷi virivil varintukoṇṭu illaṟam naṭattu māṟu tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutal.
itaṉi ‘āṟṟāttaṉmi āṟṟak kūṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa; 			(na. a. 166)
